server/api.test.ts
import test from "node:test";
import type { TestContext } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { handleApi } from "./api.ts";
import { TestDatabase } from "./test-db.ts";
import type {
  DeviceInput,
  Device,
  Team,
  Session,
  InvitationCreated,
} from "./types.ts";

const origin = "http://localhost:4173";
const identity = (id: string) => ({
  id,
  email: `${id}@example.test`,
  name: `Pessoa ${id}`,
});
class Client {
  constructor(
    readonly db: TestDatabase,
    readonly id: string | null = null,
  ) {}
  async request(
    path: string,
    method = "GET",
    body?: unknown,
    headers: Record<string, string> = {},
  ) {
    const user = this.id ? identity(this.id) : null;
    return handleApi(
      new Request(`${origin}/api/v1${path}`, {
        method,
        headers: {
          ...(user
            ? {
                "oai-authenticated-user-id": user.id,
                "oai-authenticated-user-email": user.email,
                "oai-authenticated-user-full-name": encodeURIComponent(
                  user.name,
                ),
                "oai-authenticated-user-full-name-encoding":
                  "percent-encoded-utf-8",
              }
            : {}),
          ...(body === undefined
            ? {}
            : { "content-type": "application/json", origin }),
          ...headers,
        },
        ...(body === undefined ? {} : { body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
      }),
      { DB: this.db },
    );
  }
  async data<T>(path: string, method = "GET", body?: unknown): Promise<T> {
    const response = await this.request(path, method, body);
    const result = await response.json();
    assert.ok(
      response.ok,
      `${method} ${path}: ${response.status} ${JSON.stringify(result)}`,
    );
    return result as T;
  }
  async bootstrap(name = "Empresa de teste") {
    await this.data("/workspace", "POST", { name });
    return this.data<Session>("/session");
  }
  async team(name = "Equipe de teste") {
    const result = await this.data<{ team: Team }>("/teams", "POST", {
      name,
      location: "Unidade de teste",
      color: "#22d3ee",
    });
    return result.team;
  }
  async device(extra: Partial<DeviceInput> = {}) {
    const result = await this.data<{ device: Device }>("/devices", "POST", {
      name: "Rádio de teste",
      serial: crypto.randomUUID(),
      model: "WALK TALK",
      status: "available",
      teamId: null,
      assignedTo: null,
      notes: "",
      ...extra,
    });
    return result.device;
  }
  async invite(
    role: "manager" | "collaborator",
    userId?: string,
    teamId: string | null = null,
  ) {
    return this.data<InvitationCreated>("/invitations", "POST", {
      role,
      email: userId ? identity(userId).email : null,
      teamId,
    });
  }
}

function fixture(t: TestContext) {
  const db = new TestDatabase();
  t.after(() => db.close());
  return db;
}

async function denied(response: Response, statuses: number[]) {
  assert.ok(
    statuses.includes(response.status),
    `Expected ${statuses.join("/")}, received ${response.status}: ${await response.clone().text()}`,
  );
  const body = (await response.json()) as {
    error?: { code?: string; message?: string };
  };
  assert.equal(typeof body.error?.code, "string");
  assert.equal(typeof body.error?.message, "string");
  assert.equal(
    response.headers.get("cache-control")?.includes("no-store"),
    true,
  );
}

test("identity is required and missing identity never bootstraps a workspace", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t);
  const anonymous = new Client(db);
  await denied(await anonymous.request("/session"), [401]);
  await denied(
    await anonymous.request("/workspace", "POST", { name: "Não autorizado" }),
    [401],
  );
  const response = await new Client(db, "owner-a").request("/session");
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  const session = (await response.json()) as Session;
  assert.equal(session.member, null);
  assert.equal(session.workspace, null);
  assert.equal(session.user.name, "Pessoa owner-a");
  assert.match(response.headers.get("cache-control") ?? "", /no-store/);
});

test("workspace bootstrap is atomic, unique per account and isolated between tenants", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    a = new Client(db, "owner-a"),
    b = new Client(db, "owner-b");
  const sa = await a.bootstrap("Empresa A"),
    sb = await b.bootstrap("Empresa B");
  assert.notEqual(sa.workspace?.id, sb.workspace?.id);
  assert.equal(sa.member?.role, "admin");
  assert.equal(sa.workspace?.isDemo, false);
  await denied(
    await a.request("/workspace", "POST", { name: "Empresa duplicada" }),
    [409],
  );
  const device = await a.device();
  const bDevices = await b.data<{ devices: Device[] }>("/devices");
  assert.deepEqual(bDevices.devices, []);
  await denied(
    await b.request(`/devices/${device.id}`, "PATCH", { name: "Sequestro" }),
    [403, 404],
  );
  assert.equal(
    (await a.data<{ devices: Device[] }>("/devices")).devices[0].name,
    "Rádio de teste",
  );
  assert.equal(
    db.sqlite.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS total FROM workspaces").get()?.total,
    2,
  );
});

test("collaborator permissions are enforced server-side and do not trust role fields", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    admin = new Client(db, "admin"),
    user = new Client(db, "colleague");
  await admin.bootstrap();
  const invite = await admin.invite("collaborator", "colleague");
  await user.data("/invitations/accept", "POST", { token: invite.token });
  assert.equal(
    (await user.data<Session>("/session")).member?.role,
    "collaborator",
  );
  for (const [path, method, body] of [
    [
      "/devices",
      "POST",
      {
        name: "Invasão",
        serial: "42",
        model: "Rádio",
        status: "available",
        role: "admin",
      },
    ],
    [
      "/teams",
      "POST",
      { name: "Não permitido", location: "X", color: "#22d3ee" },
    ],
    ["/invitations", "POST", { role: "manager" }],
    ["/members/admin", "PATCH", { role: "collaborator" }],
    ["/export/devices", "GET", undefined],
  ] as const)
    await denied(await user.request(path, method, body), [403]);
  const device = await admin.device();
  await denied(
    await user.request(`/devices/${device.id}/check`, "POST", {}),
    [403, 404],
  );
  const own = await admin.device({ assignedTo: "colleague" });
  const check = await user.request(`/devices/${own.id}/check`, "POST", {});
  assert.equal(check.ok, true, await check.clone().text());
});

test("cross-tenant team and assignee references cannot be written", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    a = new Client(db, "admin-a"),
    b = new Client(db, "admin-b");
  await a.bootstrap();
  await b.bootstrap();
  const otherTeam = await b.team();
  const base = {
    name: "Rádio",
    serial: "one",
    model: "WALK TALK",
    status: "available",
    teamId: null,
    assignedTo: null,
    notes: "",
  };
  await denied(
    await a.request("/devices", "POST", { ...base, teamId: otherTeam.id }),
    [400, 403, 404],
  );
  await denied(
    await a.request("/devices", "POST", { ...base, assignedTo: "admin-b" }),
    [400, 403, 404],
  );
  await denied(
    await a.request("/invitations", "POST", {
      role: "manager",
      teamId: otherTeam.id,
    }),
    [400, 403, 404],
  );
  await denied(
    await a.request("/messages", "POST", {
      body: "Canal incorreto",
      teamId: otherTeam.id,
    }),
    [400, 403, 404],
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    (await a.data<{ devices: Device[] }>("/devices")).devices,
    [],
  );
});

test("invitations are email-bound, one-time, revocable and never expose stored hashes", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    admin = new Client(db, "admin"),
    right = new Client(db, "right"),
    wrong = new Client(db, "wrong");
  await admin.bootstrap();
  const created = await admin.invite("manager", "right");
  assert.match(created.token, /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{32,}$/);
  assert.ok(new Date(created.expiresAt).getTime() > Date.now());
  await denied(
    await wrong.request("/invitations/accept", "POST", {
      token: created.token,
    }),
    [403],
  );
  await right.data("/invitations/accept", "POST", { token: created.token });
  await denied(
    await right.request("/invitations/accept", "POST", {
      token: created.token,
    }),
    [409],
  );
  await denied(
    await wrong.request("/invitations/accept", "POST", {
      token: created.token,
    }),
    [409],
  );
  const listed = await admin.request("/invitations");
  const text = await listed.text();
  assert.equal(text.includes(created.token), false);
  assert.equal(/tokenHash|token_hash/i.test(text), false);
  const stored = db.sqlite
    .prepare("SELECT token_hash FROM invitations WHERE id = ?")
    .get(created.invitation.id);
  assert.notEqual(stored?.token_hash, created.token);
  assert.equal(typeof stored?.token_hash, "string");
  const revoked = await admin.invite("collaborator");
  assert.equal(
    (await admin.request(`/invitations/${revoked.invitation.id}`, "DELETE")).ok,
    true,
  );
  await denied(
    await wrong.request("/invitations/accept", "POST", {
      token: revoked.token,
    }),
    [409, 410],
  );
});

test("request boundary rejects cross-origin writes, malformed JSON and excessive bodies", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    admin = new Client(db, "admin");
  await admin.bootstrap();
  await denied(
    await admin.request(
      "/teams",
      "POST",
      { name: "CSRF" },
      { origin: "https://attacker.example" },
    ),
    [403],
  );
  await denied(
    await admin.request(
      "/teams",
      "POST",
      { name: "CSRF" },
      { "content-type": "text/plain" },
    ),
    [400, 415],
  );
  const headers = {
    "oai-authenticated-user-id": "admin",
    "oai-authenticated-user-email": "admin@example.test",
    "content-type": "application/json",
    origin,
  };
  const malformed = await handleApi(
    new Request(`${origin}/api/v1/teams`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers,
      body: "{bad json",
    }),
    { DB: db },
  );
  await denied(malformed, [400]);
  const oversized = await handleApi(
    new Request(`${origin}/api/v1/messages`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers,
      body: JSON.stringify({ body: "x".repeat(150_000) }),
    }),
    { DB: db },
  );
  await denied(oversized, [400, 413]);
  await denied(
    await admin.request("/messages", "POST", {
      body: "x".repeat(2001),
      teamId: null,
    }),
    [400],
  );
});

test("team messages stay private to their intended membership and are plain text", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    admin = new Client(db, "admin"),
    first = new Client(db, "first"),
    second = new Client(db, "second");
  await admin.bootstrap();
  const teamA = await admin.team("Equipe A"),
    teamB = await admin.team("Equipe B");
  const ia = await admin.invite("collaborator", "first", teamA.id),
    ib = await admin.invite("collaborator", "second", teamB.id);
  await first.data("/invitations/accept", "POST", { token: ia.token });
  await second.data("/invitations/accept", "POST", { token: ib.token });
  await first.data("/messages", "POST", {
    body: "<script>alert('texto')</script>",
    teamId: teamA.id,
  });
  await denied(
    await second.request(`/messages?teamId=${teamA.id}`),
    [403, 404],
  );
  await denied(
    await second.request("/messages", "POST", {
      body: "Invasão",
      teamId: teamA.id,
    }),
    [403, 404],
  );
  const messages = await first.data<{ messages: { body: string }[] }>(
    `/messages?teamId=${teamA.id}`,
  );
  assert.equal(messages.messages[0].body, "<script>alert('texto')</script>");
  assert.deepEqual(
    (await first.data<{ messages: unknown[] }>("/messages")).messages,
    [],
  );
});

test("device CSV export defuses spreadsheet formulas and archive removes active records", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    admin = new Client(db, "admin");
  await admin.bootstrap();
  const device = await admin.device({
    name: '=HYPERLINK("https://example.test")',
    serial: "+SUM(1,2)",
    notes: "@dangerous",
  });
  const response = await admin.request("/export/devices");
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  assert.match(response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "", /text\/csv/);
  const csv = await response.text();
  assert.match(csv, /'=HYPERLINK/);
  assert.match(csv, /'\+SUM/);
  await admin.data(`/devices/${device.id}`, "DELETE");
  assert.deepEqual(
    (await admin.data<{ devices: Device[] }>("/devices")).devices,
    [],
  );
  assert.equal(
    db.sqlite
      .prepare("SELECT archived_at FROM devices WHERE id = ?")
      .get(device.id)?.archived_at !== null,
    true,
  );
});

test("manager can operate inventory but cannot administer access or change the owner", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    admin = new Client(db, "admin"),
    manager = new Client(db, "manager");
  await admin.bootstrap();
  const invite = await admin.invite("manager", "manager");
  await manager.data("/invitations/accept", "POST", { token: invite.token });
  const team = await manager.team("Operações");
  const device = await manager.device({ teamId: team.id });
  await manager.data(`/devices/${device.id}`, "PATCH", {
    status: "maintenance",
  });
  assert.equal((await manager.request("/export/devices")).status, 200);
  await denied(await manager.request("/invitations"), [403]);
  await denied(
    await manager.request("/members/admin", "PATCH", { role: "collaborator" }),
    [403],
  );
  await denied(
    await admin.request("/members/admin", "PATCH", { role: "manager" }),
    [403],
  );
  await denied(
    await admin.request("/invitations", "POST", { role: "admin" }),
    [400],
  );
});

test("expired and concurrent invitations do not create extra memberships", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    admin = new Client(db, "admin"),
    one = new Client(db, "one"),
    two = new Client(db, "two");
  await admin.bootstrap();
  const expired = await admin.invite("collaborator");
  db.sqlite
    .prepare("UPDATE invitations SET expires_at=? WHERE id=?")
    .run("2000-01-01T00:00:00.000Z", expired.invitation.id);
  await denied(
    await one.request("/invitations/accept", "POST", { token: expired.token }),
    [409],
  );
  assert.equal((await one.data<Session>("/session")).member, null);
  const shared = await admin.invite("collaborator");
  const results = await Promise.all([
    one.request("/invitations/accept", "POST", { token: shared.token }),
    two.request("/invitations/accept", "POST", { token: shared.token }),
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(results.map((r) => r.status).sort(), [201, 409]);
  assert.equal(
    db.sqlite
      .prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM members WHERE invitation_id=?")
      .get(shared.invitation.id)?.total,
    1,
  );
  assert.equal(
    db.sqlite
      .prepare(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM activity WHERE type='member_joined'",
      )
      .get()?.total,
    1,
  );
});

test("overview and activity obey team scope and membership updates clear inconsistent assignments", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    admin = new Client(db, "admin"),
    colleague = new Client(db, "colleague"),
    outsider = new Client(db, "other-company");
  const owner = await admin.bootstrap();
  await outsider.bootstrap();
  const ownTeam = await admin.team("Visível"),
    hiddenTeam = await admin.team("Privada");
  const invitation = await admin.invite(
    "collaborator",
    "colleague",
    ownTeam.id,
  );
  await colleague.data("/invitations/accept", "POST", {
    token: invitation.token,
  });
  const assigned = await admin.device({
    teamId: ownTeam.id,
    assignedTo: "colleague",
  });
  await admin.device({ teamId: hiddenTeam.id, name: "Aparelho confidencial" });
  await admin.data("/messages", "POST", {
    teamId: hiddenTeam.id,
    body: "Mensagem restrita",
  });
  await admin.data("/messages", "POST", {
    teamId: null,
    body: "Mensagem geral",
  });
  const response = await colleague.request("/overview");
  const text = await response.text();
  const overview = JSON.parse(text);
  assert.equal(overview.devices.total, 1);
  assert.equal(overview.teams, 1);
  assert.equal(overview.members, 1);
  assert.equal(overview.messages, 1);
  assert.equal(text.includes("Aparelho confidencial"), false);
  assert.equal(text.includes("Mensagem restrita"), false);
  await denied(
    await outsider.request("/members/colleague", "PATCH", { teamId: null }),
    [404],
  );
  await admin.data("/members/colleague", "PATCH", { teamId: hiddenTeam.id });
  const updated = db.sqlite
    .prepare("SELECT assigned_to FROM devices WHERE id=? AND workspace_id=?")
    .get(assigned.id, owner.workspace!.id);
  assert.equal(updated?.assigned_to, null);
});

test("large inventory metrics and CSV are not truncated by the list page limit", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    admin = new Client(db, "admin");
  const session = await admin.bootstrap();
  const insert = db.sqlite.prepare(
    "INSERT INTO devices(id,workspace_id,name,serial,model,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,'available',?,?)",
  );
  const timestamp = "2025-01-01T00:00:00.000Z";
  for (let i = 0; i < 1005; i++)
    insert.run(
      `device-${String(i).padStart(5, "0")}`,
      session.workspace!.id,
      `Aparelho ${i}`,
      `SERIAL-${i}`,
      "WALK TALK",
      timestamp,
      timestamp,
    );
  const overview = await admin.data<{
    devices: { total: number; available: number };
  }>("/overview");
  assert.equal(overview.devices.total, 1005);
  assert.equal(overview.devices.available, 1005);
  const csv = await (await admin.request("/export/devices")).text();
  assert.equal(csv.split("\r\n").length, 1006);
  assert.match(csv, /SERIAL-1004/);
});

test("a collaborator cannot complete a check after the device was concurrently reassigned", async (t) => {
  const db = fixture(t),
    admin = new Client(db, "admin"),
    user = new Client(db, "colleague");
  await admin.bootstrap();
  const invite = await admin.invite("collaborator", "colleague");
  await user.data("/invitations/accept", "POST", { token: invite.token });
  const device = await admin.device({ assignedTo: "colleague" });
  const batch = db.batch.bind(db);
  let reassign = true;
  db.batch = async (statements) => {
    if (reassign) {
      reassign = false;
      db.sqlite
        .prepare("UPDATE devices SET assigned_to=NULL WHERE id=?")
        .run(device.id);
    }
    return batch(statements);
  };
  await denied(
    await user.request(`/devices/${device.id}/check`, "POST", {}),
    [409],
  );
  assert.equal(
    db.sqlite
      .prepare("SELECT last_check_at FROM devices WHERE id=?")
      .get(device.id)?.last_check_at,
    null,
  );
  assert.equal(
    db.sqlite
      .prepare(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM activity WHERE type='device_checked'",
      )
      .get()?.total,
    0,
  );
});
server/api.ts
import type { ApiEnvironment, D1PreparedStatement } from "./d1.ts";
import type {
  User,
  Member,
  Workspace,
  Device,
  Team,
  Invitation,
  Message,
  Activity,
  DeviceStatus,
  Role,
} from "./types.ts";

class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  code: string;
  constructor(status: number, code: string, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
  }
}
type RecordBody = Record<string, unknown>;
type Membership = Member & { workspaceId: string };
type Context = {
  env: ApiEnvironment;
  user: User;
  member: Membership;
  workspace: Workspace;
  now: string;
};
const MAX_BODY = 8192;
const responseHeaders = {
  "Cache-Control": "private, no-store",
  "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
  "Referrer-Policy": "same-origin",
};
const json = (data: unknown, status = 200) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    status,
    headers: {
      ...responseHeaders,
      "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    },
  });
const fail = (status: number, code: string, message: string): never => {
  throw new ApiError(status, code, message);
};
const uuid = () => crypto.randomUUID();
const stringValue = (
  value: unknown,
  label: string,
  max: number,
  required = true,
) => {
  if (value === undefined && !required) return "";
  if (typeof value !== "string")
    return fail(400, "VALIDATION", `${label} deve ser um texto.`);
  const text = value.trim();
  if (
    (required && !text) ||
    text.length > max ||
    /[\u0000-\u0008\u000b\u000c\u000e-\u001f\u007f]/.test(text)
  )
    return fail(
      400,
      "VALIDATION",
      `${label} deve conter ${required ? "de 1 a " : "até "}${max} caracteres válidos.`,
    );
  return text;
};
const optionalId = (value: unknown, label: string): string | null =>
  value === undefined || value === null || value === ""
    ? null
    : stringValue(value, label, 300);
const allow = (body: RecordBody, keys: string[]) => {
  if (Object.keys(body).some((key) => !keys.includes(key)))
    fail(400, "VALIDATION", "O formulário contém um campo não permitido.");
};
const roleValue = (value: unknown): "manager" | "collaborator" => {
  if (value !== "manager" && value !== "collaborator")
    return fail(400, "VALIDATION", "Escolha gestor ou colaborador.");
  return value;
};
const elevated = (c: Context) => {
  if (c.member.role === "collaborator")
    fail(403, "FORBIDDEN", "Esta ação exige um administrador ou gestor.");
};
const admin = (c: Context) => {
  if (c.member.role !== "admin")
    fail(403, "FORBIDDEN", "Esta ação exige um administrador.");
};
function identity(request: Request): User {
  const id = request.headers.get("oai-authenticated-user-id")?.trim();
  const email = request.headers
    .get("oai-authenticated-user-email")
    ?.trim()
    .toLowerCase();
  if (
    !id ||
    !email ||
    id.length > 300 ||
    email.length > 254 ||
    !email.includes("@")
  )
    return fail(401, "UNAUTHENTICATED", "Entre na sua conta para continuar.");
  let name = request.headers.get("oai-authenticated-user-full-name") || "";
  if (
    request.headers.get("oai-authenticated-user-full-name-encoding") ===
    "percent-encoded-utf-8"
  ) {
    try {
      name = decodeURIComponent(name);
    } catch {
      name = "";
    }
  }
  name =
    name
      .replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/g, " ")
      .trim()
      .slice(0, 120) || email.split("@")[0];
  return { id, email, name };
}
async function bodyOf(request: Request): Promise<RecordBody> {
  if (
    !/^application\/json(?:\s*;|$)/i.test(
      request.headers.get("content-type") || "",
    )
  )
    return fail(415, "CONTENT_TYPE", "Use um corpo JSON para esta operação.");
  const declared = Number(request.headers.get("content-length") || 0);
  if (declared > MAX_BODY)
    return fail(
      413,
      "BODY_TOO_LARGE",
      "O formulário excede o tamanho permitido.",
    );
  const reader = request.body?.getReader();
  if (!reader) return fail(400, "VALIDATION", "O corpo JSON é obrigatório.");
  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let size = 0;
  try {
    for (;;) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      size += value.byteLength;
      if (size > MAX_BODY) {
        await reader.cancel();
        return fail(
          413,
          "BODY_TOO_LARGE",
          "O formulário excede o tamanho permitido.",
        );
      }
      chunks.push(value);
    }
  } finally {
    reader.releaseLock();
  }
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(size);
  let offset = 0;
  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    bytes.set(chunk, offset);
    offset += chunk.length;
  }
  let data: unknown;
  try {
    data = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes));
  } catch {
    return fail(
      400,
      "VALIDATION",
      "O corpo da requisição não é um JSON válido.",
    );
  }
  if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || Array.isArray(data))
    return fail(400, "VALIDATION", "Envie um objeto JSON.");
  return data as RecordBody;
}
function csrf(request: Request) {
  if (["GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"].includes(request.method)) return;
  const origin = request.headers.get("origin");
  if (
    (origin && origin !== new URL(request.url).origin) ||
    request.headers.get("sec-fetch-site") === "cross-site"
  )
    fail(403, "ORIGIN", "A origem desta solicitação não foi autorizada.");
}
const memberSql = `SELECT m.user_id AS id,m.workspace_id AS workspaceId,m.email,m.display_name AS name,m.role,m.team_id AS teamId,t.name AS teamName,m.joined_at AS joinedAt FROM members m LEFT JOIN teams t ON t.id=m.team_id AND t.workspace_id=m.workspace_id`;
const deviceSql = `SELECT d.id,d.name,d.serial,d.model,d.status,d.team_id AS teamId,t.name AS teamName,d.assigned_to AS assignedTo,m.display_name AS assigneeName,d.notes,d.last_check_at AS lastCheckAt,d.created_at AS createdAt,d.updated_at AS updatedAt,d.is_example AS isExample FROM devices d LEFT JOIN teams t ON t.id=d.team_id AND t.workspace_id=d.workspace_id LEFT JOIN members m ON m.user_id=d.assigned_to AND m.workspace_id=d.workspace_id`;
const messageSql = `SELECT msg.id,msg.body,msg.team_id AS teamId,msg.author_id AS authorId,m.display_name AS authorName,m.role AS authorRole,msg.created_at AS createdAt FROM messages msg JOIN members m ON m.user_id=msg.author_id AND m.workspace_id=msg.workspace_id`;
const inviteSql = `SELECT i.id,i.role,i.email,i.team_id AS teamId,t.name AS teamName,i.expires_at AS expiresAt,i.created_at AS createdAt,CASE WHEN i.revoked_at IS NOT NULL THEN 'revoked' WHEN i.accepted_at IS NOT NULL THEN 'accepted' WHEN i.expires_at<=? THEN 'expired' ELSE 'pending' END AS status FROM invitations i LEFT JOIN teams t ON t.id=i.team_id AND t.workspace_id=i.workspace_id`;
const publicMember = (m: Membership): Member => ({
  id: m.id,
  email: m.email,
  name: m.name,
  role: m.role,
  teamId: m.teamId,
  teamName: m.teamName,
  joinedAt: m.joinedAt,
});
async function getMembership(env: ApiEnvironment, user: User) {
  return env.DB.prepare(`${memberSql} WHERE m.user_id=?`)
    .bind(user.id)
    .first<Membership>();
}
async function getWorkspace(
  env: ApiEnvironment,
  id: string,
): Promise<Workspace> {
  const row = await env.DB.prepare(
    "SELECT id,name,is_demo AS isDemo FROM workspaces WHERE id=?",
  )
    .bind(id)
    .first<Workspace>();
  if (!row)
    return fail(404, "WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND", "A empresa não foi encontrada.");
  return { ...row, isDemo: Boolean(row.isDemo) };
}
async function context(
  env: ApiEnvironment,
  user: User,
  member: Membership | null,
): Promise<Context> {
  if (!member)
    return fail(
      403,
      "NO_MEMBERSHIP",
      "Crie uma empresa ou aceite um convite para continuar.",
    );
  return {
    env,
    user,
    member,
    workspace: await getWorkspace(env, member.workspaceId),
    now: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
}
function activity(
  c: Context,
  type: string,
  description: string,
  teamId: string | null = null,
): D1PreparedStatement {
  return c.env.DB.prepare(
    "INSERT INTO activity(id,workspace_id,actor_id,team_id,type,description,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
  ).bind(uuid(), c.workspace.id, c.user.id, teamId, type, description, c.now);
}
async function validTeam(c: Context, id: string | null) {
  if (!id) return;
  const row = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    "SELECT id FROM teams WHERE id=? AND workspace_id=?",
  )
    .bind(id, c.workspace.id)
    .first();
  if (!row) fail(403, "INVALID_TEAM", "A equipe não pertence a esta empresa.");
}
async function validAssignee(
  c: Context,
  id: string | null,
  teamId: string | null,
) {
  if (!id) return;
  const row = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    "SELECT team_id AS teamId FROM members WHERE user_id=? AND workspace_id=?",
  )
    .bind(id, c.workspace.id)
    .first<{ teamId: string | null }>();
  if (!row)
    fail(403, "INVALID_ASSIGNEE", "O responsável não pertence a esta empresa.");
  if (teamId && row?.teamId !== teamId)
    fail(
      400,
      "TEAM_MISMATCH",
      "O responsável deve pertencer à equipe selecionada.",
    );
}
function deviceScope(c: Context) {
  return c.member.role === "collaborator"
    ? {
        sql: " AND (d.assigned_to=? OR (d.team_id IS NOT NULL AND d.team_id=?))",
        args: [c.user.id, c.member.teamId],
      }
    : { sql: "", args: [] };
}
async function devices(c: Context) {
  const scope = deviceScope(c);
  const rows = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `${deviceSql} WHERE d.workspace_id=? AND d.archived_at IS NULL${scope.sql} ORDER BY d.created_at DESC,d.id DESC LIMIT 1000`,
  )
    .bind(c.workspace.id, ...scope.args)
    .all<Device>();
  return rows.results.map((row) => ({
    ...row,
    isExample: Boolean(row.isExample),
  }));
}
async function deviceById(c: Context, id: string, scoped = true) {
  const scope = scoped ? deviceScope(c) : { sql: "", args: [] };
  const row = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `${deviceSql} WHERE d.id=? AND d.workspace_id=? AND d.archived_at IS NULL${scope.sql}`,
  )
    .bind(id, c.workspace.id, ...scope.args)
    .first<Device>();
  if (!row) return fail(404, "NOT_FOUND", "Aparelho não encontrado.");
  return { ...row, isExample: Boolean(row.isExample) };
}
async function teams(c: Context) {
  const sql = `SELECT t.id,t.name,t.location,t.color,t.created_at AS createdAt,t.is_example AS isExample,(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM members m WHERE m.team_id=t.id AND m.workspace_id=t.workspace_id) AS members,(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM devices d WHERE d.team_id=t.id AND d.workspace_id=t.workspace_id AND d.archived_at IS NULL) AS devices FROM teams t WHERE t.workspace_id=?${c.member.role === "collaborator" ? " AND t.id=?" : ""} ORDER BY t.name LIMIT 250`;
  const rows = await c.env.DB.prepare(sql)
    .bind(
      c.workspace.id,
      ...(c.member.role === "collaborator" ? [c.member.teamId] : []),
    )
    .all<Team>();
  return rows.results.map((row) => ({
    ...row,
    isExample: Boolean(row.isExample),
  }));
}
async function members(c: Context) {
  const rows = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `${memberSql} WHERE m.workspace_id=?${c.member.role === "collaborator" ? " AND (m.user_id=? OR (m.team_id IS NOT NULL AND m.team_id=?))" : ""} ORDER BY m.display_name LIMIT 500`,
  )
    .bind(
      c.workspace.id,
      ...(c.member.role === "collaborator" ? [c.user.id, c.member.teamId] : []),
    )
    .all<Membership>();
  return rows.results.map(publicMember);
}
async function visibleMessages(
  c: Context,
  teamId?: string | null,
  limit = 100,
) {
  let filter = "",
    args: unknown[] = [];
  if (teamId === undefined) {
    if (c.member.role === "collaborator") {
      filter = " AND (msg.team_id IS NULL OR msg.team_id=?)";
      args = [c.member.teamId];
    }
  } else if (teamId === null) filter = " AND msg.team_id IS NULL";
  else {
    await validTeam(c, teamId);
    if (c.member.role === "collaborator" && c.member.teamId !== teamId)
      fail(403, "CHANNEL_FORBIDDEN", "Este canal é restrito à equipe.");
    filter = " AND msg.team_id=?";
    args = [teamId];
  }
  const rows = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `${messageSql} WHERE msg.workspace_id=?${filter} ORDER BY msg.created_at DESC,msg.id DESC LIMIT ?`,
  )
    .bind(c.workspace.id, ...args, limit)
    .all<Message>();
  return rows.results;
}
async function createWorkspace(
  env: ApiEnvironment,
  user: User,
  existing: Membership | null,
  body: RecordBody,
) {
  if (existing)
    return fail(409, "ALREADY_MEMBER", "Sua conta já pertence a uma empresa.");
  allow(body, ["name", "withExamples"]);
  const name = stringValue(body.name, "Nome da empresa", 100);
  if (body.withExamples !== undefined && typeof body.withExamples !== "boolean")
    fail(
      400,
      "VALIDATION",
      "A opção de exemplos deve ser verdadeira ou falsa.",
    );
  const demo = body.withExamples === true,
    id = uuid(),
    now = new Date().toISOString();
  const teamOne = demo ? uuid() : null,
    teamTwo = demo ? uuid() : null;
  const statements = [
    env.DB.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO workspaces(id,name,is_demo,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?)",
    ).bind(id, name, demo ? 1 : 0, now),
  ];
  if (demo)
    for (const [teamId, teamName, location, color] of [
      [
        teamOne,
        "Operações de campo · exemplo",
        "Base Norte · exemplo",
        "#22d3ee",
      ],
      [teamTwo, "Logística · exemplo", "Base Sul · exemplo", "#ff8a1f"],
    ])
      statements.push(
        env.DB.prepare(
          "INSERT INTO teams(id,workspace_id,name,location,color,is_example,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,1,?)",
        ).bind(teamId, id, teamName, location, color, now),
      );
  statements.push(
    env.DB.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO members(user_id,workspace_id,email,display_name,role,team_id,joined_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
    ).bind(user.id, id, user.email, user.name, "admin", teamOne, now),
  );
  if (demo)
    for (const [index, status, teamId, assignee] of [
      [1, "available", teamOne, null],
      [2, "in_use", teamOne, user.id],
      [3, "maintenance", teamTwo, null],
    ] as const)
      statements.push(
        env.DB.prepare(
          "INSERT INTO devices(id,workspace_id,name,serial,model,status,team_id,assigned_to,notes,created_at,updated_at,is_example) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1)",
        ).bind(
          uuid(),
          id,
          `WALK TALK ${index} · exemplo`,
          `EXEMPLO-00${index}`,
          "Modelo conceitual",
          status,
          teamId,
          assignee,
          "Registro fictício para explorar a plataforma.",
          now,
          now,
        ),
      );
  statements.push(
    env.DB.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO activity(id,workspace_id,actor_id,type,description,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)",
    ).bind(
      uuid(),
      id,
      user.id,
      "workspace_created",
      demo
        ? "Empresa criada com equipes e aparelhos de exemplo."
        : "Empresa criada. Bem-vindo ao WALK TALK.",
      now,
    ),
  );
  await env.DB.batch(statements);
  const member = await getMembership(env, user);
  return json(
    {
      user,
      member: member ? publicMember(member) : null,
      workspace: { id, name, isDemo: demo },
    },
    201,
  );
}
async function overview(c: Context) {
  // Aggregates cover the full authorized dataset, independently of list limits.
  const scope = deviceScope(c);
  const deviceCounts = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) AS total,
      COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN d.status='available' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),0) AS available,
      COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN d.status='in_use' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),0) AS inUse,
      COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN d.status='maintenance' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),0) AS maintenance,
      COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN substr(d.last_check_at,1,10)=? THEN 1 ELSE 0 END),0) AS checkedToday
     FROM devices d WHERE d.workspace_id=? AND d.archived_at IS NULL${scope.sql}`,
  )
    .bind(c.now.slice(0, 10), c.workspace.id, ...scope.args)
    .first<{
      total: number;
      available: number;
      inUse: number;
      maintenance: number;
      checkedToday: number;
    }>();
  const teamCount = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM teams t WHERE t.workspace_id=?${c.member.role === "collaborator" ? " AND t.id=?" : ""}`,
  )
    .bind(
      c.workspace.id,
      ...(c.member.role === "collaborator" ? [c.member.teamId] : []),
    )
    .first<{ total: number }>();
  const memberCount = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM members m WHERE m.workspace_id=?${c.member.role === "collaborator" ? " AND (m.user_id=? OR (m.team_id IS NOT NULL AND m.team_id=?))" : ""}`,
  )
    .bind(
      c.workspace.id,
      ...(c.member.role === "collaborator" ? [c.user.id, c.member.teamId] : []),
    )
    .first<{ total: number }>();
  const filter =
    c.member.role === "collaborator"
      ? " AND (a.actor_id=? OR (a.team_id IS NOT NULL AND a.team_id=?))"
      : "";
  const activityRows = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT a.id,a.type,a.description,m.display_name AS actorName,a.created_at AS createdAt FROM activity a JOIN members m ON m.user_id=a.actor_id AND m.workspace_id=a.workspace_id WHERE a.workspace_id=?${filter} ORDER BY a.created_at DESC,a.id DESC LIMIT 12`,
  )
    .bind(
      c.workspace.id,
      ...(c.member.role === "collaborator" ? [c.user.id, c.member.teamId] : []),
    )
    .all<Activity>();
  const count = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM messages WHERE workspace_id=?${c.member.role === "collaborator" ? " AND (team_id IS NULL OR team_id=?)" : ""}`,
  )
    .bind(
      c.workspace.id,
      ...(c.member.role === "collaborator" ? [c.member.teamId] : []),
    )
    .first<{ total: number }>();
  return json({
    workspace: c.workspace,
    devices: deviceCounts ?? {
      total: 0,
      available: 0,
      inUse: 0,
      maintenance: 0,
      checkedToday: 0,
    },
    teams: teamCount?.total ?? 0,
    members: memberCount?.total ?? 0,
    messages: count?.total ?? 0,
    activity: activityRows.results,
    recentMessages: await visibleMessages(c, undefined, 5),
  });
}
async function createTeam(c: Context, body: RecordBody) {
  elevated(c);
  allow(body, ["name", "location", "color"]);
  const name = stringValue(body.name, "Nome da equipe", 80),
    location = stringValue(body.location, "Localização", 120, false),
    color =
      body.color === undefined ? "#22d3ee" : stringValue(body.color, "Cor", 7);
  if (!/^#[\da-f]{6}$/i.test(color))
    fail(400, "VALIDATION", "Use uma cor hexadecimal válida.");
  const id = uuid();
  await c.env.DB.batch([
    c.env.DB.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO teams(id,workspace_id,name,location,color,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)",
    ).bind(id, c.workspace.id, name, location, color, c.now),
    activity(c, "team_created", `Equipe ${name} criada.`, id),
  ]);
  return json({ team: (await teams(c)).find((t) => t.id === id) }, 201);
}
async function saveDevice(c: Context, body: RecordBody, id?: string) {
  elevated(c);
  allow(body, [
    "name",
    "serial",
    "model",
    "status",
    "teamId",
    "assignedTo",
    "notes",
  ]);
  if (id && Object.keys(body).length === 0)
    fail(400, "VALIDATION", "Informe ao menos um campo para alterar.");
  const current = id ? await deviceById(c, id, false) : null;
  const input: RecordBody = {
    ...(current ?? {
      status: "available",
      teamId: null,
      assignedTo: null,
      notes: "",
    }),
    ...body,
  };
  const name = stringValue(input.name, "Nome do aparelho", 80),
    serial = stringValue(input.serial, "Número de série", 64),
    model = stringValue(input.model, "Modelo", 80),
    notes = stringValue(input.notes, "Observações", 500, false);
  if (!["available", "in_use", "maintenance"].includes(input.status as string))
    fail(400, "VALIDATION", "Escolha uma situação válida para o aparelho.");
  const status = input.status as DeviceStatus,
    teamId = optionalId(input.teamId, "Equipe"),
    assignedTo = optionalId(input.assignedTo, "Responsável");
  await validTeam(c, teamId);
  await validAssignee(c, assignedTo, teamId);
  const deviceId = id ?? uuid();
  const statement = id
    ? c.env.DB.prepare(
        "UPDATE devices SET name=?,serial=?,model=?,status=?,team_id=?,assigned_to=?,notes=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=? AND workspace_id=? AND archived_at IS NULL",
      ).bind(
        name,
        serial,
        model,
        status,
        teamId,
        assignedTo,
        notes,
        c.now,
        deviceId,
        c.workspace.id,
      )
    : c.env.DB.prepare(
        "INSERT INTO devices(id,workspace_id,name,serial,model,status,team_id,assigned_to,notes,created_at,updated_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
      ).bind(
        deviceId,
        c.workspace.id,
        name,
        serial,
        model,
        status,
        teamId,
        assignedTo,
        notes,
        c.now,
        c.now,
      );
  await c.env.DB.batch([
    statement,
    activity(
      c,
      id ? "device_updated" : "device_created",
      `Aparelho ${name} ${id ? "atualizado" : "cadastrado"}.`,
      teamId,
    ),
  ]);
  return json({ device: await deviceById(c, deviceId, false) }, id ? 200 : 201);
}
async function archiveDevice(c: Context, id: string) {
  elevated(c);
  const device = await deviceById(c, id, false);
  await c.env.DB.batch([
    c.env.DB.prepare(
      "UPDATE devices SET archived_at=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=? AND workspace_id=? AND archived_at IS NULL",
    ).bind(c.now, c.now, id, c.workspace.id),
    activity(
      c,
      "device_archived",
      `Aparelho ${device.name} arquivado.`,
      device.teamId,
    ),
  ]);
  return json({ ok: true });
}
async function checkDevice(c: Context, id: string) {
  const device = await deviceById(c, id);
  if (c.member.role === "collaborator" && device.assignedTo !== c.user.id)
    fail(
      403,
      "FORBIDDEN",
      "Você só pode registrar a conferência de um aparelho atribuído a você.",
    );
  const results = await c.env.DB.batch([
    c.env.DB.prepare(
      "UPDATE devices SET last_check_at=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=? AND workspace_id=? AND archived_at IS NULL AND (?<>'collaborator' OR assigned_to=?)",
    ).bind(c.now, c.now, id, c.workspace.id, c.member.role, c.user.id),
    c.env.DB.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO activity(id,workspace_id,actor_id,team_id,type,description,created_at) SELECT ?,workspace_id,?,team_id,'device_checked',?,? FROM devices WHERE id=? AND workspace_id=? AND archived_at IS NULL AND last_check_at=? AND (?<>'collaborator' OR assigned_to=?)",
    ).bind(
      uuid(),
      c.user.id,
      `Conferência manual registrada: ${device.name}.`,
      c.now,
      id,
      c.workspace.id,
      c.now,
      c.member.role,
      c.user.id,
    ),
  ]);
  if (results[0].meta.changes !== 1)
    fail(
      409,
      "DEVICE_CHANGED",
      "O aparelho foi alterado por outra pessoa. Atualize a lista antes de continuar.",
    );
  return json({ device: await deviceById(c, id) });
}
async function updateMember(c: Context, id: string, body: RecordBody) {
  admin(c);
  allow(body, ["role", "teamId"]);
  if (Object.keys(body).length === 0)
    fail(400, "VALIDATION", "Informe ao menos um campo para alterar.");
  const target = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `${memberSql} WHERE m.user_id=? AND m.workspace_id=?`,
  )
    .bind(id, c.workspace.id)
    .first<Membership>();
  if (!target) fail(404, "NOT_FOUND", "Pessoa não encontrada.");
  if (id === c.user.id || target?.role === "admin")
    fail(
      403,
      "ADMIN_PROTECTED",
      "A conta administradora não pode ser alterada por esta operação.",
    );
  const role = body.role === undefined ? target!.role : roleValue(body.role),
    teamId =
      body.teamId === undefined
        ? target!.teamId
        : optionalId(body.teamId, "Equipe");
  await validTeam(c, teamId);
  // Clear assignments that would become inconsistent with the new team.
  await c.env.DB.batch([
    c.env.DB.prepare(
      "UPDATE members SET role=?,team_id=? WHERE user_id=? AND workspace_id=? AND role<>'admin'",
    ).bind(role, teamId, id, c.workspace.id),
    c.env.DB.prepare(
      "UPDATE devices SET assigned_to=NULL,updated_at=? WHERE workspace_id=? AND assigned_to=? AND team_id IS NOT NULL AND (team_id<>? OR ? IS NULL)",
    ).bind(c.now, c.workspace.id, id, teamId, teamId),
    activity(
      c,
      "member_updated",
      `Acesso de ${target!.name} atualizado.`,
      teamId,
    ),
  ]);
  const updated = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `${memberSql} WHERE m.user_id=? AND m.workspace_id=?`,
  )
    .bind(id, c.workspace.id)
    .first<Membership>();
  return json({ member: publicMember(updated!) });
}
async function createMessage(c: Context, body: RecordBody) {
  allow(body, ["body", "teamId"]);
  const text = stringValue(body.body, "Mensagem", 2000),
    teamId = optionalId(body.teamId, "Canal");
  await validTeam(c, teamId);
  if (teamId && c.member.role === "collaborator" && teamId !== c.member.teamId)
    fail(403, "CHANNEL_FORBIDDEN", "Este canal é restrito à equipe.");
  const id = uuid();
  await c.env.DB.batch([
    c.env.DB.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO messages(id,workspace_id,team_id,author_id,body,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)",
    ).bind(id, c.workspace.id, teamId, c.user.id, text, c.now),
    activity(
      c,
      "message_posted",
      teamId
        ? "Uma mensagem foi publicada no canal da equipe."
        : "Uma mensagem foi publicada no canal geral.",
      teamId,
    ),
  ]);
  const message = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `${messageSql} WHERE msg.id=? AND msg.workspace_id=?`,
  )
    .bind(id, c.workspace.id)
    .first<Message>();
  return json({ message }, 201);
}
async function hashToken(token: string) {
  const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest(
    "SHA-256",
    new TextEncoder().encode(token),
  );
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest), (b) =>
    b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"),
  ).join("");
}
async function invitations(c: Context) {
  admin(c);
  const rows = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `${inviteSql} WHERE i.workspace_id=? ORDER BY i.created_at DESC LIMIT 250`,
  )
    .bind(c.now, c.workspace.id)
    .all<Invitation>();
  return rows.results;
}
async function createInvitation(c: Context, body: RecordBody, origin: string) {
  admin(c);
  allow(body, ["role", "email", "teamId"]);
  const role = roleValue(body.role),
    teamId = optionalId(body.teamId, "Equipe");
  await validTeam(c, teamId);
  const email =
    body.email === undefined || body.email === null || body.email === ""
      ? null
      : stringValue(body.email, "E-mail", 254).toLowerCase();
  if (email && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email))
    fail(400, "VALIDATION", "Informe um e-mail válido.");
  const token = Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)), (b) =>
    b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"),
  ).join("");
  const tokenHash = await hashToken(token),
    id = uuid(),
    expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
  await c.env.DB.batch([
    c.env.DB.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO invitations(id,workspace_id,token_hash,role,email,team_id,created_by,created_at,expires_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
    ).bind(
      id,
      c.workspace.id,
      tokenHash,
      role,
      email,
      teamId,
      c.user.id,
      c.now,
      expiresAt,
    ),
    activity(
      c,
      "invitation_created",
      `Convite de ${role === "manager" ? "gestor" : "colaborador"} criado.`,
      teamId,
    ),
  ]);
  const invitation = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    `${inviteSql} WHERE i.id=? AND i.workspace_id=?`,
  )
    .bind(c.now, id, c.workspace.id)
    .first<Invitation>();
  return json(
    { invitation, token, url: `${origin}/portal?convite=${token}`, expiresAt },
    201,
  );
}
async function acceptInvitation(
  env: ApiEnvironment,
  user: User,
  existing: Membership | null,
  body: RecordBody,
) {
  allow(body, ["token"]);
  const token = stringValue(body.token, "Convite", 64);
  if (!/^[\da-f]{64}$/.test(token))
    fail(400, "INVALID_INVITATION", "O convite não é válido.");
  if (existing)
    fail(409, "ALREADY_MEMBER", "Sua conta já pertence a uma empresa.");
  const hash = await hashToken(token),
    now = new Date().toISOString();
  const invitation = await env.DB.prepare(
    "SELECT id,workspace_id AS workspaceId,email,expires_at AS expiresAt,accepted_at AS acceptedAt,revoked_at AS revokedAt FROM invitations WHERE token_hash=?",
  )
    .bind(hash)
    .first<{
      id: string;
      workspaceId: string;
      email: string | null;
      expiresAt: string;
      acceptedAt: string | null;
      revokedAt: string | null;
    }>();
  if (!invitation)
    fail(404, "INVALID_INVITATION", "Este convite não foi encontrado.");
  if (
    invitation!.acceptedAt ||
    invitation!.revokedAt ||
    invitation!.expiresAt <= now
  )
    fail(
      409,
      "INVITATION_UNAVAILABLE",
      "Este convite expirou, foi revogado ou já foi utilizado.",
    );
  if (invitation!.email && invitation!.email !== user.email)
    fail(
      403,
      "INVITATION_EMAIL",
      "Entre com o e-mail destinatário deste convite.",
    );
  const results = await env.DB.batch([
    env.DB.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO members(user_id,workspace_id,email,display_name,role,team_id,invitation_id,joined_at) SELECT ?,workspace_id,?,?,role,team_id,id,? FROM invitations WHERE token_hash=? AND accepted_at IS NULL AND revoked_at IS NULL AND expires_at>? AND (email IS NULL OR email=?)",
    ).bind(user.id, user.email, user.name, now, hash, now, user.email),
    env.DB.prepare(
      "UPDATE invitations SET accepted_at=?,accepted_by=? WHERE token_hash=? AND accepted_at IS NULL AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM members WHERE user_id=? AND invitation_id=invitations.id)",
    ).bind(now, user.id, hash, user.id),
    env.DB.prepare(
      "INSERT INTO activity(id,workspace_id,actor_id,team_id,type,description,created_at) SELECT ?,workspace_id,user_id,team_id,'member_joined',?,? FROM members WHERE user_id=? AND invitation_id=?",
    ).bind(
      uuid(),
      `${user.name} entrou na empresa.`,
      now,
      user.id,
      invitation!.id,
    ),
  ]);
  if (results[0].meta.changes !== 1)
    fail(
      409,
      "INVITATION_UNAVAILABLE",
      "Este convite não está mais disponível.",
    );
  const member = await getMembership(env, user);
  return json(
    {
      user,
      member: member ? publicMember(member) : null,
      workspace: await getWorkspace(env, invitation!.workspaceId),
    },
    201,
  );
}
async function revokeInvitation(c: Context, id: string) {
  admin(c);
  const row = await c.env.DB.prepare(
    "SELECT id,accepted_at AS acceptedAt FROM invitations WHERE id=? AND workspace_id=?",
  )
    .bind(id, c.workspace.id)
    .first<{ id: string; acceptedAt: string | null }>();
  if (!row) fail(404, "NOT_FOUND", "Convite não encontrado.");
  if (row?.acceptedAt)
    fail(409, "INVITATION_USED", "Um convite utilizado não pode ser revogado.");
  await c.env.DB.batch([
    c.env.DB.prepare(
      "UPDATE invitations SET revoked_at=? WHERE id=? AND workspace_id=? AND accepted_at IS NULL",
    ).bind(c.now, id, c.workspace.id),
    activity(c, "invitation_revoked", "Convite revogado."),
  ]);
  return json({ ok: true });
}
function csvCell(value: unknown) {
  let text = String(value ?? "");
  if (/^[\s]*[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(text)) text = "'" + text;
  return `"${text.replaceAll('"', '""')}"`;
}
async function exportDevices(c: Context) {
  elevated(c);
  const labels = {
    available: "Disponível",
    in_use: "Em uso",
    maintenance: "Manutenção",
  };
  const heading = [
    "Nome",
    "Número de série",
    "Modelo",
    "Situação",
    "Equipe",
    "Responsável",
    "Última conferência",
    "Observações",
  ];
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  // Stream bounded pages so large exports are complete without loading the
  // entire company's inventory into the Worker's limited memory.
  let cursor: { createdAt: string; id: string } | null = null;
  let cancelled = false;
  const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
    start(controller) {
      controller.enqueue(
        encoder.encode("\uFEFF" + heading.map(csvCell).join(";")),
      );
    },
    async pull(controller) {
      try {
        const rows = await c.env.DB.prepare(
          `${deviceSql} WHERE d.workspace_id=? AND d.archived_at IS NULL AND d.created_at<=?${cursor ? " AND (d.created_at>? OR (d.created_at=? AND d.id>?))" : ""} ORDER BY d.created_at ASC,d.id ASC LIMIT 250`,
        )
          .bind(
            c.workspace.id,
            c.now,
            ...(cursor ? [cursor.createdAt, cursor.createdAt, cursor.id] : []),
          )
          .all<Device>();
        if (cancelled) return;
        if (rows.results.length) {
          const lines = rows.results.map((d) =>
            [
              d.name,
              d.serial,
              d.model,
              labels[d.status],
              d.teamName ?? "",
              d.assigneeName ?? "",
              d.lastCheckAt ?? "",
              d.notes,
            ]
              .map(csvCell)
              .join(";"),
          );
          controller.enqueue(encoder.encode("\r\n" + lines.join("\r\n")));
          const last = rows.results[rows.results.length - 1];
          cursor = { createdAt: last.createdAt, id: last.id };
        }
        if (rows.results.length < 250) controller.close();
      } catch (error) {
        if (!cancelled) controller.error(error);
      }
    },
    cancel() {
      cancelled = true;
    },
  });
  return new Response(stream, {
    headers: {
      ...responseHeaders,
      "Content-Type": "text/csv; charset=utf-8",
      "Content-Disposition": 'attachment; filename="walk-talk-aparelhos.csv"',
    },
  });
}

export async function handleApi(
  request: Request,
  env: ApiEnvironment,
): Promise<Response> {
  try {
    const url = new URL(request.url),
      path = url.pathname.replace(/\/$/, ""),
      method = request.method;
    if (!path.startsWith("/api/v1/"))
      return json(
        { error: { code: "NOT_FOUND", message: "Rota não encontrada." } },
        404,
      );
    csrf(request);
    const user = identity(request);
    if (!env.DB)
      fail(
        503,
        "DATABASE_UNAVAILABLE",
        "O serviço de dados está temporariamente indisponível.",
      );
    const member = await getMembership(env, user);
    if (path === "/api/v1/session" && method === "GET")
      return json({
        user,
        member: member ? publicMember(member) : null,
        workspace: member ? await getWorkspace(env, member.workspaceId) : null,
      });
    if (path === "/api/v1/workspace" && method === "POST")
      return await createWorkspace(env, user, member, await bodyOf(request));
    if (path === "/api/v1/invitations/accept" && method === "POST")
      return await acceptInvitation(env, user, member, await bodyOf(request));
    const c = await context(env, user, member);
    if (path === "/api/v1/overview" && method === "GET")
      return await overview(c);
    if (path === "/api/v1/teams") {
      if (method === "GET") return json({ teams: await teams(c) });
      if (method === "POST") return await createTeam(c, await bodyOf(request));
    }
    if (path === "/api/v1/devices") {
      if (method === "GET") return json({ devices: await devices(c) });
      if (method === "POST") return await saveDevice(c, await bodyOf(request));
    }
    const deviceMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/v1\/devices\/([^/]+)(\/check)?$/);
    if (deviceMatch) {
      const id = decodeURIComponent(deviceMatch[1]);
      if (deviceMatch[2] && method === "POST") {
        const body = await bodyOf(request);
        allow(body, []);
        return await checkDevice(c, id);
      }
      if (!deviceMatch[2] && method === "PATCH")
        return await saveDevice(c, await bodyOf(request), id);
      if (!deviceMatch[2] && method === "DELETE")
        return await archiveDevice(c, id);
    }
    if (path === "/api/v1/members" && method === "GET")
      return json({ members: await members(c) });
    const memberMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/v1\/members\/([^/]+)$/);
    if (memberMatch && method === "PATCH")
      return await updateMember(
        c,
        decodeURIComponent(memberMatch[1]),
        await bodyOf(request),
      );
    if (path === "/api/v1/messages") {
      if (method === "GET") {
        const teamId = optionalId(url.searchParams.get("teamId"), "Canal");
        return json({ messages: await visibleMessages(c, teamId) });
      }
      if (method === "POST")
        return await createMessage(c, await bodyOf(request));
    }
    if (path === "/api/v1/invitations") {
      if (method === "GET") return json({ invitations: await invitations(c) });
      if (method === "POST")
        return await createInvitation(c, await bodyOf(request), url.origin);
    }
    const invitationMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/v1\/invitations\/([^/]+)$/);
    if (invitationMatch && method === "DELETE")
      return await revokeInvitation(c, decodeURIComponent(invitationMatch[1]));
    if (path === "/api/v1/export/devices" && method === "GET")
      return await exportDevices(c);
    return fail(404, "NOT_FOUND", "Rota ou operação não encontrada.");
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError)
      return json(
        { error: { code: error.code, message: error.message } },
        error.status,
      );
    if (error instanceof URIError)
      return json(
        {
          error: {
            code: "VALIDATION",
            message: "O identificador informado não é válido.",
          },
        },
        400,
      );
    if (
      error instanceof Error &&
      /UNIQUE constraint|SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE|SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_PRIMARYKEY/i.test(
        error.message,
      )
    )
      return json(
        {
          error: {
            code: "CONFLICT",
            message: "Este registro já existe ou o convite já foi utilizado.",
          },
        },
        409,
      );
    console.error(
      "WALK TALK API request failed",
      error instanceof Error ? error.name : "UnknownError",
    );
    return json(
      {
        error: {
          code: "INTERNAL_ERROR",
          message: "Não foi possível concluir a operação. Tente novamente.",
        },
      },
      500,
    );
  }
}
server/d1.ts
/** Minimal structural D1 contract: works with the Cloudflare binding and test adapters. */
export interface D1Result<T = unknown> {
  success: boolean;
  results: T[];
  meta: { changes?: number; [key: string]: unknown };
  error?: string;
}
export interface D1PreparedStatement {
  bind(...values: unknown[]): D1PreparedStatement;
  first<T = Record<string, unknown>>(column?: string): Promise<T | null>;
  all<T = Record<string, unknown>>(): Promise<D1Result<T>>;
  run<T = unknown>(): Promise<D1Result<T>>;
}
export interface D1Database {
  prepare(query: string): D1PreparedStatement;
  batch<T = unknown>(statements: D1PreparedStatement[]): Promise<D1Result<T>[]>;
}
export interface ApiEnvironment {
  DB: D1Database;
}
server/index.ts
export { handleApi } from "./api.ts";
export type { ApiEnvironment } from "./d1.ts";
server/test-db.ts
import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import type { D1Database, D1PreparedStatement, D1Result } from "./d1.ts";

class Prepared implements D1PreparedStatement {
  constructor(
    private readonly owner: TestDatabase,
    private readonly query: string,
    private readonly values: unknown[] = [],
  ) {}
  bind(...values: unknown[]): D1PreparedStatement {
    return new Prepared(this.owner, this.query, values);
  }
  execute<T>(): D1Result<T> {
    const statement = this.owner.sqlite.prepare(this.query);
    const values = this.values as (
      null | number | bigint | string | Uint8Array
    )[];
    if (statement.columns().length)
      return {
        success: true,
        results: statement.all(...values) as T[],
        meta: { changes: 0 },
      };
    const result = statement.run(...values);
    return {
      success: true,
      results: [],
      meta: {
        changes: Number(result.changes),
        last_row_id: Number(result.lastInsertRowid),
      },
    };
  }
  async first<T = Record<string, unknown>>(column?: string): Promise<T | null> {
    const row = this.owner.sqlite
      .prepare(this.query)
      .get(
        ...(this.values as (null | number | bigint | string | Uint8Array)[]),
      );
    if (!row) return null;
    return (column ? row[column] : row) as T;
  }
  async all<T = Record<string, unknown>>(): Promise<D1Result<T>> {
    return this.execute<T>();
  }
  async run<T = unknown>(): Promise<D1Result<T>> {
    return this.execute<T>();
  }
}

/** Applies authored/generated migrations; never reproduces the schema in tests. */
export class TestDatabase implements D1Database {
  readonly sqlite = new DatabaseSync(":memory:");
  constructor() {
    this.sqlite.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON");
    const directory =
      process.env.WALK_TALK_TEST_MIGRATIONS ||
      fileURLToPath(new URL("../drizzle/", import.meta.url));
    const migrations = readdirSync(directory)
      .filter((file) => file.endsWith(".sql"))
      .sort();
    if (!migrations.length)
      throw new Error(
        "Generate the D1 SQL migration before running API tests.",
      );
    for (const file of migrations)
      this.sqlite.exec(readFileSync(`${directory}/${file}`, "utf8"));
  }
  prepare(query: string): D1PreparedStatement {
    return new Prepared(this, query);
  }
  async batch<T = unknown>(
    statements: D1PreparedStatement[],
  ): Promise<D1Result<T>[]> {
    this.sqlite.exec("BEGIN IMMEDIATE");
    try {
      const results = statements.map((statement) =>
        (statement as Prepared).execute<T>(),
      );
      this.sqlite.exec("COMMIT");
      return results;
    } catch (error) {
      this.sqlite.exec("ROLLBACK");
      throw error;
    }
  }
  close() {
    this.sqlite.close();
  }
}
server/types.ts
/** Shared transport types. No identity or role in a write body grants permission. */
export type Role = "admin" | "manager" | "collaborator";
export type DeviceStatus = "available" | "in_use" | "maintenance";
export interface User {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  name: string;
}
export interface Workspace {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  isDemo: boolean;
}
export interface Member {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  name: string;
  role: Role;
  teamId: string | null;
  teamName: string | null;
  joinedAt: string;
}
export interface Session {
  user: User;
  member: Member | null;
  workspace: Workspace | null;
}
export interface Team {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  location: string;
  color: string;
  members: number;
  devices: number;
  createdAt: string;
  isExample: boolean;
}
export interface Device {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  serial: string;
  model: string;
  status: DeviceStatus;
  teamId: string | null;
  teamName: string | null;
  assignedTo: string | null;
  assigneeName: string | null;
  notes: string;
  lastCheckAt: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  isExample: boolean;
}
export interface Activity {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  description: string;
  actorName: string;
  createdAt: string;
}
export interface Message {
  id: string;
  body: string;
  teamId: string | null;
  authorId: string;
  authorName: string;
  authorRole: Role;
  createdAt: string;
}
export interface Invitation {
  id: string;
  role: "manager" | "collaborator";
  email: string | null;
  teamId: string | null;
  teamName: string | null;
  expiresAt: string;
  createdAt: string;
  status: "pending" | "accepted" | "revoked" | "expired";
}
export interface Overview {
  devices: {
    total: number;
    available: number;
    inUse: number;
    maintenance: number;
    checkedToday: number;
  };
  teams: number;
  members: number;
  messages: number;
  activity: Activity[];
  recentMessages: Message[];
  workspace: Workspace;
}
export interface DeviceInput {
  name: string;
  serial: string;
  model: string;
  status: DeviceStatus;
  teamId: string | null;
  assignedTo: string | null;
  notes: string;
}
export interface TeamInput {
  name: string;
  location: string;
  color: string;
}
export interface ApiErrorBody {
  error: { code: string; message: string };
}
/** Collection endpoints return an object rather than a bare array. */
export interface TeamsResponse {
  teams: Team[];
}
export interface DevicesResponse {
  devices: Device[];
}
export interface MembersResponse {
  members: Member[];
}
export interface MessagesResponse {
  messages: Message[];
}
export interface InvitationsResponse {
  invitations: Invitation[];
}
export interface InvitationCreated {
  invitation: Invitation;
  token: string;
  url: string;
  expiresAt: string;
}
src/App.tsx
"use client";
import { lazy } from "react";
import { MotionConfig } from "framer-motion";
import { Navbar } from "./components/Navbar";
import { Hero } from "./sections/Hero";
import { Footer } from "./components/Footer";
import { Cursor } from "./components/Cursor";
import { DeferredSection } from "./components/DeferredSection";
import { useAudio } from "./hooks/useAudio";
import { useInstall } from "./hooks/useInstall";
import { useExperienceEffects } from "./hooks/useExperienceEffects";
import { Enterprise } from "./sections/Enterprise";
const FieldExperience = lazy(() =>
  import("./sections/FieldExperience").then((module) => ({
    default: module.FieldExperience,
  })),
);
const Journey = lazy(() =>
  import("./sections/Journey").then((module) => ({ default: module.Journey })),
);
const Coverage = lazy(() =>
  import("./sections/Coverage").then((module) => ({
    default: module.Coverage,
  })),
);
const Benefits = lazy(() =>
  import("./sections/Benefits").then((module) => ({
    default: module.Benefits,
  })),
);
export default function App() {
  useAudio();
  useExperienceEffects();
  const installState = useInstall();
  return (
    <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">
      <a href="#conteudo" className="skip-link">
        Pular para o conteúdo
      </a>
      <Navbar />
      <main id="conteudo">
        <Hero />
        <DeferredSection id="expedicao">
          <FieldExperience />
        </DeferredSection>
        <DeferredSection id="jornada" alias="simulador">
          <Journey />
        </DeferredSection>
        <DeferredSection id="cobertura">
          <Coverage />
        </DeferredSection>
        <DeferredSection id="beneficios">
          <Benefits installState={installState} />
        </DeferredSection>
        <Enterprise />
      </main>
      <Footer />
      <Cursor />
    </MotionConfig>
  );
}

src/components/Cursor.tsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
export function Cursor() {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      !matchMedia("(pointer: fine) and (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)")
        .matches
    )
      return;
    const move = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      if (ref.current) {
        ref.current.style.transform = `translate3d(${event.clientX - 12}px,${event.clientY - 12}px,0)`;
        ref.current.style.opacity = "1";
        ref.current.classList.toggle(
          "over-link",
          !!(event.target as HTMLElement).closest("button,a"),
        );
      }
    };
    const leave = () => {
      if (ref.current) ref.current.style.opacity = "0";
    };
    window.addEventListener("pointermove", move, { passive: true });
    document.addEventListener("pointerleave", leave);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move);
      document.removeEventListener("pointerleave", leave);
    };
  }, []);
  return <div ref={ref} className="custom-cursor" aria-hidden="true" />;
}
src/components/DeferredSection.tsx
import { Suspense, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
export function DeferredSection({
  id,
  alias,
  children,
}: {
  id: string;
  alias?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if ([id, alias].includes(location.hash.slice(1))) setVisible(true);
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          setVisible(true);
          observer.disconnect();
        }
      },
      { rootMargin: "250px" },
    );
    if (ref.current) observer.observe(ref.current);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, [id, alias]);
  const fallback = (
    <div
      className={`section-placeholder placeholder-${id}`}
      role="status"
      aria-label="Carregando seção"
    >
      <span className="status-dot" />
    </div>
  );
  return (
    <div id={id} ref={ref}>
      {alias && <span id={alias} className="anchor-alias" />}
      <Suspense fallback={fallback}>{visible ? children : fallback}</Suspense>
    </div>
  );
}
src/components/EarthScene.tsx
import { Suspense, useMemo, useRef, useEffect } from "react";
import { Canvas, useFrame, useLoader, useThree } from "@react-three/fiber";
import * as THREE from "three";
import { tilt } from "../hooks/useParallax";
import { useExperience } from "../store/useExperience";
const vertex = `varying vec3 vNormal; varying vec3 vPosition; void main(){ vNormal=normalize(normalMatrix*normal); vec4 p=modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0); vPosition=p.xyz; gl_Position=projectionMatrix*p; }`;
const fragment = `varying vec3 vNormal; varying vec3 vPosition; void main(){ float rim=pow(1.0-abs(dot(normalize(vNormal),normalize(-vPosition))),3.8); gl_FragColor=vec4(0.08,0.56,1.0,rim*0.65); }`;
function FrameBudget() {
  const invalidate = useThree((state) => state.invalidate);
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (!document.hidden) invalidate();
    }, 1000 / 30);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [invalidate]);
  return null;
}
function Satellite({ angle }: { angle: number }) {
  const ref = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
  useFrame(({ clock }) => {
    if (ref.current) {
      const t = clock.elapsedTime * 0.09 + angle;
      ref.current.position.set(
        Math.cos(t) * 2.57,
        Math.sin(t) * 1.03,
        Math.sin(t) * 1.8,
      );
      ref.current.rotation.set(0.4, t, -0.5);
    }
  });
  return (
    <group ref={ref}>
      <mesh>
        <boxGeometry args={[0.11, 0.12, 0.13]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial
          color="#e8f4ff"
          metalness={0.8}
          roughness={0.25}
        />
      </mesh>
      {[-1, 1].map((side) => (
        <mesh key={side} position={[side * 0.16, 0, 0]}>
          <boxGeometry args={[0.18, 0.08, 0.015]} />
          <meshStandardMaterial
            color="#239bd1"
            emissive="#075787"
            emissiveIntensity={0.7}
          />
        </mesh>
      ))}
      <mesh position={[0, 0.1, 0]}>
        <sphereGeometry args={[0.025, 8, 8]} />
        <meshBasicMaterial color="#9cfcff" />
      </mesh>
    </group>
  );
}
function Globe({ story, onReady }: { story: boolean; onReady: () => void }) {
  const group = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
  const earth = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const packet = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
  const [day, night] = useLoader(THREE.TextureLoader, [
    "/textures/earth.jpg",
    "/textures/night.jpg",
  ]);
  useEffect(() => {
    onReady();
  }, [onReady]);
  day.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  night.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  const curve = useMemo(
    () =>
      new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3([
        new THREE.Vector3(-1.3, -0.9, 1.2),
        new THREE.Vector3(-1.85, 0.65, 1.8),
        new THREE.Vector3(0.3, 2.65, 0.6),
        new THREE.Vector3(1.65, 0.75, 1.1),
        new THREE.Vector3(1.1, -0.4, 1.5),
      ]),
    [],
  );
  const trail = useMemo(
    () => new THREE.TubeGeometry(curve, 100, 0.006, 4, false),
    [curve],
  );
  const lastPulse = useRef(0);
  const started = useRef(-99);
  useFrame(({ clock, pointer, camera }, delta) => {
    const s = useExperience.getState();
    if (group.current) {
      group.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.damp(
        group.current.rotation.x,
        (tilt.enabled ? tilt.y : pointer.y) * 0.055,
        2,
        delta,
      );
      group.current.rotation.y = THREE.MathUtils.damp(
        group.current.rotation.y,
        (tilt.enabled ? tilt.x : pointer.x) * 0.09,
        2,
        delta,
      );
    }
    if (earth.current) earth.current.rotation.y += delta * 0.025;
    if (s.pulse !== lastPulse.current) {
      lastPulse.current = s.pulse;
      started.current = clock.elapsedTime;
    }
    if (packet.current) {
      const t = story
        ? s.step / 5
        : (clock.elapsedTime - started.current) / 2.6;
      packet.current.visible = story || (t >= 0 && t <= 1) || s.ptt;
      packet.current.position.copy(
        curve.getPoint(
          Math.max(0, Math.min(1, s.ptt ? (clock.elapsedTime % 2.6) / 2.6 : t)),
        ),
      );
    }
    if (story) {
      camera.position.x = THREE.MathUtils.damp(
        camera.position.x,
        (s.journey - 0.5) * 1.2,
        2,
        delta,
      );
      camera.position.y = THREE.MathUtils.damp(
        camera.position.y,
        Math.sin((s.step / 5) * Math.PI) * 0.8,
        2,
        delta,
      );
      camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
    }
  });
  return (
    <group ref={group} rotation={[0.06, 0, -0.15]}>
      <mesh ref={earth} rotation={[0, 2.9, 0]}>
        <sphereGeometry args={[1.87, 48, 48]} />
        <meshStandardMaterial
          map={day}
          emissiveMap={night}
          emissive="#e5ecff"
          emissiveIntensity={1.15}
          roughness={0.96}
          color="#6aa1d4"
        />
      </mesh>
      <mesh>
        <sphereGeometry args={[1.905, 48, 48]} />
        <shaderMaterial
          vertexShader={vertex}
          fragmentShader={fragment}
          transparent
          blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
          depthWrite={false}
        />
      </mesh>
      {[0, 1].map((i) => (
        <mesh key={i} rotation={[Math.PI / 2 + (i ? 0.7 : -0.45), i * 0.9, 0]}>
          <torusGeometry args={[2.5 + i * 0.12, 0.004, 5, 96]} />
          <meshBasicMaterial color="#30b8df" transparent opacity={0.28} />
        </mesh>
      ))}
      <mesh geometry={trail}>
        <meshBasicMaterial color="#63e8ff" transparent opacity={0.52} />
      </mesh>
      <mesh ref={packet}>
        <sphereGeometry args={[0.045, 12, 12]} />
        <meshBasicMaterial color="#c6fcff" />
      </mesh>
      <Satellite angle={0.5} />
      <Satellite angle={3.65} />
    </group>
  );
}
export default function EarthScene({
  story = false,
  onReady,
}: {
  story?: boolean;
  onReady: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <Canvas
      dpr={[1, 1.25]}
      frameloop="demand"
      camera={{ position: [0, 0, 6.7], fov: 44 }}
      gl={{ alpha: true, antialias: true, powerPreference: "low-power" }}
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <FrameBudget />
      <ambientLight intensity={0.65} />
      <directionalLight position={[-3, 4, 6]} intensity={2.6} color="#a0dfff" />
      <Suspense fallback={null}>
        <Globe story={story} onReady={onReady} />
      </Suspense>
    </Canvas>
  );
}
