src/components/Globe.tsx
import {
  Component,
  lazy,
  Suspense,
  useEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
  useCallback,
} from "react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { useMedia } from "../hooks/useExperienceEffects";
const EarthScene = lazy(() => import("./EarthScene"));
function EarthFallback() {
  return (
    <div className="earth-fallback" aria-hidden="true">
      <div className="earth-flat" />
      <div className="flat-orbit one" />
      <div className="flat-orbit two" />
    </div>
  );
}
class SceneBoundary extends Component<
  { children: ReactNode },
  { failed: boolean }
> {
  state = { failed: false };
  static getDerivedStateFromError() {
    return { failed: true };
  }
  render() {
    return this.state.failed ? <EarthFallback /> : this.props.children;
  }
}
export function Globe({ story = false }: { story?: boolean }) {
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const onReady = useCallback(() => setReady(true), []);
  const reduced = useMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
  const narrow = useMedia("(max-width: 600px)");
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(!story);
  const [webgl, setWebgl] = useState<boolean | undefined>(undefined);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const device = (
    typeof navigator !== "undefined" ? navigator : {}
  ) as Navigator & {
    deviceMemory?: number;
    connection?: { saveData?: boolean };
  };
  const lowPower =
    reduced ||
    !!device.connection?.saveData ||
    (narrow &&
      ((device.deviceMemory ?? 4) <= 4 ||
        (device.hardwareConcurrency ?? 4) <= 4));
  const lightweight = lowPower || !webgl;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (lowPower || !visible || webgl !== undefined) return;
    // Canvas is created only after the initial UI has painted. Its error boundary
    // handles unsupported WebGL without allocating a second test context.
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setWebgl(true), 700);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [lowPower, visible, webgl]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => setVisible(entry.isIntersecting),
      { rootMargin: "180px" },
    );
    if (ref.current) observer.observe(ref.current);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);
  return (
    <div className={`globe-wrap ${story ? "story-globe" : ""}`} ref={ref}>
      {lightweight || !visible ? (
        <EarthFallback />
      ) : (
        <SceneBoundary>
          <Suspense fallback={<EarthFallback />}>
            <>
              {!ready && <EarthFallback />}
              <EarthScene story={story} onReady={onReady} />
            </>
          </Suspense>
        </SceneBoundary>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
src/components/Navbar.tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Globe2, Volume2, VolumeX, ArrowUpRight, Menu, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useExperience } from "../store/useExperience";
import { unlockAudio } from "../hooks/useAudio";
export function Logo() {
  return (
    <a className="logo" href="#inicio">
      <Globe2 strokeWidth={1.3} />
      <span>
        WALK<span className="cyan">TALK</span>
        <small>COMUNICAÇÃO SEM FRONTEIRAS</small>
      </span>
    </a>
  );
}
const links = [
  { id: "inicio", label: "Início", href: "#inicio" },
  { id: "produto", label: "Produto", href: "/produto" },
  { id: "jornada", label: "Como funciona", href: "#jornada" },
  { id: "solucoes", label: "Empresas", href: "/solucoes" },
  { id: "saude", label: "Saúde", href: "/saude" },
  { id: "ajuda", label: "Ajuda", href: "/ajuda" },
];
export function Navbar() {
  const [active, setActive] = useState("inicio");
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const header = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const menuButton = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const sound = useExperience((s) => s.sound);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const dismissOutside = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      if (
        event.target instanceof Node &&
        !header.current?.contains(event.target)
      )
        setOpen(false);
    };
    const dismissKeyboard = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key !== "Escape") return;
      event.preventDefault();
      setOpen(false);
      menuButton.current?.focus();
    };
    const dismissDesktop = () => {
      if (matchMedia("(min-width: 601px)").matches) setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", dismissOutside);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", dismissKeyboard);
    window.addEventListener("resize", dismissDesktop);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", dismissOutside);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", dismissKeyboard);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", dismissDesktop);
    };
  }, [open]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const update = () => {
      const height = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - innerHeight;
      setProgress(height > 0 ? scrollY / height : 0);
      let current = "inicio";
      for (const link of links) {
        if (
          (document.getElementById(link.id)?.getBoundingClientRect().top ??
            Infinity) <
          innerHeight * 0.4
        )
          current = link.id;
      }
      setActive(current);
    };
    update();
    window.addEventListener("scroll", update, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("resize", update);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("scroll", update);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", update);
    };
  }, []);
  return (
    <header className="header" ref={header}>
      <div className="nav-shell">
        <Logo />
        <nav
          id="primary-navigation"
          aria-label="Navegação principal"
          className={open ? "open" : ""}
        >
          {links.map((link) => (
            <a
              key={link.id}
              href={link.href}
              aria-current={active === link.id ? "location" : undefined}
              onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
            >
              {link.label}
            </a>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="nav-actions">
          <button
            className="icon-button sound-toggle hover:text-orbit transition-colors"
            aria-label={sound ? "Silenciar sons" : "Ativar sons"}
            aria-pressed={sound}
            onClick={() => {
              unlockAudio();
              useExperience.getState().toggleSound();
            }}
          >
            {sound ? <Volume2 size={17} /> : <VolumeX size={17} />}
          </button>
          <a className="nav-cta" href="/portal" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
            Portal da empresa <ArrowUpRight size={15} />
          </a>
          <button
            ref={menuButton}
            className="icon-button menu-toggle"
            aria-label={open ? "Fechar menu" : "Abrir menu"}
            aria-expanded={open}
            aria-controls="primary-navigation"
            onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}
          >
            {open ? <X /> : <Menu />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div
        className="reading-progress"
        style={{ transform: `scaleX(${progress})` }}
      />
    </header>
  );
}
src/components/PTTButton.tsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { AudioLines, Radio, ArrowUpRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useExperience } from "../store/useExperience";
import { unlockAudio } from "../hooks/useAudio";
export function PTTButton() {
  const sound = useExperience((s) => s.sound);
  const active = useExperience((s) => s.ptt);
  const pulse = useExperience((s) => s.pulse);
  const held = useRef(false);
  const begin = () => {
    if (useExperience.getState().sound) unlockAudio();
    held.current = true;
    useExperience.getState().setPTT(true);
  };
  const end = () => {
    if (held.current) {
      held.current = false;
      useExperience.getState().setPTT(false);
    }
  };
  useEffect(() => {
    const down = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const element = event.target as HTMLElement;
      if (
        event.code === "Space" &&
        !event.repeat &&
        !element.closest(
          "button, a, input, textarea, select, [contenteditable]",
        )
      ) {
        event.preventDefault();
        begin();
      }
    };
    const up = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.code === "Space") end();
    };
    const hidden = () => {
      if (document.hidden) end();
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", down);
    window.addEventListener("keyup", up);
    window.addEventListener("blur", end);
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", hidden);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", down);
      window.removeEventListener("keyup", up);
      window.removeEventListener("blur", end);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", hidden);
      end();
    };
  }, []);
  return (
    <div className="ptt-module">
      <button
        id="ptt"
        className={`ptt-button ${active ? "transmitting" : ""}`}
        aria-label={
          active
            ? "Transmitindo... PTT: solte para enviar o sinal simulado"
            : "Segure para falar. PTT: simular transmissão"
        }
        aria-pressed={active}
        onPointerDown={(e) => {
          if (e.button !== 0) return;
          e.preventDefault();
          e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
          begin();
        }}
        onPointerUp={end}
        onPointerCancel={end}
        onLostPointerCapture={end}
        onBlur={end}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if ([" ", "Enter"].includes(e.key)) {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (!e.repeat) begin();
          }
        }}
        onKeyUp={(e) => {
          if ([" ", "Enter"].includes(e.key)) {
            e.preventDefault();
            end();
          }
        }}
      >
        {active ? <AudioLines size={22} /> : <Radio size={22} />}{" "}
        <span>{active ? "Transmitindo..." : "Segure para falar"}</span>
        <ArrowUpRight size={18} />
      </button>
      <div className="ptt-hint" aria-live="polite">
        {active ? (
          <span className="orange">Sua mensagem está em movimento</span>
        ) : pulse ? (
          <span className="cyan">Sinal enviado ao satélite · simulação</span>
        ) : (
          <>
            {sound ? "SOM ATIVADO" : "SOM DESLIGADO"}{" "}
            <span>
              ou segure <kbd>espaço</kbd>
            </span>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
src/components/PWARegistration.tsx
"use client";
import { useEffect } from "react";
export function PWARegistration() {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!("serviceWorker" in navigator) || import.meta.env.DEV) return;
    void navigator.serviceWorker
      .register("/sw.js", { scope: "/" })
      .then((registration) => registration.update())
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);
  return null;
}

export function ProductStage() {

    <div
      id="aparelho"
      className={`product-stage product-scene-${scene} ${active ? "is-transmitting" : ""}`}

          e.pointerType !== "mouse" ||
          matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches
        )
          return;
        const box = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
        e.currentTarget.style.setProperty(
          "--product-x",
          `${(e.clientX - box.left - box.width / 2) * 0.018}px`,
        );
        e.currentTarget.style.setProperty(
          "--product-y",
          `${(e.clientY - box.top - box.height / 2) * 0.012}px`,
        );
      }}
      onPointerLeave={() => {
        stage.current?.style.setProperty("--product-x", "0px");
        stage.current?.style.setProperty("--product-y", "0px");

src/components/TransmissionFeedback.tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AudioLines, Check, Radio, Satellite } from "lucide-react";
import { useExperience } from "../store/useExperience";
import "./transmission-feedback.css";

const nodes = [
  { label: "Seu aparelho", icon: Radio },
  { label: "Satélite", icon: Satellite },
  { label: "Destinatário", icon: AudioLines },
];

/** Visual feedback only: this demo never captures or transmits microphone audio. */
export function TransmissionFeedback() {
  const ptt = useExperience((state) => state.ptt);
  const pulse = useExperience((state) => state.pulse);
  const [completedPulse, setCompletedPulse] = useState(0);
  const phase = ptt
    ? "talking"
    : pulse > completedPulse
      ? "transit"
      : pulse > 0
        ? "received"
        : "ready";

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ptt || pulse === 0) return;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setCompletedPulse(pulse), 2400);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [ptt, pulse]);

  const status = {
    ready: "Pronto para conectar",
    talking: "Preparando sua mensagem",
    transit: "Sinal em trânsito",
    received: "Mensagem recebida",
  }[phase];
  const instruction = {
    ready: "Segure o botão PTT para começar.",
    talking: "Solte o PTT para acompanhar o sinal.",
    transit: "Do seu aparelho ao outro lado da conversa.",
    received: "Uma conexão completa. Experimente de novo.",
  }[phase];

  return (
    <section
      className={`transmission-feedback transmission-${phase}`}
      aria-label="Acompanhe a transmissão simulada"
    >
      <ol className="transmission-track" aria-label="Caminho do sinal">
        {nodes.map(({ label, icon: Icon }, index) => (
          <li
            key={label}
            className={`transmission-node transmission-node-${index}`}
          >
            {index < nodes.length - 1 && (
              <span className="transmission-connector" aria-hidden="true">
                <i key={`${pulse}-${phase}`} />
              </span>
            )}
            <span className="transmission-node-icon" aria-hidden="true">
              {phase === "received" && index === 2 ? (
                <Check size={17} />
              ) : (
                <Icon size={17} />
              )}
            </span>
            <span>{label}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
      <div className="transmission-result">
        <div
          className="transmission-status"
          role="status"
          aria-live="polite"
          aria-atomic="true"
        >
          <span className="transmission-status-light" aria-hidden="true" />
          {status}
        </div>
        <p>{instruction}</p>
        <small>Simulação visual. Nenhuma voz é gravada ou enviada.</small>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

src/hooks/useAudio.ts
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useExperience } from "../store/useExperience";
let context: AudioContext | undefined;
let noise: AudioBufferSourceNode | undefined;
let master: GainNode | undefined;
export function unlockAudio() {
  try {
    context ??= new AudioContext();
    void context.resume().catch(() => {});
  } catch {
    /* Audio is optional. */
  }
}
function tone(frequency: number, duration: number) {
  if (!context || !master) return;
  const oscillator = context.createOscillator();
  const gain = context.createGain();
  oscillator.frequency.value = frequency;
  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.09, context.currentTime);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, context.currentTime + duration);
  oscillator.connect(gain).connect(master);
  oscillator.start();
  oscillator.stop(context.currentTime + duration);
  oscillator.onended = () => {
    oscillator.disconnect();
    gain.disconnect();
  };
}
export function useAudio() {
  const ptt = useExperience((s) => s.ptt);
  const sound = useExperience((s) => s.sound);
  const pulse = useExperience((s) => s.pulse);
  useEffect(() => {
    noise?.stop();
    noise = undefined;
    master?.disconnect();
    master = undefined;
    if (!sound || !context) return;
    master = context.createGain();
    master.gain.value = 0.45;
    master.connect(context.destination);
    if (ptt) {
      tone(520, 0.06);
      const buffer = context.createBuffer(
        1,
        context.sampleRate,
        context.sampleRate,
      );
      const channel = buffer.getChannelData(0);
      for (let i = 0; i < channel.length; i++)
        channel[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.035;
      noise = context.createBufferSource();
      noise.buffer = buffer;
      noise.loop = true;
      const filter = context.createBiquadFilter();
      filter.type = "bandpass";
      filter.frequency.value = 1800;
      noise.connect(filter).connect(master);
      noise.start();
    } else if (pulse > 0) tone(1100, 0.16);
    return () => {
      noise?.stop();
      noise = undefined;
      if (master) {
        master.gain.value = 0;
        master.disconnect();
        master = undefined;
      }
    };
  }, [ptt, sound, pulse]);
}
src/hooks/useExperienceEffects.ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useExperience } from "../store/useExperience";

export function useMedia(query: string) {
  const [matches, setMatches] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const media = matchMedia(query);
    const update = () => setMatches(media.matches);
    media.addEventListener("change", update);
    update();
    return () => media.removeEventListener("change", update);
  }, [query]);
  return matches;
}

export function useExperienceEffects() {
  const reduced = useMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)");
  useEffect(() => {
    let disposed = false;
    let cleanup: (() => void) | undefined;
    // Touch devices already have native momentum scrolling.
    if (!reduced && matchMedia("(pointer: fine)").matches) {
      void Promise.all([import("lenis"), import("gsap")]).then(
        ([{ default: Lenis }, { gsap }]) => {
          if (disposed) return;
          const lenis = new Lenis({
            duration: 1.05,
            smoothWheel: true,
            anchors: { offset: -90 },
          });
          const tick = (time: number) => lenis.raf(time * 1000);
          gsap.ticker.add(tick);
          cleanup = () => {
            gsap.ticker.remove(tick);
            lenis.destroy();
          };
        },
      );
    }
    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      cleanup?.();
    };
  }, [reduced]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let disposed = false;
    let cleanup: (() => void) | undefined;
    // Load storytelling only as its section approaches the viewport.
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
        observer.disconnect();
        void Promise.all([import("gsap"), import("gsap/ScrollTrigger")]).then(
          ([{ gsap }, { ScrollTrigger }]) => {
            if (disposed) return;
            gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);
            const trigger = ScrollTrigger.create({
              trigger: "#jornada",
              start: "top 75%",
              end: "bottom 35%",
              onUpdate: (self) => {
                const state = useExperience.getState();
                state.setJourney(self.progress);
                state.setScrollStep(Math.min(5, Math.floor(self.progress * 6)));
              },
            });
            const resize = new ResizeObserver(() => trigger.refresh());
            const target = document.getElementById("jornada");
            if (target) resize.observe(target);
            cleanup = () => {
              resize.disconnect();
              trigger.kill();
            };
          },
        );
      },
      { rootMargin: "150px" },
    );
    const resume = () => useExperience.getState().resumeScroll();
    const keyboardScroll = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (["PageDown", "PageUp", "ArrowDown", "ArrowUp"].includes(event.key))
        resume();
    };
    window.addEventListener("wheel", resume, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("touchmove", resume, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("keydown", keyboardScroll);
    const section = document.getElementById("jornada");
    if (section) observer.observe(section);
    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      observer.disconnect();
      cleanup?.();
      window.removeEventListener("wheel", resume);
      window.removeEventListener("touchmove", resume);
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", keyboardScroll);
    };
  }, []);
}
src/hooks/useInstall.ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
interface InstallEvent extends Event {
  prompt(): Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: "accepted" | "dismissed" }>;
}
export function useInstall() {
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState<InstallEvent | null>(null);
  const [installed, setInstalled] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  useEffect(() => {
    setInstalled(matchMedia("(display-mode: standalone)").matches);
    const before = (event: Event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      setPrompt(event as InstallEvent);
    };
    const done = () => {
      setInstalled(true);
      setPrompt(null);
      setMessage(
        "WALK TALK instalado. Sua experiência está pronta para acompanhar você.",
      );
    };
    window.addEventListener("beforeinstallprompt", before);
    window.addEventListener("appinstalled", done);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("beforeinstallprompt", before);
      window.removeEventListener("appinstalled", done);
    };
  }, []);
  const install = async () => {
    if (prompt) {
      try {
        await prompt.prompt();
        const result = await prompt.userChoice;
        setPrompt(null);
        setMessage(
          result.outcome === "accepted"
            ? "Instalação solicitada. Conclua a etapa no seu navegador."
            : "Você pode instalar depois pelo menu do navegador.",
        );
      } catch {
        setMessage("Abra o menu do navegador e escolha Instalar aplicativo.");
      }
    } else
      setMessage(
        "No Chrome ou Edge, abra o menu e escolha “Instalar aplicativo”. No iPhone, use Safari → Compartilhar → Adicionar à Tela de Início. A instalação depende do navegador e de uma conexão HTTPS.",
      );
  };
  return { install, available: !!prompt, installed, message };
}
src/hooks/useParallax.ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
export const tilt = { x: 0, y: 0, enabled: false };
type MotionAPI = typeof DeviceOrientationEvent & {
  requestPermission?: () => Promise<string>;
};
export function useParallax() {
  const [enabled, setEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [supported, setSupported] = useState(false);
  useEffect(
    () => setSupported(typeof DeviceOrientationEvent !== "undefined"),
    [],
  );
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) {
      tilt.enabled = false;
      return;
    }
    const update = (event: DeviceOrientationEvent) => {
      tilt.x = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, (event.gamma ?? 0) / 35));
      tilt.y = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, ((event.beta ?? 30) - 30) / 35));
      tilt.enabled = true;
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
        "--tilt-x",
        `${tilt.x * 6}deg`,
      );
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
        "--tilt-y",
        `${tilt.y * 6}deg`,
      );
    };
    window.addEventListener("deviceorientation", update);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("deviceorientation", update);
      tilt.enabled = false;
      document.documentElement.style.removeProperty("--tilt-x");
      document.documentElement.style.removeProperty("--tilt-y");
    };
  }, [enabled]);
  const toggle = async () => {
    if (enabled) {
      setEnabled(false);
      setMessage("");
      return;
    }
    if (matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) {
      setMessage(
        "Movimento reduzido está ativo nas preferências do dispositivo.",
      );
      return;
    }
    try {
      const api = DeviceOrientationEvent as MotionAPI;
      if (
        api.requestPermission &&
        (await api.requestPermission()) !== "granted"
      ) {
        setMessage("Acesso ao movimento não autorizado.");
        return;
      }
      setEnabled(true);
      setMessage("Incline o celular para explorar a Terra 3D.");
    } catch {
      setMessage("O sensor não está disponível neste navegador.");
    }
  };
  return { supported, enabled, message, toggle };
}
src/hooks/usePlayback.ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useExperience } from "../store/useExperience";
/** Pauseable, bounded six-step demo. Stops when hidden, offscreen or operated manually. */
export function usePlayback() {
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!playing) return;
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      const state = useExperience.getState();
      if (state.step >= 5) {
        setPlaying(false);
        return;
      }
      state.setStep(state.step + 1);
    }, 2800);
    const hide = () => {
      if (document.hidden) setPlaying(false);
    };
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => {
      if (!entry.isIntersecting) setPlaying(false);
    });
    const panel = document.getElementById("simulator-panel");
    if (panel) observer.observe(panel);
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", hide);
    return () => {
      clearInterval(timer);
      observer.disconnect();
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", hide);
    };
  }, [playing]);
  const selectStep = (step: number) => {
    setPlaying(false);
    useExperience.getState().setStep(step);
  };
  const toggle = () => {
    if (!playing && useExperience.getState().step === 5)
      useExperience.getState().setStep(0);
    setPlaying(!playing);
  };
  return { playing, toggle, selectStep };
}

import { StrictMode } from "react";
import { createRoot, hydrateRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import "@fontsource/space-grotesk/latin-400.css";
import "@fontsource/space-grotesk/latin-500.css";
import "@fontsource/space-grotesk/latin-600.css";
import "@fontsource/space-grotesk/latin-700.css";
import "@fontsource/inter/latin-400.css";
import "@fontsource/inter/latin-500.css";
import "lenis/dist/lenis.css";
import "./index.css";
import App from "./App";
const root = document.getElementById("root")!;
const app = (
  <StrictMode>
    <App />
  </StrictMode>
);
if (root.hasChildNodes()) hydrateRoot(root, app);
else createRoot(root).render(app);
src/portal/DeviceView.tsx
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  Archive,
  ArrowDownToLine,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  ClipboardCheck,
  LoaderCircle,
  Pencil,
  Plus,
  Radio,
  Search,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import type {
  Device,
  DeviceInput,
  DevicesResponse,
  MembersResponse,
  Session,
  TeamsResponse,
} from "../../server/types";
import { api, formatDate, notify, useResource } from "./api";
import { EmptyState, ErrorNotice, Loading, Modal, Panel } from "./ui";
const statusText = {
  available: "Disponível",
  in_use: "Em operação",
  maintenance: "Manutenção",
};
export function DeviceView({ session }: { session: Session }) {
  const devices = useResource<DevicesResponse>("/devices");
  const teams = useResource<TeamsResponse>("/teams");
  const members = useResource<MembersResponse>("/members");
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("all");
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Device | "new" | null>(null);
  const [archive, setArchive] = useState<Device | null>(null);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState("");
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const manage = session.member?.role !== "collaborator";
  const visible = useMemo(
    () =>
      (devices.data?.devices || []).filter(
        (d) =>
          (filter === "all" || d.status === filter) &&
          `${d.name} ${d.serial} ${d.teamName || ""}`
            .toLocaleLowerCase("pt-BR")
            .includes(search.toLocaleLowerCase("pt-BR")),
      ),
    [devices.data, search, filter],
  );
  const check = async (device: Device) => {
    setChecking(device.id);
    try {
      await api(`/devices/${device.id}/check`, { method: "POST", body: "{}" });
      notify("Verificação registrada pela sua conta.");
      await devices.reload();
    } catch (e) {
      notify(
        e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Não foi possível registrar.",
        "error",
      );
    } finally {
      setChecking(null);
    }
  };
  return (
    <>
      <div className="wt-page-heading">
        <div>
          <span className="wt-eyebrow">ORGANIZAÇÃO QUE ACOMPANHA</span>
          <h1>
            Cada aparelho.
            <br />
            No lugar certo.
          </h1>
          <p>
            Acompanhe disponibilidade, responsáveis e verificações da equipe.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="wt-heading-actions">
          {manage && (
            <a
              className="wt-button-secondary"
              href="/api/v1/export/devices"
              download
            >
              <ArrowDownToLine size={16} /> Exportar CSV
            </a>
          )}
          {manage && (
            <button className="wt-button" onClick={() => setEditing("new")}>
              <Plus size={17} /> Cadastrar equipamento
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="wt-equipment-summary">
        <div>
          <Radio size={20} />
          <strong>{devices.data?.devices.length ?? "—"}</strong>
          <span>equipamentos</span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span className="wt-dot available" />
          <strong>
            {devices.data?.devices.filter((d) => d.status === "available")
              .length ?? "—"}
          </strong>
          <span>disponíveis</span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span className="wt-dot in-use" />
          <strong>
            {devices.data?.devices.filter((d) => d.status === "in_use")
              .length ?? "—"}
          </strong>
          <span>em operação</span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <span className="wt-dot maintenance" />
          <strong>
            {devices.data?.devices.filter((d) => d.status === "maintenance")
              .length ?? "—"}
          </strong>
          <span>em manutenção</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <Panel className="wt-equipment-panel">
        <div className="wt-device-toolbar">
          <label className="wt-search">
            <Search size={18} />
            <input
              aria-label="Buscar equipamentos"
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Buscar por nome, série ou equipe"
            />
            {search && (
              <button aria-label="Limpar busca" onClick={() => setSearch("")}>
                <X size={14} />
              </button>
            )}
          </label>
          <label className="wt-filter">
            <SlidersHorizontal size={16} />
            <span className="wt-sr-only">Filtrar equipamentos por status</span>
            <select value={filter} onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="all">Todos os status</option>
              <option value="available">Disponíveis</option>
              <option value="in_use">Em operação</option>
              <option value="maintenance">Manutenção</option>
            </select>
          </label>
        </div>
        {devices.error ? (
          <ErrorNotice
            message={devices.error}
            onRetry={() => void devices.reload()}
          />
        ) : devices.loading && !devices.data ? (
          <Loading />
        ) : !visible.length ? (
          <EmptyState
            icon={Radio}
            title={
              search || filter !== "all"
                ? "Nenhum aparelho encontrado"
                : "Seu primeiro equipamento está por vir"
            }
            description={
              search || filter !== "all"
                ? "Ajuste a busca ou o filtro para encontrar outro aparelho."
                : "Organize seus aparelhos, responsáveis e equipes em um só lugar."
            }
            action={
              manage && !search && filter === "all" ? (
                <button
                  className="wt-button-secondary"
                  onClick={() => setEditing("new")}
                >
                  Cadastrar primeiro equipamento
                </button>
              ) : undefined
            }
          />
        ) : (
          <div className="wt-device-cards">
            {visible.map((device) => (
              <article className="wt-equipment-card" key={device.id}>
                <div className="wt-equipment-top">
                  <span className="wt-equipment-image">
                    <img
                      src="/product/walk-talk-mobile.webp"
                      alt=""
                      width="480"
                      height="720"
                      loading="lazy"
                    />
                  </span>
                  <span className={`wt-status status-${device.status}`}>
                    <i />
                    {statusText[device.status]}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="wt-equipment-title">
                  <h2>{device.name}</h2>
                  {device.isExample && (
                    <span className="wt-example-label">EXEMPLO</span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="wt-device-serial">
                  {device.model} · {device.serial}
                </p>
                <dl>
                  <div>
                    <dt>Equipe</dt>
                    <dd>{device.teamName || "Sem equipe"}</dd>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <dt>Responsável</dt>
                    <dd>{device.assigneeName || "Não atribuído"}</dd>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <dt>Última verificação</dt>
                    <dd>{formatDate(device.lastCheckAt, true)}</dd>
                  </div>
                </dl>
                {device.notes && (
                  <p className="wt-device-notes">{device.notes}</p>
                )}
                <div className="wt-equipment-actions">
                  {(manage || device.assignedTo === session.user.id) && (
                    <button
                      className="wt-text-button"
                      disabled={checking === device.id}
                      onClick={() => void check(device)}
                    >
                      {checking === device.id ? (
                        <LoaderCircle size={16} />
                      ) : (
                        <ClipboardCheck size={16} />
                      )}{" "}
                      Verificar
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {manage && (
                    <div>
                      <button
                        className="wt-icon-button"
                        aria-label={`Editar ${device.name}`}
                        onClick={() => setEditing(device)}
                      >
                        <Pencil size={16} />
                      </button>
                      <button
                        className="wt-icon-button"
                        aria-label={`Arquivar ${device.name}`}
                        onClick={() => {
                          setArchive(device);
                          setActionError("");
                        }}
                      >
                        <Archive size={16} />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </article>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="wt-panel-footnote">
          <CheckCircle2 size={14} />
          <span>
            Verificar registra uma conferência manual. Este portal não mede
            sinal, bateria ou localização.
          </span>
        </div>
      </Panel>
      {editing && (
        <DeviceEditor
          device={editing === "new" ? null : editing}
          teams={teams.data?.teams ?? []}
          members={members.data?.members ?? []}
          onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
          onSaved={() => {
            setEditing(null);
            void devices.reload();
          }}
        />
      )}
      {archive && (
        <Modal
          title="Arquivar equipamento?"
          onClose={() => {
            if (!pending) setArchive(null);
          }}
        >
          <div className="wt-confirm-copy">
            <span className="wt-confirm-icon">
              <Archive size={28} />
            </span>
            <p>
              <strong>{archive.name}</strong> sairá da lista de equipamentos
              ativos. O histórico do registro será preservado.
            </p>
          </div>
          {actionError && <ErrorNotice message={actionError} />}
          <div className="wt-form-actions">
            <button
              className="wt-button-secondary"
              disabled={pending}
              onClick={() => setArchive(null)}
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>
            <button
              className="wt-button wt-button-danger"
              disabled={pending}
              onClick={async () => {
                setPending(true);
                setActionError("");
                try {
                  await api(`/devices/${archive.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
                  setArchive(null);
                  notify("Equipamento arquivado.");
                  void devices.reload();
                } catch (e) {
                  setActionError(
                    e instanceof Error
                      ? e.message
                      : "Não foi possível arquivar.",
                  );
                } finally {
                  setPending(false);
                }
              }}
            >
              {pending ? "Arquivando…" : "Arquivar equipamento"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
function DeviceEditor({
  device,
  teams,
  members,
  onClose,
  onSaved,
}: {
  device: Device | null;
  teams: TeamsResponse["teams"];
  members: MembersResponse["members"];
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaved: () => void;
}) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState<DeviceInput>({
    name: device?.name ?? "",
    serial: device?.serial ?? "",
    model: device?.model ?? "WALK TALK",
    status: device?.status ?? "available",
    teamId: device?.teamId ?? null,
    assignedTo: device?.assignedTo ?? null,
    notes: device?.notes ?? "",
  });
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const update = <K extends keyof DeviceInput>(key: K, value: DeviceInput[K]) =>
    setForm({ ...form, [key]: value });
  return (
    <Modal
      title={device ? "Editar equipamento" : "Cadastrar equipamento"}
      onClose={() => {
        if (!pending) onClose();
      }}
    >
      <form
        onSubmit={async (event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          setPending(true);
          setError("");
          try {
            await api(device ? `/devices/${device.id}` : "/devices", {
              method: device ? "PATCH" : "POST",
              body: JSON.stringify(form),
            });
            notify(
              device ? "Equipamento atualizado." : "Equipamento cadastrado.",
            );
            onSaved();
          } catch (e) {
            setError(
              e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Não foi possível salvar.",
            );
          } finally {
            setPending(false);
          }
        }}
      >
        <div className="wt-field">
          <label htmlFor="device-name" className="wt-label">
            Nome do equipamento
          </label>
          <input
            id="device-name"
            className="wt-input"
            value={form.name}
            onChange={(e) => update("name", e.target.value)}
            required
            minLength={2}
            maxLength={100}
            placeholder="Ex.: Rádio da equipe de campo"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="wt-form-grid">
          <div className="wt-field">
            <label htmlFor="device-serial" className="wt-label">
              Número de série / patrimônio
            </label>
            <input
              id="device-serial"
              className="wt-input"
              value={form.serial}
              onChange={(e) => update("serial", e.target.value)}
              required
              maxLength={100}
              placeholder="WT-001"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="wt-field">
            <label htmlFor="device-model" className="wt-label">
              Modelo
            </label>
            <input
              id="device-model"
              className="wt-input"
              value={form.model}
              onChange={(e) => update("model", e.target.value)}
              required
              maxLength={100}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="wt-field">
          <label htmlFor="device-status" className="wt-label">
            Status
          </label>
          <select
            id="device-status"
            className="wt-input"
            value={form.status}
            onChange={(e) =>
              update("status", e.target.value as Device["status"])
            }
          >
            <option value="available">Disponível</option>
            <option value="in_use">Em operação</option>
            <option value="maintenance">Manutenção</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="wt-form-grid">
          <div className="wt-field">
            <label htmlFor="device-team" className="wt-label">
              Equipe
            </label>
            <select
              id="device-team"
              className="wt-input"
              value={form.teamId ?? ""}
              onChange={(e) => update("teamId", e.target.value || null)}
            >
              <option value="">Sem equipe</option>
              {teams.map((team) => (
                <option value={team.id} key={team.id}>
                  {team.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="wt-field">
            <label htmlFor="device-assignee" className="wt-label">
              Responsável
            </label>
            <select
              id="device-assignee"
              className="wt-input"
              value={form.assignedTo ?? ""}
              onChange={(e) => update("assignedTo", e.target.value || null)}
            >
              <option value="">Não atribuído</option>
              {members.map((member) => (
                <option value={member.id} key={member.id}>
                  {member.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="wt-field">
          <label htmlFor="device-notes" className="wt-label">
            Observações operacionais <span>(opcional)</span>
          </label>
          <textarea
            id="device-notes"
            className="wt-input"
            rows={3}
            value={form.notes}
            onChange={(e) => update("notes", e.target.value)}
            maxLength={500}
            placeholder="Ex.: Guardado no armário da base."
          />
        </div>
        {error && <ErrorNotice message={error} />}
        <div className="wt-form-actions">
          <button
            className="wt-button-secondary"
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            disabled={pending}
          >
            Cancelar
          </button>
          <button className="wt-button" disabled={pending}>
            {pending ? <LoaderCircle size={17} /> : <Check size={17} />}{" "}
            {pending
              ? "Salvando…"
              : device
                ? "Salvar alterações"
                : "Cadastrar equipamento"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function normalize(value: string) {
  return value
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .toLowerCase();
}

export function HealthPage({ standalone = false }: { standalone?: boolean }) {
  const Content = standalone ? "main" : "div";
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [observed, setObserved] = useState<Set<number>>(() => new Set());
  const filteredTopics = useMemo(() => {
    const term = normalize(query.trim());
    return topics.filter((topic) =>
      normalize(
        `${topic.tag} ${topic.title} ${topic.summary} ${topic.body} ${topic.action}`,
      ).includes(term),
    );
  }, [query]);
  const toggleObservation = (index: number) => {
    setObserved((current) => {
      const next = new Set(current);
      if (next.has(index)) next.delete(index);
      else next.add(index);
      return next;
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className={`wt-health ${standalone ? "wt-health-standalone" : ""}`}>
      {standalone && (
        <header className="wt-health-topbar">
          <a href="/" className="wt-health-brand">
            <Globe2 size={28} strokeWidth={1.5} />
            <span>
              WALK<span>TALK</span>
              <small>COMUNICAÇÃO QUE CUIDA</small>
            </span>
          </a>
          <nav aria-label="Navegação de saúde ocupacional">
            <a href="/produto">Produto</a>
            <a href="/solucoes">Soluções</a>
            <a href="/ajuda">Ajuda</a>
            <a href="/portal" className="wt-health-portal-link">
              Portal da empresa <ArrowUpRight size={15} />
            </a>
          </nav>
        </header>
      )}
      <Content className="wt-health-content">
        <section className="wt-health-hero" aria-labelledby="wt-health-title">
          <div className="wt-health-hero-copy">
            <div className="wt-health-eyebrow">
              <span /> SAÚDE OCUPACIONAL
            </div>
            <h1 id="wt-health-title">
              CUIDAR DA ROTINA.
              <br />
              <span>PROTEGER O FUTURO.</span>
            </h1>
            <p>
              Um trabalho melhor começa com informação. Conheça cuidados gerais
              para prevenir LER/DORT e tornar a rotina mais confortável.
            </p>
            <div className="wt-health-hero-actions">
              <a href="#wt-health-topics" className="wt-health-button">
                Explorar orientações <ArrowUpRight size={16} />
              </a>
              <span className="wt-health-education">
                <ShieldCheck size={15} /> CONTEÚDO EDUCATIVO
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <WorkstationIllustration />
        </section>

        <div className="wt-health-intro">
          <Info size={20} />
          <p>
            A prevenção envolve{" "}
            <strong>o posto, as tarefas e a organização do trabalho</strong>,
            com participação de quem realiza as atividades. Esta página informa;
            não substitui orientação profissional.
          </p>
        </div>

        <section
          id="wt-health-topics"
          className="wt-health-topics"
          aria-labelledby="wt-health-topics-title"
        >
          <div className="wt-health-section-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="wt-health-kicker">
                PEQUENAS LEITURAS. CONVERSAS IMPORTANTES.
              </span>
              <h2 id="wt-health-topics-title">
                Conhecimento para o dia a dia.
              </h2>
            </div>
            <div className="wt-health-search">
              <label htmlFor="wt-health-search">Buscar orientações</label>
              <div>
                <Search size={17} />
                <input
                  id="wt-health-search"
                  type="search"
                  value={query}
                  onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Ex.: pausas, ergonomia..."
                  autoComplete="off"
                />
                {query && (
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setQuery("")}
                    aria-label="Limpar busca"
                  >
                    <X size={16} />
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <p
            className="wt-health-search-status"
            role="status"
            aria-live="polite"
          >
            {query
              ? `${filteredTopics.length} ${filteredTopics.length === 1 ? "orientação encontrada" : "orientações encontradas"}.`
              : "Abra um tema para conhecer orientações e fontes."}
          </p>
          <div className="wt-health-topic-grid">
            {filteredTopics.map((topic) => {
              const Icon = topic.icon;
              return (
                <details key={topic.id} className="wt-health-topic-card">
                  <summary>
                    <span className="wt-health-topic-top">
                      <span className="wt-health-topic-icon">
                        <Icon size={23} strokeWidth={1.6} />
                      </span>
                      <span className="wt-health-topic-tag">{topic.tag}</span>
                    </span>
                    <h3>{topic.title}</h3>
                    <p>{topic.summary}</p>
                    <span className="wt-health-read">
                      <span>Explorar orientação</span>
                      <ChevronDown size={17} />
                    </span>
                  </summary>
                  <div className="wt-health-topic-body">
                    <p>{topic.body}</p>
                    <div className="wt-health-action">
                      <span>NA PRÁTICA</span>
                      <p>{topic.action}</p>
                    </div>
                    <a
                      href={topic.source.url}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                    >
                      Fonte: {topic.source.title} <ExternalLink size={13} />
                      <span className="wt-health-sr-only">
                        {" "}
                        (abre em nova aba)
                      </span>
                    </a>
                  </div>
                </details>
              );
            })}
          </div>
          {filteredTopics.length === 0 && (
            <div className="wt-health-empty">
              <Search size={29} />
              <h3>Nenhum tema encontrado.</h3>
              <p>Tente buscar por “rotina”, “pausas” ou “apoio”.</p>
              <button
                className="wt-health-text-button"
                onClick={() => setQuery("")}
              >
                Ver todas as orientações <ArrowUpRight size={15} />
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>

        <section
          className="wt-health-checklist-section"
          aria-labelledby="wt-health-checklist-title"
        >
          <div className="wt-health-checklist-copy">
            <span className="wt-health-kicker">UM OLHAR PARA O AMBIENTE</span>
            <h2 id="wt-health-checklist-title">
              O que vale
              <br />
              <span>observar hoje?</span>
            </h2>
            <p>
              Use este roteiro para iniciar uma conversa sobre as condições de
              trabalho. Marque apenas o que você já observou.
            </p>
            <div className="wt-health-local-note">
              <ShieldCheck size={18} />
              <p>
                Suas marcações ficam apenas nesta tela e desaparecem ao
                recarregar. Nenhuma resposta é enviada.
              </p>
            </div>
            <button
              className="wt-health-text-button"
              onClick={() => setObserved(new Set())}
              disabled={observed.size === 0}
            >
              Limpar marcações <X size={14} />
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="wt-health-checklist">
            <div className="wt-health-checklist-label">
              <span>ROTEIRO EDUCATIVO</span>
              <span>JÁ OBSERVEI</span>
            </div>
            {checklist.map((item, index) => (
              <label
                key={item}
                className={`wt-health-check-item ${observed.has(index) ? "is-observed" : ""}`}
              >
                <span>{item}</span>
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={observed.has(index)}
                  onChange={() => toggleObservation(index)}
                />
                <span className="wt-health-check-box" aria-hidden="true">
                  {observed.has(index) && <Check size={15} />}
                </span>
              </label>
            ))}
            <p className="wt-health-checklist-disclaimer">
              Este roteiro não é uma avaliação ergonômica, um diagnóstico ou uma
              comprovação de atendimento à NR-17.
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section
          className="wt-health-faq-section"
          aria-labelledby="wt-health-faq-title"
        >
          <div className="wt-health-section-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="wt-health-kicker">
                INFORMAÇÃO SEM COMPLICAÇÃO
              </span>
              <h2 id="wt-health-faq-title">Dúvidas que merecem atenção.</h2>
            </div>
            <MessageCircle size={27} strokeWidth={1.4} />
          </div>
          <div className="wt-health-faq">
            {questions.map((item) => (
              <details key={item.question}>
                <summary>
                  <h3>{item.question}</h3>
                  <ChevronDown size={18} />
                </summary>
                <div>
                  <p>{item.answer}</p>
                  <a
                    href={item.source.url}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  >
                    Consultar fonte oficial <ExternalLink size={13} />
                    <span className="wt-health-sr-only">
                      {" "}
                      (abre em nova aba)
                    </span>
                  </a>
                </div>
              </details>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>

        <aside
          className="wt-health-support"
          aria-labelledby="wt-health-support-title"
        >
          <div className="wt-health-support-icon">
            <HeartPulse size={29} strokeWidth={1.5} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="wt-health-kicker">
              VOCÊ NÃO PRECISA LIDAR COM ISSO SOZINHO
            </span>
            <h2 id="wt-health-support-title">Persistiu? Procure orientação.</h2>
            <p>
              Se o desconforto persistir ou dificultar suas atividades, procure
              a equipe de saúde ocupacional ou um serviço de saúde. Interrompa
              movimentos que provoquem dor e peça orientação individual.
            </p>
          </div>
        </aside>

        <section
          className="wt-health-sources-section"
          aria-labelledby="wt-health-sources-title"
        >
          <div className="wt-health-section-heading">
            <div>
              <span className="wt-health-kicker">
                PARA CONTINUAR APRENDENDO
              </span>
              <h2 id="wt-health-sources-title">
                Informação de fontes oficiais.
              </h2>
            </div>
            <span className="wt-health-reviewed">
              Referências consultadas em 04/11/2026
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="wt-health-sources">
            {Object.values(sources).map((source) => (
              <a
                key={source.url}
                href={source.url}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
              >
                <span className="wt-health-source-org">{source.title}</span>
                <h3>{source.label}</h3>
                <p>{source.description}</p>
                <span className="wt-health-source-link">
                  Consultar publicação <ArrowUpRight size={16} />
                  <span className="wt-health-sr-only"> (abre em nova aba)</span>
                </span>
              </a>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      </Content>
      {standalone && (
        <footer className="wt-health-footer">
          <span>
            WALK TALK{" "}
            <span>· Comunicação que aproxima. Informação que cuida.</span>
          </span>
          <a href="/portal">
            Voltar à plataforma <ArrowUpRight size={14} />
          </a>
        </footer>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
src/portal/MessageView.tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { FormEvent } from "react";
import {
  Globe,
  Hash,
  LoaderCircle,
  MessageSquare,
  RefreshCw,
  Send,
} from "lucide-react";
import type {
  MessagesResponse,
  Session,
  TeamsResponse,
} from "../../server/types";
import { api, notify, useResource } from "./api";
import { Avatar, EmptyState, ErrorNotice } from "./ui";
import "./portal-views.css";

const roleNames = {
  admin: "Administrador",
  manager: "Gestor",
  collaborator: "Colaborador",
};
const messageDate = (value: string) =>
  new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  }).format(new Date(value));

export function MessageView({ session }: { session: Session }) {
  const teams = useResource<TeamsResponse>("/teams");
  const [teamId, setTeamId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [drafts, setDrafts] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);
  const [sendError, setSendError] = useState("");
  const channelKey = teamId ?? "general";
  const body = drafts[channelKey] ?? "";
  const channelName = teamId
    ? (teams.data?.teams.find((team) => team.id === teamId)?.name ?? "Equipe")
    : "Geral";
  const messages = useResource<MessagesResponse>(
    teamId ? `/messages?teamId=${encodeURIComponent(teamId)}` : "/messages",
  );
  const orderedMessages = useMemo(
    () =>
      [...(messages.data?.messages ?? [])].sort((a, b) =>
        a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt),
      ),
    [messages.data],
  );
  const reloadMessages = messages.reload;
  useEffect(() => {
    const refresh = () => {
      if (!document.hidden) void reloadMessages();
    };
    const timer = window.setInterval(refresh, 30000);
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", refresh);
    return () => {
      window.clearInterval(timer);
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", refresh);
    };
  }, [reloadMessages]);
  const chooseChannel = (id: string | null) => {
    if (sending) return;
    setTeamId(id);
    setSendError("");
  };
  const sendMessage = async (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!body.trim() || sending) return;
    setSending(true);
    setSendError("");
    try {
      await api("/messages", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ body: body.trim(), teamId }),
      });
      setDrafts((current) => ({ ...current, [channelKey]: "" }));
      await messages.reload();
      notify("Mensagem publicada no canal.");
    } catch (cause) {
      setSendError(
        cause instanceof Error
          ? cause.message
          : "Não foi possível enviar a mensagem. Seu texto foi preservado.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSending(false);
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="wt-messages-view">
      <div className="wt-view-intro">
        <div>
          <span className="wt-kicker">CONVERSAS QUE CONECTAM</span>
          <h2>O próximo passo, em conjunto.</h2>
          <p>Compartilhe atualizações e mantenha sua equipe na mesma página.</p>
        </div>
        <span className="wt-internet-label">
          <Globe size={15} />
          Mensagens do portal · via internet
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="wt-messaging-shell">
        <aside className="wt-channels" aria-label="Canais de comunicação">
          <div className="wt-channel-heading">
            <span>CANAIS</span>
            <span>{1 + (teams.data?.teams.length ?? 0)}</span>
          </div>
          <button
            className={teamId === null ? "selected" : ""}
            aria-pressed={teamId === null}
            onClick={() => chooseChannel(null)}
            disabled={sending}
          >
            <Globe size={17} />
            <span>
              Geral<small>Toda a empresa</small>
            </span>
          </button>
          {teams.loading && !teams.data && (
            <p className="wt-channel-state" role="status">
              Carregando equipes…
            </p>
          )}
          {teams.data?.teams.map((team) => (
            <button
              key={team.id}
              className={teamId === team.id ? "selected" : ""}
              aria-pressed={teamId === team.id}
              disabled={sending}
              onClick={() => chooseChannel(team.id)}
            >
              <Hash size={17} />
              <span>
                {team.name}
                <small>{team.location || "Canal da equipe"}</small>
              </span>
            </button>
          ))}
          {teams.error && (
            <div className="wt-channel-state">
              <ErrorNotice message={teams.error} onRetry={teams.reload} />
            </div>
          )}
          <p className="wt-channel-help">
            Um espaço para assuntos da operação. Compartilhe somente informações
            adequadas à equipe.
          </p>
        </aside>
        <section className="wt-conversation" aria-labelledby="wt-channel-title">
          <header className="wt-conversation-header">
            <span className="wt-channel-icon">
              {teamId ? <Hash size={22} /> : <Globe size={22} />}
            </span>
            <div>
              <h3 id="wt-channel-title">{channelName}</h3>
              <p>
                {teamId
                  ? "Atualizações desta equipe"
                  : "Comunicados para toda a empresa"}
              </p>
            </div>
            <button
              className="wt-message-refresh"
              aria-label="Atualizar mensagens"
              title="Atualizar mensagens"
              disabled={messages.loading}
              onClick={() => void messages.reload()}
            >
              <RefreshCw
                size={17}
                className={messages.loading ? "wt-spin" : ""}
              />
            </button>
          </header>
          <div
            className="wt-message-history"
            role="log"
            aria-label={`Mensagens do canal ${channelName}`}
            aria-live="polite"
            aria-relevant="additions"
          >
            {messages.error ? (
              <ErrorNotice message={messages.error} onRetry={messages.reload} />
            ) : messages.loading && !messages.data ? (
              <p className="wt-loading" role="status">
                Carregando mensagens…
              </p>
            ) : orderedMessages.length ? (
              orderedMessages.map((message) => (
                <article
                  key={message.id}
                  className={`wt-message ${message.authorId === session.user.id ? "wt-message-own" : ""}`}
                >
                  <Avatar name={message.authorName} />
                  <div className="wt-message-content">
                    <div className="wt-message-author">
                      <strong>{message.authorName}</strong>
                      <span>{roleNames[message.authorRole]}</span>
                      <time dateTime={message.createdAt}>
                        {messageDate(message.createdAt)}
                      </time>
                    </div>
                    <p>{message.body}</p>
                  </div>
                </article>
              ))
            ) : (
              <EmptyState
                icon={MessageSquare}
                title="Toda conversa começa com uma mensagem"
                description={`Compartilhe a primeira atualização no canal ${channelName}.`}
              />
            )}
          </div>
          <form className="wt-message-composer" onSubmit={sendMessage}>
            <label className="wt-label" htmlFor="wt-message-body">
              Mensagem para {channelName}
            </label>
            <textarea
              id="wt-message-body"
              className="wt-input"
              placeholder="Escreva uma atualização para a equipe…"
              rows={3}
              value={body}
              maxLength={2000}
              disabled={sending}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setDrafts((current) => ({
                  ...current,
                  [channelKey]: event.target.value,
                }))
              }
            />
            {sendError && (
              <p className="wt-error" role="alert">
                {sendError}
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="wt-composer-bottom">
              <span>
                {body.length}/2.000 caracteres <span>· envio pelo portal</span>
              </span>
              <button
                className="wt-button"
                type="submit"
                disabled={sending || !body.trim()}
              >
                {sending ? (
                  <LoaderCircle size={16} className="wt-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <Send size={16} />
                )}
                {sending ? "Enviando…" : "Enviar mensagem"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </section>
      </div>
      <p className="wt-message-footnote">
        As mensagens são atualizadas periodicamente e precisam de acesso à
        internet. Este canal não realiza transmissões via satélite e não é um
        serviço de emergência.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
src/portal/PortalApp.tsx
"use client";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  Activity,
  ArrowDownToLine,
  ArrowRight,
  ArrowUpRight,
  Check,
  ChevronRight,
  CircleCheck,
  CircleHelp,
  Globe2,
  HeartPulse,
  LayoutDashboard,
  LoaderCircle,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  MessageSquare,
  Plus,
  Radio,
  RefreshCw,
  Settings,
  ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles,
  Users,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import type {
  Session,
  Overview,
  DevicesResponse,
  Device,
  InvitationCreated,
  InvitationsResponse,
  TeamsResponse,
  Role,
} from "../../server/types";
import {
  api,
  ApiError,
  formatDate,
  notify,
  roleLabel,
  useNotice,
  useResource,
} from "./api";
import { Avatar, EmptyState, ErrorNotice, Loading, Modal, Panel } from "./ui";
import { DeviceView } from "./DeviceView";
import { TeamView } from "./TeamView";
import { MessageView } from "./MessageView";
import { HealthPage } from "./HealthPage";
import "./portal.css";

type View =
  "overview" | "devices" | "teams" | "messages" | "health" | "settings";
const links = [
  { id: "overview" as const, label: "Visão geral", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { id: "devices" as const, label: "Equipamentos", icon: Radio },
  { id: "teams" as const, label: "Equipes e pessoas", icon: Users },
  { id: "messages" as const, label: "Comunicação", icon: MessageSquare },
  { id: "health" as const, label: "Saúde ocupacional", icon: HeartPulse },
  { id: "settings" as const, label: "Acessos e convites", icon: Settings },
];
export function PortalLogo() {
  return (
    <a href="/" className="wt-logo">
      <Globe2 size={29} strokeWidth={1.3} />
      <span>
        WALK<span>TALK</span>
        <small>EMPRESAS</small>
      </span>
    </a>
  );
}
function Toast() {
  const { notice, dismiss } = useNotice();
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!notice) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(dismiss, 6000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [notice, dismiss]);
  return notice ? (
    <div
      className={`wt-toast ${notice.kind}`}
      role={notice.kind === "error" ? "alert" : "status"}
    >
      <Check size={18} />
      <span>{notice.message}</span>
      <button aria-label="Fechar aviso" onClick={dismiss}>
        <X size={16} />
      </button>
    </div>
  ) : null;
}
export function PortalApp() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [unauthorized, setUnauthorized] = useState(false);
  const [view, setView] = useState<View>("overview");
  const [menu, setMenu] = useState(false);
  const [invite, setInvite] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const menuButton = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const loadSession = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      setSession(await api<Session>("/session"));
      setUnauthorized(false);
    } catch (cause) {
      if (cause instanceof ApiError && cause.status === 401)
        setUnauthorized(true);
      else
        setError(
          cause instanceof Error
            ? cause.message
            : "Falha ao carregar o portal.",
        );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    const token = new URL(location.href).searchParams.get("convite");
    if (token) setInvite(token);
    void loadSession();
    const parse = () => {
      const id = location.hash.slice(1);
      if (links.some((l) => l.id === id)) setView(id as View);
    };
    parse();
    window.addEventListener("hashchange", parse);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("hashchange", parse);
  }, [loadSession]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!menu) return;
    const close = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        setMenu(false);
        menuButton.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", close);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", close);
  }, [menu]);
  const select = (next: View) => {
    setView(next);
    setMenu(false);
    history.replaceState(
      null,
      "",
      `${location.pathname}${location.search}#${next}`,
    );
    document.getElementById("portal-content")?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "instant" });
  };
  if (loading && !session)
    return (
      <div className="wt-app wt-gate">
        <PortalLogo />
        <Loading label="Preparando seu espaço de trabalho…" />
      </div>
    );
  if (error && !session)
    return (
      <div className="wt-app wt-gate">
        <PortalLogo />
        <ErrorNotice message={error} onRetry={() => void loadSession()} />
        <a href="/">Voltar para WALK TALK</a>
      </div>
    );
  if (unauthorized || !session)
    return (
      <div className="wt-app">
        <EntryScreen invite={invite} />
      </div>
    );
  if (invite && !session.member)
    return (
      <div className="wt-app">
        <JoinInvitation
          token={invite}
          session={session}
          onJoined={() => {
            setInvite(null);
            history.replaceState(null, "", "/portal");
            void loadSession();
          }}
        />
        <Toast />
      </div>
    );
  if (!session.member || !session.workspace)
    return (
      <div className="wt-app">
        <WorkspaceSetup
          session={session}
          onCreated={() => void loadSession()}
        />
        <Toast />
      </div>
    );
  const effectiveView =
    view === "settings" && session.member.role !== "admin" ? "overview" : view;
  return (
    <div className="wt-app">
      <a className="wt-skip" href="#portal-content">
        Pular para o conteúdo
      </a>
      {menu && (
        <button
          className="wt-sidebar-backdrop"
          aria-label="Fechar navegação"
          onClick={() => setMenu(false)}
        />
      )}
      <aside className={`wt-sidebar ${menu ? "is-open" : ""}`}>
        <PortalLogo />
        <div className="wt-workspace">
          <span className="wt-workspace-icon">
            {session.workspace.name.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}
          </span>
          <span>
            <strong>{session.workspace.name}</strong>
            <small>Seu espaço de trabalho</small>
          </span>
          <ShieldCheck size={16} />
        </div>
        <span className="wt-nav-label">ESPAÇO DA EMPRESA</span>
        <nav aria-label="Navegação do portal">
          {links
            .filter(
              (l) => l.id !== "settings" || session.member!.role === "admin",
            )
            .map(({ id, label, icon: Icon }) => (
              <button
                key={id}
                onClick={() => select(id)}
                className={effectiveView === id ? "active" : ""}
                aria-current={effectiveView === id ? "page" : undefined}
              >
                <Icon size={19} />
                <span>{label}</span>
                {id === "health" && <span className="wt-nav-dot" />}
              </button>
            ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="wt-sidebar-resource">
          <span className="wt-mini-orbit">
            <Globe2 size={30} strokeWidth={1} />
          </span>
          <strong>Conexão sem fronteiras.</strong>
          <p>Conheça a experiência WALK TALK.</p>
          <a href="/produto">
            Explorar o produto <ArrowUpRight size={15} />
          </a>
        </div>
        <div className="wt-sidebar-user">
          <Avatar name={session.user.name} />
          <span>
            <strong>{session.user.name}</strong>
            <small>{roleLabel[session.member.role]}</small>
          </span>
          <a
            href="/signout-with-chatgpt?return_to=/portal"
            target="_top"
            aria-label="Sair do portal"
          >
            <LogOut size={17} />
          </a>
        </div>
      </aside>
      <div className="wt-main">
        <header className="wt-topbar">
          <div>
            <button
              ref={menuButton}
              className="wt-icon-button wt-menu-button"
              aria-label="Abrir navegação do portal"
              aria-expanded={menu}
              onClick={() => setMenu(!menu)}
            >
              <Menu size={21} />
            </button>
            <span className="wt-breadcrumb">
              Empresa <ChevronRight size={13} />
              <strong>
                {links.find((l) => l.id === effectiveView)?.label}
              </strong>
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="wt-topbar-right">
            <span className="wt-access">
              <span /> Acesso {roleLabel[session.member.role].toLowerCase()}
            </span>
            <a
              href="/ajuda"
              className="wt-help-link"
              aria-label="Abrir central de ajuda"
            >
              <CircleHelp size={18} />
            </a>
            <a
              href="/saude"
              className="wt-help-link"
              aria-label="Abrir página de saúde ocupacional"
            >
              <HeartPulse size={18} />
            </a>
            <Avatar name={session.user.name} />
          </div>
        </header>
        <main
          id="portal-content"
          tabIndex={-1}
          className={`wt-content ${effectiveView === "health" ? "wt-content-health" : ""}`}
        >
          {session.workspace.isDemo && (
            <div className="wt-demo-banner">
              <Sparkles size={15} />
              <span>
                Ambiente com exemplos fictícios. Os registros de exemplo estão
                identificados.
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          {effectiveView === "overview" && (
            <OverviewView session={session} navigate={select} />
          )}
          {effectiveView === "devices" && <DeviceView session={session} />}
          {effectiveView === "teams" && <TeamView session={session} />}
          {effectiveView === "messages" && <MessageView session={session} />}
          {effectiveView === "health" && <HealthPage />}
          {effectiveView === "settings" && <SettingsView session={session} />}
        </main>
        <footer className="wt-app-footer">
          <span>WALK TALK EMPRESAS · COMUNICAÇÃO QUE APROXIMA</span>
          <a href="/privacidade">
            Dados e privacidade <ArrowUpRight size={12} />
          </a>
        </footer>
      </div>
      <Toast />
    </div>
  );
}
