function WorkspaceSetup({
  session,
  onCreated,
}: {
  session: Session;
  onCreated: () => void;
}) {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [examples, setExamples] = useState(false);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  return (
    <div className="wt-setup">
      <PortalLogo />
      <div className="wt-setup-card">
        <span className="wt-entry-icon">
          <Users size={27} />
        </span>
        <span className="wt-eyebrow">COMECE PELA SUA EQUIPE</span>
        <h1>
          Um espaço para
          <br />
          ir mais longe.
        </h1>
        <p>
          Olá, {session.user.name.split(" ")[0]}. Crie sua empresa para
          gerenciar equipamentos e acessos. Você será o administrador.
        </p>
        <form
          onSubmit={async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            setPending(true);
            setError("");
            try {
              await api("/workspace", {
                method: "POST",
                body: JSON.stringify({ name, withExamples: examples }),
              });
              onCreated();
            } catch (c) {
              setError(
                c instanceof Error
                  ? c.message
                  : "Não foi possível criar a empresa.",
              );
            } finally {
              setPending(false);
            }
          }}
        >
          <label className="wt-label" htmlFor="workspace-name">
            Nome da empresa
          </label>
          <input
            id="workspace-name"
            className="wt-input"
            placeholder="Ex.: Horizonte Operações"
            value={name}
            onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
            minLength={2}
            maxLength={100}
            required
          />
          <label className="wt-checkbox">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={examples}
              onChange={(e) => setExamples(e.target.checked)}
            />
            <span>
              Incluir equipes e aparelhos fictícios para explorar
              <small>
                Os exemplos ficam identificados; você poderá arquivar os
                aparelhos.
              </small>
            </span>
          </label>
          {error && <ErrorNotice message={error} />}
          <button className="wt-button" disabled={pending}>
            {pending ? <LoaderCircle size={18} /> : <Plus size={18} />}{" "}
            {pending ? "Criando seu espaço…" : "Criar empresa"}
          </button>
        </form>
        <p className="wt-setup-hint">
          Recebeu um convite? Abra o link enviado pelo administrador para entrar
          na empresa existente.
        </p>
      </div>
      <a href="/">← Voltar à apresentação</a>
    </div>
  );
}
function JoinInvitation({
  token,
  session,
  onJoined,
}: {
  token: string;
  session: Session;
  onJoined: () => void;
}) {
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  return (
    <div className="wt-setup">
      <PortalLogo />
      <div className="wt-setup-card">
        <span className="wt-entry-icon">
          <Users size={28} />
        </span>
        <h1>Vamos trabalhar juntos.</h1>
        <p>
          Você está conectado como <strong>{session.user.email}</strong>. Aceite
          o convite para entrar na empresa com o perfil definido pelo
          administrador.
        </p>
        {error && <ErrorNotice message={error} />}
        <button
          className="wt-button"
          disabled={pending}
          onClick={async () => {
            setPending(true);
            setError("");
            try {
              await api("/invitations/accept", {
                method: "POST",
                body: JSON.stringify({ token }),
              });
              onJoined();
            } catch (c) {
              setError(
                c instanceof Error ? c.message : "Convite indisponível.",
              );
            } finally {
              setPending(false);
            }
          }}
        >
          {pending ? "Validando convite…" : "Aceitar convite"}
          <ArrowRight size={17} />
        </button>
        <a
          href="/signout-with-chatgpt?return_to=/portal"
          target="_top"
          className="wt-entry-back"
        >
          Entrar com outra conta
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
function OverviewView({
  session,
  navigate,
}: {
  session: Session;
  navigate: (view: View) => void;
}) {
  const { data, loading, error, reload } = useResource<Overview>("/overview");
  const inventory = useResource<DevicesResponse>("/devices");
  if (loading && !data) return <Loading />;
  if (error)
    return <ErrorNotice message={error} onRetry={() => void reload()} />;
  if (!data) return null;
  const stats = [
    {
      label: "Equipamentos",
      value: data.devices.total,
      detail: "no seu alcance de acesso",
      icon: Radio,
      color: "blue",
    },
    {
      label: "Disponíveis",
      value: data.devices.available,
      detail: "prontos para atribuição",
      icon: CircleCheck,
      color: "teal",
    },
    {
      label: "Em operação",
      value: data.devices.inUse,
      detail: "com uso registrado",
      icon: Activity,
      color: "orange",
    },
    {
      label: "Pessoas conectadas",
      value: data.members,
      detail: `em ${data.teams} ${data.teams === 1 ? "equipe" : "equipes"}`,
      icon: Users,
      color: "purple",
    },
  ];
  return (
    <>
      <div className="wt-page-heading">
        <div>
          <span className="wt-eyebrow">SUA OPERAÇÃO, MAIS PERTO</span>
          <h1>
            Olá, {session.user.name.split(" ")[0]}.{" "}
            <span className="wt-greeting-dot" />
          </h1>
          <p>Uma visão clara para começar o próximo movimento.</p>
        </div>
        <button
          className="wt-button-secondary"
          onClick={() => {
            void reload();
            void inventory.reload();
          }}
        >
          <RefreshCw size={15} /> Atualizar
        </button>
      </div>
      <section className="wt-welcome-banner">
        <div>
          <span className="wt-pill">
            <span /> CONECTE. ORGANIZE. CUIDE.
          </span>
          <h2>
            O próximo passo
            <br />
            começa com a sua equipe.
          </h2>
          <p>
            Equipamentos organizados. Conversas próximas.
            <br />
            Mais espaço para fazer um bom trabalho.
          </p>
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate("devices")}
            className="wt-banner-action"
          >
            Explorar equipamentos <ArrowUpRight size={17} />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="wt-banner-product">
          <span className="wt-banner-ring ring-one" />
          <span className="wt-banner-ring ring-two" />
          <Globe2 className="wt-banner-globe" strokeWidth={0.5} />
          <img
            src="/product/walk-talk-mobile.webp"
            alt="Conceito visual do comunicador WALK TALK"
            width="480"
            height="720"
          />
          <span className="wt-banner-product-label">
            <span /> WALK TALK · DESIGN CONCEITUAL
          </span>
        </div>
      </section>
      <div className="wt-stat-grid">
        {stats.map(({ label, value, detail, icon: Icon, color }) => (
          <article className="wt-stat" key={label}>
            <div>
              <span>{label}</span>
              <span className={`wt-stat-icon ${color}`}>
                <Icon size={19} />
              </span>
            </div>
            <strong>{value.toString().padStart(2, "0")}</strong>
            <small>{detail}</small>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="wt-dashboard-columns">
        <Panel
          title="Seus equipamentos"
          action={
            <button
              className="wt-text-button"
              onClick={() => navigate("devices")}
            >
              Ver todos <ArrowUpRight size={15} />
            </button>
          }
        >
          {inventory.data?.devices.length ? (
            <div className="wt-device-preview">
              {inventory.data.devices.slice(0, 4).map((device) => (
                <button
                  onClick={() => navigate("devices")}
                  className="wt-preview-row"
                  key={device.id}
                >
                  <span className="wt-device-symbol">
                    <Radio size={22} />
                  </span>
                  <span>
                    <strong>{device.name}</strong>
                    <small>
                      {device.teamName || "Sem equipe"} · {device.serial}
                    </small>
                  </span>
                  <DeviceBadge status={device.status} />
                  <ChevronRight size={15} />
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <EmptyState
              icon={Radio}
              title="Tudo começa com o primeiro aparelho"
              description="Cadastre seus equipamentos para acompanhar a disponibilidade e as verificações."
              action={
                session.member?.role !== "collaborator" ? (
                  <button
                    className="wt-button-secondary"
                    onClick={() => navigate("devices")}
                  >
                    Cadastrar equipamento
                  </button>
                ) : undefined
              }
            />
          )}
          <div className="wt-panel-footnote">
            <ShieldCheck size={14} /> Status atualizados pela equipe. Não
            representam telemetria do aparelho.
          </div>
        </Panel>
        <section className="wt-health-promo">
          <div className="wt-health-promo-top">
            <span className="wt-health-icon">
              <HeartPulse size={24} />
            </span>
            <span>SAÚDE OCUPACIONAL</span>
            <ArrowUpRight size={20} />
          </div>
          <h2>
            Uma boa rotina
            <br />
            também é cuidado.
          </h2>
          <p>
            Entenda LER/DORT e conheça práticas de prevenção, ergonomia e
            organização do trabalho.
          </p>
          <div className="wt-topic-pills">
            <span>Ergonomia</span>
            <span>Prevenção</span>
            <span>Pausas</span>
          </div>
          <button
            className="wt-button-secondary"
            onClick={() => navigate("health")}
          >
            Explorar orientações <ArrowRight size={17} />
          </button>
        </section>
      </div>
      <div className="wt-dashboard-columns wt-lower-columns">
        <Panel
          title="Atividade recente"
          action={<span className="wt-small-note">Registros da empresa</span>}
        >
          {data.activity.length ? (
            <ol className="wt-activity-list">
              {data.activity.slice(0, 5).map((event) => (
                <li key={event.id}>
                  <span className="wt-activity-dot" />
                  <div>
                    <strong>{event.description}</strong>
                    <small>
                      {event.actorName} · {formatDate(event.createdAt, true)}
                    </small>
                  </div>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
          ) : (
            <EmptyState
              icon={Activity}
              title="Sua operação começa aqui"
              description="Cadastros e verificações aparecerão nesta linha do tempo."
            />
          )}
        </Panel>
        <Panel
          title="Conversa da equipe"
          action={
            <button
              className="wt-text-button"
              onClick={() => navigate("messages")}
            >
              Abrir canal <ArrowUpRight size={15} />
            </button>
          }
        >
          {data.recentMessages.length ? (
            <div className="wt-recent-messages">
              {data.recentMessages.slice(0, 3).map((message) => (
                <div key={message.id}>
                  <Avatar name={message.authorName} />
                  <div>
                    <strong>
                      {message.authorName}
                      <small>{formatDate(message.createdAt, true)}</small>
                    </strong>
                    <p>{message.body}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <EmptyState
              icon={MessageSquare}
              title="Aproxime sua equipe"
              description="Envie a primeira mensagem no canal geral. A comunicação do portal usa internet."
              action={
                <button
                  className="wt-text-button"
                  onClick={() => navigate("messages")}
                >
                  Começar uma conversa <ArrowRight size={15} />
                </button>
              }
            />
          )}
        </Panel>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
export function DeviceBadge({ status }: { status: Device["status"] }) {
  return (
    <span className={`wt-status status-${status}`}>
      <i />
      {
        {
          available: "Disponível",
          in_use: "Em operação",
          maintenance: "Manutenção",
        }[status]
      }
    </span>
  );
}
function SettingsView({ session }: { session: Session }) {
  const invites = useResource<InvitationsResponse>("/invitations");
  const teams = useResource<TeamsResponse>("/teams");
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [created, setCreated] = useState<InvitationCreated | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  return (
    <>
      <div className="wt-page-heading">
        <div>
          <span className="wt-eyebrow">GESTÃO DE ACESSOS</span>
          <h1>
            As pessoas certas.
            <br />O acesso certo.
          </h1>
          <p>
            Crie convites para {session.workspace?.name} com o perfil adequado.
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          className="wt-button"
          onClick={() => {
            setCreated(null);
            setError("");
            setOpen(true);
          }}
        >
          <Plus size={17} /> Criar convite
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="wt-role-grid">
        {(
          [
            {
              role: "admin",
              title: "Administrador",
              text: "Configura a empresa, controla convites e gerencia perfis, equipes e aparelhos.",
            },
            {
              role: "manager",
              title: "Gestor",
              text: "Organiza equipes, cadastra aparelhos e acompanha as conversas da empresa.",
            },
            {
              role: "collaborator",
              title: "Colaborador",
              text: "Acessa os canais da sua equipe e registra a verificação dos aparelhos atribuídos.",
            },
          ] as { role: Role; title: string; text: string }[]
        ).map((item) => (
          <Panel key={item.role}>
            <span className={`wt-role-mark ${item.role}`}>
              <ShieldCheck size={22} />
            </span>
            <h2>{item.title}</h2>
            <p>{item.text}</p>
          </Panel>
        ))}
      </div>
      <Panel
        title="Convites da empresa"
        action={
          <button
            className="wt-text-button"
            onClick={() => void invites.reload()}
          >
            <RefreshCw size={14} /> Atualizar
          </button>
        }
      >
        {invites.error ? (
          <ErrorNotice
            message={invites.error}
            onRetry={() => void invites.reload()}
          />
        ) : invites.loading && !invites.data ? (
          <Loading />
        ) : invites.data?.invitations.length ? (
          <div className="wt-table-wrap">
            <table className="wt-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Destino</th>
                  <th>Perfil</th>
                  <th>Equipe</th>
                  <th>Validade</th>
                  <th>Status</th>
                  <th>
                    <span className="wt-sr-only">Ações</span>
                  </th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {invites.data.invitations.map((inv) => (
                  <tr key={inv.id}>
                    <td>
                      <strong>{inv.email || "Link de convite"}</strong>
                    </td>
                    <td>{roleLabel[inv.role]}</td>
                    <td>{inv.teamName || "Sem equipe"}</td>
                    <td>{formatDate(inv.expiresAt)}</td>
                    <td>
                      <span className="wt-badge">
                        {
                          {
                            pending: "Pendente",
                            accepted: "Aceito",
                            revoked: "Revogado",
                            expired: "Expirado",
                          }[inv.status]
                        }
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                      {inv.status === "pending" && (
                        <button
                          className="wt-text-button wt-danger-text"
                          onClick={async () => {
                            try {
                              await api(`/invitations/${inv.id}`, {
                                method: "DELETE",
                              });
                              notify("Convite revogado.");
                              void invites.reload();
                            } catch (c) {
                              notify(
                                c instanceof Error
                                  ? c.message
                                  : "Não foi possível revogar.",
                                "error",
                              );
                            }
                          }}
                        >
                          Revogar
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <EmptyState
            icon={Users}
            title="Sua equipe começa com um convite"
            description="Gere um link e compartilhe com a pessoa que fará parte da empresa."
          />
        )}
      </Panel>
      <div className="wt-access-note">
        <ShieldCheck size={20} />
        <p>
          O convite libera o perfil no portal. A pessoa também precisa ter
          acesso à hospedagem do site. Nenhum e-mail é enviado automaticamente.
        </p>
      </div>
      {open && (
        <Modal
          title={
            created
              ? "Convite pronto para compartilhar"
              : "Convidar para a empresa"
          }
          onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
        >
          {created ? (
            <div className="wt-invite-created">
              <span className="wt-success-circle">
                <Check size={26} />
              </span>
              <p>
                Este link é de uso único e vale até{" "}
                <strong>{formatDate(created.expiresAt, true)}</strong>.
                Compartilhe somente com a pessoa convidada.
              </p>
              <label className="wt-label" htmlFor="invite-url">
                Link do convite
              </label>
              <input
                id="invite-url"
                className="wt-input"
                readOnly
                value={created.url}
                onFocus={(e) => e.currentTarget.select()}
              />
              <button
                className="wt-button"
                onClick={async () => {
                  try {
                    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(created.url);
                    notify("Link copiado.");
                  } catch {
                    notify("Selecione e copie o link no campo acima.", "error");
                  }
                }}
              >
                Copiar link <ArrowUpRight size={16} />
              </button>
              <small>
                O link só é exibido nesta criação. Se perder o link, revogue o
                convite e crie outro.
              </small>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form
              onSubmit={async (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                setBusy(true);
                setError("");
                const values = new FormData(e.currentTarget);
                try {
                  const result = await api<InvitationCreated>("/invitations", {
                    method: "POST",
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                      role: values.get("role"),
                      email: values.get("email") || null,
                      teamId: values.get("teamId") || null,
                    }),
                  });
                  setCreated(result);
                  void invites.reload();
                } catch (c) {
                  setError(
                    c instanceof Error
                      ? c.message
                      : "Não foi possível criar o convite.",
                  );
                } finally {
                  setBusy(false);
                }
              }}
            >
              <div className="wt-field">
                <label className="wt-label" htmlFor="invite-email">
                  E-mail da pessoa <span>(opcional)</span>
                </label>
                <input
                  id="invite-email"
                  name="email"
                  className="wt-input"
                  type="email"
                  maxLength={254}
                  placeholder="pessoa@empresa.com"
                />
                <small>Se informado, apenas essa conta poderá aceitar.</small>
              </div>
              <div className="wt-form-grid">
                <div className="wt-field">
                  <label className="wt-label" htmlFor="invite-role">
                    Perfil
                  </label>
                  <select
                    id="invite-role"
                    name="role"
                    className="wt-input"
                    defaultValue="collaborator"
                  >
                    <option value="collaborator">Colaborador</option>
                    <option value="manager">Gestor</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div className="wt-field">
                  <label className="wt-label" htmlFor="invite-team">
                    Equipe
                  </label>
                  <select id="invite-team" name="teamId" className="wt-input">
                    <option value="">Sem equipe</option>
                    {teams.data?.teams.map((team) => (
                      <option value={team.id} key={team.id}>
                        {team.name}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>
              {error && <ErrorNotice message={error} />}
              <div className="wt-form-actions">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="wt-button-secondary"
                  onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button className="wt-button" disabled={busy}>
                  {busy ? "Criando…" : "Gerar convite"}
                  <ArrowRight size={17} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          )}
        </Modal>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
src/portal/TeamView.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import type { FormEvent } from "react";
import {
  Check,
  LoaderCircle,
  MapPin,
  Pencil,
  Plus,
  Radio,
  Search,
  ShieldCheck,
  Users,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import type {
  Member,
  MembersResponse,
  Session,
  Team,
  TeamsResponse,
} from "../../server/types";
import { api, notify, useResource } from "./api";
import { Avatar, EmptyState, ErrorNotice, Modal, Panel } from "./ui";
import "./portal-views.css";

const roleNames = {
  admin: "Administrador",
  manager: "Gestor",
  collaborator: "Colaborador",
};
const teamColors = [
  "#007e91",
  "#5469b1",
  "#bf6b30",
  "#41715d",
  "#9a5278",
  "#526578",
];

function MemberRow({
  member,
  teams,
  canEdit,
  onSaved,
}: {
  member: Member;
  teams: Team[];
  canEdit: boolean;
  onSaved: () => Promise<void>;
}) {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const [role, setRole] = useState(member.role);
  const [teamId, setTeamId] = useState(member.teamId ?? "");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const save = async (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await api(`/members/${member.id}`, {
        method: "PATCH",
        body: JSON.stringify({ role, teamId: teamId || null }),
      });
      await onSaved();
      setEditing(false);
      notify("Permissões e equipe atualizadas.");
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(
        cause instanceof Error
          ? cause.message
          : "Não foi possível atualizar este membro.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };
  return (
    <li className="wt-member-row">
      <div className="wt-member-identity">
        <Avatar name={member.name} />
        <div>
          <strong>{member.name}</strong>
          <span>{member.email}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      {!editing ? (
        <>
          <span className="wt-member-team">
            {member.teamName || "Sem equipe"}
          </span>
          <span className={`wt-badge wt-role-${member.role}`}>
            {member.role === "admin" && <ShieldCheck size={12} />}
            {roleNames[member.role]}
          </span>
          {canEdit ? (
            <button
              className="wt-member-edit"
              aria-label={`Editar permissões de ${member.name}`}
              onClick={() => {
                setRole(member.role);
                setTeamId(member.teamId ?? "");
                setError("");
                setEditing(true);
              }}
            >
              <Pencil size={15} />
            </button>
          ) : (
            <span className="wt-member-edit-space" />
          )}
        </>
      ) : (
        <form className="wt-member-editor" onSubmit={save}>
          <label className="wt-field">
            <span className="wt-label">Perfil de {member.name}</span>
            <select
              className="wt-input"
              value={role}
              disabled={saving}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setRole(event.target.value as "manager" | "collaborator")
              }
            >
              <option value="manager">Gestor</option>
              <option value="collaborator">Colaborador</option>
            </select>
          </label>
          <label className="wt-field">
            <span className="wt-label">Equipe de {member.name}</span>
            <select
              className="wt-input"
              value={teamId}
              disabled={saving}
              onChange={(event) => setTeamId(event.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="">Sem equipe</option>
              {teams.map((team) => (
                <option value={team.id} key={team.id}>
                  {team.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
          <div className="wt-member-edit-actions">
            <button className="wt-button" disabled={saving} type="submit">
              {saving ? (
                <LoaderCircle size={14} className="wt-spin" />
              ) : (
                <Check size={14} />
              )}
              Salvar
            </button>
            <button
              className="wt-button-secondary"
              disabled={saving}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setEditing(false)}
            >
              <X size={14} />
              Cancelar
            </button>
          </div>
          {error && (
            <p className="wt-error" role="alert">
              {error}
            </p>
          )}
        </form>
      )}
    </li>
  );
}

export function TeamView({ session }: { session: Session }) {
  const teams = useResource<TeamsResponse>("/teams");
  const members = useResource<MembersResponse>("/members");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [location, setLocation] = useState("");
  const [color, setColor] = useState(teamColors[0]);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");
  const canManage =
    session.member?.role === "admin" || session.member?.role === "manager";
  const canEditMembers = session.member?.role === "admin";
  const normalized = query.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("pt-BR");
  const filteredTeams = (teams.data?.teams ?? []).filter((team) =>
    `${team.name} ${team.location}`
      .toLocaleLowerCase("pt-BR")
      .includes(normalized),
  );
  const filteredMembers = (members.data?.members ?? []).filter((member) =>
    `${member.name} ${member.email} ${member.teamName ?? ""}`
      .toLocaleLowerCase("pt-BR")
      .includes(normalized),
  );
  const reloadAll = async () => {
    await Promise.all([teams.reload(), members.reload()]);
  };
  const createTeam = async (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setFormError("");
    try {
      await api("/teams", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: name.trim(),
          location: location.trim(),
          color,
        }),
      });
      await teams.reload();
      setCreating(false);
      setName("");
      setLocation("");
      setColor(teamColors[0]);
      notify("Equipe criada. Ela já pode receber aparelhos e membros.");
    } catch (cause) {
      setFormError(
        cause instanceof Error
          ? cause.message
          : "Não foi possível criar a equipe.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="wt-team-view">
      <div className="wt-view-intro">
        <div>
          <span className="wt-kicker">PESSOAS QUE CONECTAM</span>
          <h2>Uma equipe. Muitas possibilidades.</h2>
          <p>
            Organize as frentes de trabalho e saiba quem está em cada operação.
          </p>
        </div>
        {canManage && (
          <button
            className="wt-button"
            onClick={() => {
              setFormError("");
              setCreating(true);
            }}
          >
            <Plus size={17} />
            Nova equipe
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="wt-directory-toolbar">
        <label className="wt-directory-search">
          <Search size={17} />
          <span className="wt-sr-only">Buscar equipes ou pessoas</span>
          <input
            className="wt-input"
            type="search"
            placeholder="Buscar equipes ou pessoas…"
            value={query}
            onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
          />
        </label>
        <span>
          {teams.data?.teams.length ?? 0} equipes ·{" "}
          {members.data?.members.length ?? 0} pessoas
        </span>
      </div>
      {teams.error && (
        <ErrorNotice message={teams.error} onRetry={teams.reload} />
      )}
      {teams.loading && !teams.data ? (
        <p className="wt-loading" role="status">
          Carregando equipes…
        </p>
      ) : (
        !teams.error && (
          <>
            {filteredTeams.length ? (
              <div className="wt-team-grid">
                {filteredTeams.map((team) => (
                  <article className="wt-team-card" key={team.id}>
                    <div className="wt-team-card-top">
                      <span
                        className="wt-team-emblem"
                        style={{
                          color: team.color,
                          backgroundColor: `${team.color}15`,
                        }}
                      >
                        <Users size={23} />
                      </span>
                      {team.isExample && (
                        <span className="wt-example-tag">Exemplo</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <h3>{team.name}</h3>
                    <p className="wt-team-location">
                      <MapPin size={14} />
                      {team.location || "Local não informado"}
                    </p>
                    <div className="wt-team-card-stats">
                      <span>
                        <Users size={15} />
                        <strong>{team.members}</strong> pessoas
                      </span>
                      <span>
                        <Radio size={15} />
                        <strong>{team.devices}</strong> aparelhos
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </article>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <EmptyState
                icon={Users}
                title={
                  query
                    ? "Nenhuma equipe encontrada"
                    : "Sua próxima operação começa aqui"
                }
                description={
                  query
                    ? "Experimente outro nome ou local."
                    : canManage
                      ? "Crie a primeira equipe para organizar as pessoas e os aparelhos da empresa."
                      : "Quando você for associado a uma equipe, os detalhes aparecerão aqui."
                }
                action={
                  !query && canManage ? (
                    <button
                      className="wt-button-secondary"
                      onClick={() => setCreating(true)}
                    >
                      <Plus size={16} />
                      Criar equipe
                    </button>
                  ) : undefined
                }
              />
            )}
          </>
        )
      )}
      <Panel
        title="Pessoas e permissões"
        action={
          <span className="wt-muted">
            {filteredMembers.length}{" "}
            {filteredMembers.length === 1 ? "pessoa" : "pessoas"}
          </span>
        }
      >
        <p className="wt-member-intro">
          {canEditMembers
            ? "Defina as equipes e os perfis de acesso. Novas pessoas entram pelos convites em Configurações."
            : "Cada perfil tem acesso às funções necessárias para sua rotina."}
        </p>
        {members.error ? (
          <ErrorNotice message={members.error} onRetry={members.reload} />
        ) : members.loading && !members.data ? (
          <p role="status" className="wt-loading">
            Carregando pessoas…
          </p>
        ) : filteredMembers.length ? (
          <ul className="wt-member-list">
            {filteredMembers.map((member) => (
              <MemberRow
                key={member.id}
                member={member}
                teams={teams.data?.teams ?? []}
                canEdit={
                  canEditMembers &&
                  member.role !== "admin" &&
                  member.id !== session.user.id
                }
                onSaved={reloadAll}
              />
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : (
          <p className="wt-muted wt-directory-empty">
            Nenhuma pessoa corresponde à busca.
          </p>
        )}
      </Panel>
      <div className="wt-permission-note">
        <ShieldCheck size={18} />
        <p>
          <strong>Permissões por função.</strong> Administradores gerenciam
          acessos; gestores organizam a operação; colaboradores acompanham sua
          equipe e seus aparelhos.
        </p>
      </div>
      {creating && (
        <Modal
          title="Criar equipe"
          onClose={() => {
            if (!saving) setCreating(false);
          }}
        >
          <form className="wt-team-form" onSubmit={createTeam}>
            <p className="wt-muted">
              Reúna pessoas e equipamentos em uma frente de trabalho.
            </p>
            <label className="wt-field">
              <span className="wt-label">Nome da equipe</span>
              <input
                className="wt-input"
                autoFocus
                required
                maxLength={80}
                value={name}
                onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ex.: Operação de campo"
                disabled={saving}
              />
            </label>
            <label className="wt-field">
              <span className="wt-label">Local de operação</span>
              <input
                className="wt-input"
                maxLength={120}
                value={location}
                onChange={(event) => setLocation(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ex.: Unidade Norte"
                disabled={saving}
              />
            </label>
            <fieldset className="wt-team-colors">
              <legend className="wt-label">Cor da equipe</legend>
              <div>
                {teamColors.map((option, index) => (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={`Cor ${["petróleo", "azul", "laranja", "verde", "rosa", "cinza"][index]}`}
                    aria-pressed={color === option}
                    key={option}
                    style={{ background: option }}
                    disabled={saving}
                    onClick={() => setColor(option)}
                  >
                    {color === option && <Check size={17} />}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </fieldset>
            {formError && (
              <p className="wt-error" role="alert">
                {formError}
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="wt-form-actions">
              <button
                type="button"
                className="wt-button-secondary"
                disabled={saving}
                onClick={() => setCreating(false)}
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                className="wt-button"
                type="submit"
                disabled={saving || !name.trim()}
              >
                {saving ? (
                  <LoaderCircle size={16} className="wt-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <Plus size={16} />
                )}
                {saving ? "Criando…" : "Criar equipe"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
src/portal/api.ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { create } from "zustand";

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    public status: number,
    public code: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
  }
}
export async function api<T>(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
  const response = await fetch(`/api/v1${path}`, {
    ...init,
    credentials: "same-origin",
    cache: "no-store",
    headers: {
      ...(init.body ? { "Content-Type": "application/json" } : {}),
      ...init.headers,
    },
  });
  const body: unknown = await response.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!response.ok) {
    const result = body as {
      error?: { message?: string; code?: string };
    } | null;
    throw new ApiError(
      result?.error?.message || "Não foi possível concluir. Tente novamente.",
      response.status,
      result?.error?.code || "REQUEST_FAILED",
    );
  }
  return body as T;
}
export function useResource<T>(path: string) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<T | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const controller = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  const reload = useCallback(async () => {
    controller.current?.abort();
    const active = new AbortController();
    controller.current = active;
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const result = await api<T>(path, { signal: active.signal });
      if (!active.signal.aborted) setData(result);
    } catch (cause) {
      if (!active.signal.aborted)
        setError(
          cause instanceof Error
            ? cause.message
            : "Não foi possível carregar os dados.",
        );
    } finally {
      if (!active.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [path]);
  useEffect(() => {
    setData(null);
    void reload();
    return () => controller.current?.abort();
  }, [reload]);
  return { data, loading, error, reload };
}
interface Notice {
  id: number;
  message: string;
  kind: "success" | "error";
}
export const useNotice = create<{ notice: Notice | null; dismiss: () => void }>(
  (set) => ({ notice: null, dismiss: () => set({ notice: null }) }),
);
export function notify(message: string, kind: "success" | "error" = "success") {
  useNotice.setState({ notice: { id: Date.now(), message, kind } });
}
export const roleLabel = {
  admin: "Administrador",
  manager: "Gestor",
  collaborator: "Colaborador",
};
export function formatDate(value: string | null, time = false) {
  if (!value) return "Não verificado";
  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "Data indisponível";
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    ...(time ? { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" } : { year: "numeric" }),
  }).format(date);
}

src/portal/ui.tsx
import { useEffect, useId, useRef } from "react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { AlertCircle, X, LoaderCircle } from "lucide-react";
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
export function Panel({
  title,
  action,
  children,
  className = "",
}: {
  title?: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <section className={`wt-panel ${className}`}>
      {(title || action) && (
        <div className="wt-panel-heading">
          {title && <h2>{title}</h2>}
          {action}
        </div>
      )}
      {children}
    </section>
  );
}
export function EmptyState({
  icon: Icon,
  title,
  description,
  action,
}: {
  icon: LucideIcon;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="wt-empty">
      <span>
        <Icon size={25} />
      </span>
      <h3>{title}</h3>
      <p>{description}</p>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}
export function ErrorNotice({
  message,
  onRetry,
}: {
  message: string;
  onRetry?: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="wt-error" role="alert">
      <AlertCircle size={19} />
      <p>{message}</p>
      {onRetry && (
        <button className="wt-button-secondary" onClick={onRetry}>
          Tentar novamente
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
export function Loading({ label = "Carregando dados…" }: { label?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="wt-loading" role="status">
      <LoaderCircle size={20} />
      {label}
    </div>
  );
}
export function Avatar({ name, size = 34 }: { name: string; size?: number }) {
  const initials = name
    .trim()
    .split(/\s+/)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map((p) => p[0])
    .join("")
    .toUpperCase();
  return (
    <span
      className="wt-avatar"
      style={{ width: size, height: size }}
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      {initials || "WT"}
    </span>
  );
}
export function Modal({
  title,
  onClose,
  children,
}: {
  title: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const dialog = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);
  const titleId = useId();
  const close = useRef(onClose);
  close.current = onClose;
  useEffect(() => {
    const element = dialog.current;
    const previous = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    element?.showModal();
    const original = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    return () => {
      element?.close();
      document.body.style.overflow = original;
      previous?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    };
  }, []);
  return (
    <dialog
      ref={dialog}
      className="wt-modal"
      aria-labelledby={titleId}
      onCancel={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        close.current();
      }}
      onClick={(e) => {
        if (e.target !== e.currentTarget) return;
        const bounds = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (
          e.clientX < bounds.left ||
          e.clientX > bounds.right ||
          e.clientY < bounds.top ||
          e.clientY > bounds.bottom
        )
          close.current();
      }}
    >
      <div className="wt-modal-heading">
        <div>
          <span className="wt-eyebrow">WALK TALK EMPRESAS</span>
          <h2 id={titleId}>{title}</h2>
        </div>
        <button
          className="wt-icon-button"
          aria-label="Fechar janela"
          onClick={onClose}
        >
          <X size={20} />
        </button>
      </div>
      {children}
    </dialog>
  );
}
src/sections/Benefits.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { Plus, Minus, ArrowUpRight, Download } from "lucide-react";
import { benefits } from "../assets/content";
import type { useInstall } from "../hooks/useInstall";
export function Benefits({
  installState,
}: {
  installState: ReturnType<typeof useInstall>;
}) {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const { install, available, installed, message } = installState;
  return (
    <section className="section benefits-section">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <div className="eyebrow">
              <span className="section-index">03 /</span> FEITO PARA IR ALÉM
            </div>
            <h2>
              MENOS LIMITES.
              <br />
              <span>MAIS POSSIBILIDADES.</span>
            </h2>
          </div>
          <p className="heading-aside">
            Tecnologia que aproxima.
            <br />
            Liberdade que acompanha.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="benefit-grid grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          {benefits.map(({ icon: Icon, title, label, description }, i) => (
            <article
              key={title}
              className={`benefit-card ${expanded === i ? "expanded" : ""}`}
            >
              <button
                aria-expanded={expanded === i}
                aria-controls={`benefit-${i}`}
                onClick={() => setExpanded(expanded === i ? null : i)}
              >
                <div className="benefit-top">
                  <Icon size={28} strokeWidth={1.4} />
                  <span>0{i + 1}</span>
                </div>
                <span className="micro-label">{label}</span>
                <h3>{title}</h3>
                <span className="benefit-more">
                  {expanded === i ? "Menos detalhes" : "Explore o benefício"}
                  {expanded === i ? <Minus size={18} /> : <Plus size={18} />}
                </span>
              </button>
              <div id={`benefit-${i}`}>
                <AnimatePresence>
                  {expanded === i && (
                    <motion.p
                      initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                      animate={{ height: "auto", opacity: 1 }}
                      exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                    >
                      {description}
                    </motion.p>
                  )}
                </AnimatePresence>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="closing">
          <span className="eyebrow">
            <span className="status-dot" /> A DISTÂNCIA MUDOU DE SIGNIFICADO
          </span>
          <h2>
            O PRÓXIMO DESTINO?
            <br />
            <span>VOCÊ ESCOLHE.</span>
          </h2>
          <p>Leve a sua voz aonde a vida levar você.</p>
          <div className="closing-actions">
            <a
              href="#ptt"
              className="primary-button"
              onClick={() => document.getElementById("ptt")?.focus()}
            >
              Experimente a conexão <ArrowUpRight size={18} />
            </a>
            <button
              className="secondary-button"
              onClick={install}
              disabled={installed}
            >
              <Download size={17} />
              {installed
                ? "Aplicativo instalado"
                : available
                  ? "Instalar WALK TALK"
                  : "Como instalar"}
            </button>
          </div>
          {message && (
            <p className="install-message" role="status">
              {message}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
src/sections/Coverage.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowUpRight,
  ArrowRight,
  MapPin,
  Radio,
  RefreshCw,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cities } from "../assets/content";
import paths from "../assets/world-paths.json";
const point = (i: number) => ({
  x: (cities[i].lng + 180) * 2.5,
  y: (90 - cities[i].lat) * 2.5,
});
export function Coverage() {
  const [origin, setOrigin] = useState(0);
  const [destination, setDestination] = useState(1);
  const [selecting, setSelecting] = useState<"origin" | "destination">(
    "destination",
  );
  const distance = Math.round(
    6371 *
      2 *
      Math.asin(
        Math.sqrt(
          Math.sin(
            ((cities[destination].lat - cities[origin].lat) * Math.PI) / 360,
          ) **
            2 +
            Math.cos((cities[origin].lat * Math.PI) / 180) *
              Math.cos((cities[destination].lat * Math.PI) / 180) *
              Math.sin(
                ((cities[destination].lng - cities[origin].lng) * Math.PI) /
                  360,
              ) **
                2,
        ),
      ),
  );
  const a = point(origin),
    b = point(destination);
  const curve = `M${a.x},${a.y} Q${(a.x + b.x) / 2},${Math.min(a.y, b.y) - 85} ${b.x},${b.y}`;
  const choose = (i: number) => {
    if (selecting === "origin") {
      if (i === destination) setDestination(origin);
      setOrigin(i);
      setSelecting("destination");
    } else {
      if (i === origin) setOrigin(destination);
      setDestination(i);
      setSelecting("origin");
    }
  };
  return (
    <section className="section coverage-section">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <div className="eyebrow">
              <span className="section-index">02 /</span> SEM FRONTEIRAS
            </div>
            <h2>
              LONGE É SÓ
              <br />
              <span>UM PONTO NO MAPA.</span>
            </h2>
          </div>
          <p className="heading-aside">
            De um continente a outro.
            <br />A mesma vontade de estar perto.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="map-card glass">
          <div className="map-toolbar">
            <span>
              <i className="status-dot" /> REDE GLOBAL WALK TALK
            </span>
            <span className="map-demo">
              ROTA ILUSTRATIVA <ArrowUpRight size={14} />
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="world-map">
            <svg
              viewBox="0 0 900 450"
              role="group"
              aria-label={`Rota ilustrativa de ${cities[origin].name} para ${cities[destination].name}`}
            >
              <defs>
                <pattern
                  id="map-grid"
                  width="45"
                  height="45"
                  patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse"
                >
                  <path
                    d="M 45 0 L 0 0 0 45"
                    fill="none"
                    stroke="#194352"
                    strokeWidth="0.5"
                  />
                </pattern>
                <filter id="route-glow">
                  <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="3" />
                </filter>
              </defs>
              <rect
                width="900"
                height="450"
                fill="url(#map-grid)"
                opacity="0.4"
              />
              <g className="continents">
                {paths.map((d, i) => (
                  <path d={d} key={i} />
                ))}
              </g>
              <path
                d={curve}
                className="map-route-glow"
                filter="url(#route-glow)"
              />
              <path
                d={curve}
                className="map-route"
                key={`${origin}-${destination}`}
              />
              {cities.map((city, i) => {
                const p = point(i);
                const selected = i === origin || i === destination;
                return (
                  <g
                    key={city.name}
                    className={`map-point ${selected ? "selected" : ""}`}
                    role="button"
                    tabIndex={0}
                    aria-label={`Selecionar ${city.name} como ${selecting === "origin" ? "origem" : "destino"}`}
                    onClick={() => choose(i)}
                    onKeyDown={(e) => {
                      if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
                        e.preventDefault();
                        choose(i);
                      }
                    }}
                  >
                    <circle cx={p.x} cy={p.y} r="18" fill="transparent" />
                    <circle className="point-halo" cx={p.x} cy={p.y} r="11" />
                    <circle className="point-core" cx={p.x} cy={p.y} r="4" />
                    <text
                      x={p.x}
                      y={p.y + (i === 1 ? -18 : 27)}
                      textAnchor="middle"
                    >
                      {city.name}
                    </text>
                  </g>
                );
              })}
            </svg>
          </div>
          <div className="map-distance">
            <span>UMA CONVERSA ENTRE</span>
            <strong>
              {cities[origin].name} <span>↔</span> {cities[destination].name}
            </strong>
            <small>
              ≈ {new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR").format(distance)} km em linha
              reta · rota ilustrativa
            </small>
          </div>
          <div className="route-panel">
            <button
              className={
                selecting === "origin" ? "route-place selected" : "route-place"
              }
              onClick={() => setSelecting("origin")}
              aria-pressed={selecting === "origin"}
            >
              <MapPin size={19} />
              <span>
                <small>ORIGEM</small>
                {cities[origin].name}
                <em>{cities[origin].country}</em>
              </span>
            </button>
            <button
              className="route-switch icon-button"
              aria-label="Inverter origem e destino"
              onClick={() => {
                setOrigin(destination);
                setDestination(origin);
              }}
            >
              <RefreshCw size={18} />
            </button>
            <button
              className={
                selecting === "destination"
                  ? "route-place selected"
                  : "route-place"
              }
              onClick={() => setSelecting("destination")}
              aria-pressed={selecting === "destination"}
            >
              <Radio size={19} />
              <span>
                <small>DESTINO</small>
                {cities[destination].name}
                <em>{cities[destination].country}</em>
              </span>
            </button>
            <div className="route-status" aria-live="polite">
              <span>
                <i className="status-dot" /> ROTA CONECTADA
              </span>
              <small>
                Escolha {selecting === "origin" ? "a origem" : "o destino"} no
                mapa <ArrowRight size={13} />
              </small>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div
          className="city-options"
          role="group"
          aria-label="Selecionar cidade"
        >
          {cities.map((city, i) => (
            <button
              key={city.name}
              onClick={() => choose(i)}
              aria-pressed={i === origin || i === destination}
            >
              {city.name}
              <ArrowUpRight size={12} />
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <p className="map-caption">
          Uma demonstração de conexão entre aparelhos compatíveis. Cobertura e
          disponibilidade dependem da rede contratada.
        </p>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export function FieldExperience() {

            >

              </span>
              <blockquote>“{current.message}”</blockquote>
              <p>{current.description}</p>
            </motion.div>
            <a href="#ptt" className="field-ptt-link">
              Experimente o PTT <ArrowUpRight size={17} />
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="field-tabs-shell">
          <div
            className="container field-tabs"
            role="tablist"
            aria-label="Momentos da expedição"
          >
            {moments.map((item, index) => (
              <button
                key={item.title}
                ref={(node) => {
                  tabs.current[index] = node;
                }}
                role="tab"
                id={`field-tab-${index}`}
                aria-controls="field-panel"
                aria-selected={moment === index}
                tabIndex={moment === index ? 0 : -1}
                onClick={() => setMoment(index)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (
                    !["ArrowLeft", "ArrowRight", "Home", "End"].includes(e.key)
                  )
                    return;
                  e.preventDefault();
                  const next =
                    e.key === "Home"
                      ? 0
                      : e.key === "End"
                        ? 2
                        : (moment + (e.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : 2)) % 3;
                  setMoment(next);
                  tabs.current[next]?.focus();
                }}
              >
                <span className="field-tab-index">0{index + 1}</span>
                <span>
                  <strong>{item.title}</strong>
                  <small>{item.label}</small>
                </span>
                <ArrowUpRight size={18} />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="container field-footnote">
        <span>UMA EXPEDIÇÃO. MUITOS MOTIVOS PARA ESTAR PERTO.</span>
        <span>Cenário e mensagens ilustrativos.</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export function AudioWave({ active = false }: { active?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <span className={`audio-wave ${active ? "active" : ""}`} aria-hidden="true">
      {Array.from({ length: 9 }, (_, i) => (
        <i
          key={i}
          style={{
            height: `${8 + Math.sin(i * 1.8) ** 2 * 20}px`,
            animationDelay: `${i * 0.07}s`,
          }}
        />
      ))}
    </span>
  );
}
src/sections/Journey.tsx
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  RotateCcw,
  Check,
  Radio,
  Satellite,
  AudioLines,
  ArrowUpRight,
  Mountain,
  TreePine,
  Waves,
  Sun,
  Play,
  Pause,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useExperience } from "../store/useExperience";
import { steps } from "../assets/content";
import { Globe } from "../components/Globe";
import { usePlayback } from "../hooks/usePlayback";
export function Journey() {
  const { playing, toggle, selectStep } = usePlayback();
  const step = useExperience((s) => s.step);
  const detailed = useExperience((s) => s.detailed);

  const current = steps[step];
  const CurrentIcon = current.icon;
  return (
    <section className="section journey-section">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="section-heading">
          <div>
            <div className="eyebrow">
              <span className="section-index">01 /</span> UMA CONEXÃO
              EXTRAORDINÁRIA
            </div>
            <h2>
              DA SUA VOZ.
              <br />
              <span>PARA QUALQUER LUGAR.</span>
            </h2>
          </div>
          <div className="heading-aside">
            <p>
              Um toque inicia uma jornada.
              <br />
              Descubra o caminho que conecta vocês.
            </p>
            <div
              className="segmented"
              role="group"
              aria-label="Quantidade de detalhes"
            >
              <button
                aria-pressed={!detailed}
                onClick={() => useExperience.getState().setDetailed(false)}
              >
                Resumido
              </button>
              <button
                aria-pressed={detailed}
                onClick={() => useExperience.getState().setDetailed(true)}
              >
                Detalhado
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div
          id="simulator-panel"
          role="region"
          className="simulator glass"
          tabIndex={0}
          aria-label="Simulador da mensagem. Use as setas esquerda e direita para navegar."
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (["ArrowLeft", "ArrowRight", "Home"].includes(e.key)) {
              e.preventDefault();
              selectStep(
                e.key === "Home" ? 0 : step + (e.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : -1),
              );
            }
          }}
        >
          <div className="sim-visual">
            <div className="sim-label">
              <span className="status-dot" /> JORNADA DO SINAL{" "}
              <span>SIMULAÇÃO INTERATIVA</span>
            </div>
            <Globe story />
            <img
              className="journey-device"
              src="/product/walk-talk-mobile.webp"
              width="480"
              height="720"
              loading="lazy"
              alt="Aparelho WALK TALK de origem"
            />
            <div className="journey-phase">
              <span className="status-dot" />
              {current.phase}
            </div>
            <div className={`signal-path signal-step-${step}`}>
              <i />
              <i />
              <i />
            </div>
            <div className="sim-endpoints">
              <span>
                <Radio size={15} /> SEU WALK TALK
              </span>
              <span>
                <Satellite size={15} /> REDE SATELITAL
              </span>
              <span>
                <AudioLines size={15} /> DESTINATÁRIO
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="sim-controls">
            <div className="sim-topline">
              <span>EXPLORE A CONEXÃO</span>
              <span className="cyan">
                0{step + 1}
                <span className="muted"> / 06</span>
              </span>
            </div>
            <div
              className="step-indicators"
              role="group"
              aria-label="Etapas da transmissão"
            >
              {steps.map((s, i) => (
                <button
                  key={s.title}
                  onClick={() => selectStep(i)}
                  aria-label={`Passo ${i + 1}: ${s.title}`}
                  aria-current={step === i ? "step" : undefined}
                  className={i <= step ? "complete" : ""}
                >
                  {i < step ? <Check size={13} /> : i + 1}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <motion.div
              className="current-step"
              key={`${step}-${detailed}`}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.25 }}
              aria-live="polite"
            >
              <div className="step-icon">
                <CurrentIcon size={26} />
              </div>
              <span className="micro-label">{current.location}</span>
              <h3>{current.title}</h3>
              <p>{detailed ? current.detail : current.short}</p>
            </motion.div>
            <div
              className="sim-progress"
              role="progressbar"
              aria-label="Progresso da simulação"
              aria-valuenow={step + 1}
              aria-valuemin={0}
              aria-valuemax={6}
            >
              <span style={{ width: `${((step + 1) / 6) * 100}%` }} />
            </div>
            <div className="playback-row">
              <button
                className="playback-button"
                aria-pressed={playing}
                onClick={toggle}
              >
                {playing ? <Pause size={15} /> : <Play size={15} />}{" "}
                {playing ? "Pausar jornada" : "Reproduzir jornada"}
              </button>
              <span>6 ETAPAS · UMA CONEXÃO</span>
            </div>
            <div className="sim-navigation">
              <button
                className="icon-button"
                aria-label="Reiniciar simulação"
                onClick={() => selectStep(0)}
              >
                <RotateCcw size={17} />
              </button>
              <div>
                <button
                  className="icon-button"
                  aria-label="Passo anterior"
                  disabled={step === 0}
                  onClick={() => selectStep(step - 1)}
                >
                  <ArrowLeft size={18} />
                </button>
                <button
                  className="next-button"
                  disabled={step === 5}
                  onClick={() => selectStep(step + 1)}
                >
                  Próximo <ArrowRight size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <small className="keyboard-note">
              Use também ← → com o simulador selecionado
            </small>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="story-layout">
          {[0, 1].map((part) => (
            <div key={part} className="journey-part">
              <div className="part-heading">
                <span>PARTE 0{part + 1}</span>
                <h3>
                  {part === 0
                    ? "Da sua voz ao satélite."
                    : "Do satélite até outro aparelho."}
                </h3>
                <ArrowUpRight size={22} />
              </div>
              <div className="story-grid">
                {steps.slice(part * 3, part * 3 + 3).map((s, index) => {
                  const number = part * 3 + index;
                  const Icon = s.icon;
                  return (
                    <article
                      key={s.title}
                      data-story-step={number}
                      className={`story-card ${step === number ? "highlighted" : ""}`}
                    >
                      <div className="story-card-top">
                        <Icon size={23} />
                        <span>0{number + 1}</span>
                      </div>
                      <h4>{s.title}</h4>
                      <p>{detailed ? s.detail : s.short}</p>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => {
                          selectStep(number);
                          document
                            .getElementById("simulator-panel")
                            ?.scrollIntoView({
                              behavior: matchMedia(
                                "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)",
                              ).matches
                                ? "instant"
                                : "smooth",
                              block: "center",
                            });
                          document
                            .getElementById("simulator-panel")
                            ?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
                        }}
                        aria-label={`Explorar etapa ${number + 1}: ${s.title}`}
                      >
                        Explorar etapa <ArrowUpRight size={14} />
                      </button>
                    </article>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="remote-banner">
          <div>
            <Radio size={19} />
            <p>
              O sinal de celular termina.
              <br />
              <strong>A sua conexão continua.</strong>
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="landscapes">
            {[
              { icon: Mountain, name: "Montanhas" },
              { icon: TreePine, name: "Florestas" },
              { icon: Waves, name: "Mar" },
              { icon: Sun, name: "Desertos" },
            ].map(({ icon: Icon, name }) => (
              <span key={name}>
                <Icon size={22} />
                {name}
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

src/store/useExperience.ts
import { create } from "zustand";

type Experience = {
  step: number;
  ptt: boolean;
  sound: boolean;
  detailed: boolean;
  pulse: number;
  journey: number;
  manual: boolean;
  resumeScroll: () => void;
  setScrollStep: (step: number) => void;
  setStep: (step: number) => void;
  setPTT: (active: boolean) => void;
  toggleSound: () => void;
  setDetailed: (value: boolean) => void;
  setJourney: (value: number) => void;
};
export const useExperience = create<Experience>((set) => ({
  step: 0,
  ptt: false,
  sound: false,
  detailed: false,
  pulse: 0,
  journey: 0,
  manual: false,
  resumeScroll: () => set({ manual: false }),
  setScrollStep: (step) =>
    set((s) => (s.manual ? s : { step: Math.max(0, Math.min(5, step)) })),
  setStep: (step) =>
    set({ step: Math.max(0, Math.min(5, step)), manual: true }),
  setPTT: (active) =>
    set((s) =>
      s.ptt === active
        ? s
        : { ptt: active, pulse: !active ? s.pulse + 1 : s.pulse },
    ),
  toggleSound: () => set((s) => ({ sound: !s.sound })),
  setDetailed: (detailed) => set({ detailed }),
  setJourney: (journey) => set({ journey }),
}));

function normalize(value: string) {
  return value
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .toLocaleLowerCase("pt-BR");
}

export function HelpPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("Todos");
  const results = useMemo(
    () =>
      questions.filter(
        (item) =>
          (category === "Todos" || category === item.category) &&
          normalize(`${item.question} ${item.answer}`).includes(
            normalize(search.trim()),
          ),
      ),
    [search, category],
  );
  const reset = () => {
    setSearch("");
    setCategory("Todos");
  };
  return (
    <PublicShell active="/ajuda">
      <section className="wp-wrap wp-intro wp-help-intro">
        <PageHeading
          eyebrow="WALK TALK / CENTRAL DE AJUDA"
          title={
            <>
              MENOS DÚVIDAS.
              <br />
              <em>MAIS CLAREZA.</em>
            </>
          }
          description="Encontre orientações para começar, organizar a equipe e aproveitar o portal no dia a dia."
        />
        <div className="wp-help-search">
          <Search size={22} />
          <label htmlFor="help-search" className="wp-sr-only">
            Buscar na central de ajuda
          </label>
          <input
            id="help-search"
            type="search"
            placeholder="Busque por convites, aparelhos, instalação…"
            value={search}
            onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
          />
          {search && (
            <button
              type="button"
              aria-label="Limpar busca"
              onClick={() => setSearch("")}
            >
              <X size={19} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        <p className="wp-search-hint">
          Experimente:{" "}
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              setCategory("Todos");
              setSearch("convite");
            }}
          >
            convite
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              setCategory("Todos");
              setSearch("instalar");
            }}
          >
            instalar
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              setCategory("Todos");
              setSearch("conferência");
            }}
          >
            conferência
          </button>
        </p>
      </section>
      <section
        className="wp-wrap wp-help-layout"
        aria-label="Perguntas frequentes"
      >
        <aside className="wp-help-sidebar">
          <h2>EXPLORE POR ASSUNTO</h2>
          <div
            className="wp-help-categories"
            role="group"
            aria-label="Filtrar por assunto"
          >
            {categories.map((item) => (
              <button
                key={item}
                aria-pressed={category === item}
                onClick={() => setCategory(item)}
              >
                {item}
                <span>
                  {item === "Todos"
                    ? questions.length
                    : questions.filter((question) => question.category === item)
                        .length}
                </span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="wp-help-sidebar-note">
            <LifeBuoy size={23} />
            <h3>Precisa de acesso?</h3>
            <p>
              Procure o administrador da sua empresa ou o proprietário do site
              para questões de conta e permissões.
            </p>
          </div>
        </aside>
        <div className="wp-faq-results">
          <div className="wp-results-heading">
            <h2>{category === "Todos" ? "Perguntas frequentes" : category}</h2>
            <span role="status" aria-live="polite">
              {results.length}{" "}
              {results.length === 1 ? "resultado" : "resultados"}
            </span>
          </div>
          {results.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="wp-faq-list">
              {results.map((item) => (
                <details key={item.question} className="wp-faq">
                  <summary>
                    <span>
                      <small>{item.category}</small>
                      {item.question}
                    </span>
                    <ChevronDown size={21} />
                  </summary>
                  <div>
                    <p>{item.answer}</p>
                    {item.link && (
                      <a className="wp-text-link" href={item.link}>
                        {item.label} <ArrowUpRight size={16} />
                      </a>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </details>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="wp-help-empty">
              <Search size={34} />
              <h3>Nenhum resultado por aqui.</h3>
              <p>
                Tente outra palavra ou limpe os filtros para ver todos os
                assuntos.
              </p>
              <button className="wp-button" onClick={reset}>
                Limpar filtros
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
      <section className="wp-wrap wp-help-resources">
        <a href="/guia.html">
          <BookOpen />
          <div>
            <h2>Guia do projeto</h2>
            <p>Instalação, estrutura e hospedagem explicadas.</p>
          </div>
          <ArrowUpRight />
        </a>
        <a href="/privacidade">
          <ShieldCheck />
          <div>
            <h2>Privacidade e dados</h2>
            <p>Entenda o que o portal guarda e como usa.</p>
          </div>
          <ArrowUpRight />
        </a>
        <a href="/walk-talk-source.zip" download>
          <Download />
          <div>
            <h2>Código completo</h2>
            <p>Baixe o projeto para estudar e evoluir.</p>
          </div>
          <ArrowUpRight />
        </a>
      </section>
    </PublicShell>
  );
}
