src/website/PublicShell.tsx
"use client";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { ArrowUpRight, Globe2, Menu, X } from "lucide-react";
import "./public-pages.css";

const navigation = [
  ["/produto", "Produto"],
  ["/solucoes", "Soluções"],
  ["/saude", "Saúde ocupacional"],
  ["/ajuda", "Ajuda"],
] as const;

export function PublicShell({
  active,
  children,
}: {
  active: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const header = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const toggle = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const keydown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(false);
        toggle.current?.focus();
      }
    };
    const pointerdown = (event: PointerEvent) => {
      if (
        event.target instanceof Node &&
        !header.current?.contains(event.target)
      )
        setOpen(false);
    };
    const resize = () => {
      if (matchMedia("(min-width: 901px)").matches) setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", keydown);
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", pointerdown);
    window.addEventListener("resize", resize);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", keydown);
      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", pointerdown);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", resize);
    };
  }, [open]);
  return (
    <div className="wp-site">
      <a className="wp-skip" href="#conteudo">
        Pular para o conteúdo
      </a>
      <header className="wp-header" ref={header}>
        <div className="wp-header-inner">
          <a href="/" className="wp-logo">
            <Globe2 size={29} strokeWidth={1.3} />
            <span>
              WALK<span>TALK</span>
              <small>COMUNICAÇÃO SEM FRONTEIRAS</small>
            </span>
          </a>
          <nav
            id="wp-navigation"
            aria-label="Navegação principal"
            className={open ? "wp-navigation is-open" : "wp-navigation"}
          >
            {navigation.map(([href, label]) => (
              <a
                key={href}
                href={href}
                aria-current={active === href ? "page" : undefined}
                onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
              >
                {label}
              </a>
            ))}
            <a href="/portal" className="wp-mobile-portal">
              Acessar portal <ArrowUpRight size={15} />
            </a>
          </nav>
          <a href="/portal" className="wp-header-cta">
            Portal da empresa <ArrowUpRight size={15} />
          </a>
          <button
            ref={toggle}
            className="wp-menu"
            aria-label={open ? "Fechar menu" : "Abrir menu"}
            aria-expanded={open}
            aria-controls="wp-navigation"
            onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}
          >
            {open ? <X /> : <Menu />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>
      <main id="conteudo" tabIndex={-1}>
        {children}
      </main>
      <footer className="wp-footer">
        <div className="wp-wrap wp-footer-grid">
          <div>
            <a className="wp-footer-brand" href="/">
              WALK<span>TALK</span>
            </a>
            <p>
              O mundo é grande.
              <br />
              Sua voz, maior.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2>Explore</h2>
            <a href="/produto">O aparelho</a>
            <a href="/solucoes">Para empresas</a>
            <a href="/saude">Saúde ocupacional</a>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2>Seu espaço</h2>
            <a href="/portal">Portal da empresa</a>
            <a href="/ajuda">Central de ajuda</a>
            <a href="/privacidade">Privacidade e dados</a>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2>O projeto</h2>
            <a href="/guia.html">Guia técnico</a>
            <a href="/walk-talk-source.zip" download>
              Código completo
            </a>
            <a href="/#jornada">Experiência interativa</a>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="wp-wrap wp-footer-bottom">
          <span>© {new Date().getFullYear()} WALK TALK</span>
          <span>Conceito de produto · Portal empresarial funcional</span>
          <span>PORTUGUÊS / BRASIL</span>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}

export function PageHeading({
  eyebrow,
  title,
  description,
}: {
  eyebrow: string;
  title: ReactNode;
  description: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="wp-heading">
      <span className="wp-eyebrow">
        <i />
        {eyebrow}
      </span>
      <h1>{title}</h1>
      <p>{description}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

export function PortalCTA() {
  return (
    <section className="wp-wrap wp-cta">
      <div>
        <span className="wp-eyebrow">UM ESPAÇO PARA SUA EQUIPE</span>
        <h2>Organize o próximo passo.</h2>
        <p>
          Crie sua empresa e comece com equipes, aparelhos e canais de
          comunicação.
        </p>
      </div>
      <a className="wp-button" href="/portal">
        Acessar o portal <ArrowUpRight size={18} />
      </a>
    </section>
  );
}
src/website/SolutionsPage.tsx
"use client";
import { useRef, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from "react";
import {
  ArrowUpRight,
  Building2,
  Check,
  ClipboardCheck,
  HardHat,
  HeartPulse,
  MessagesSquare,
  Mountain,
  Radio,
  Truck,
  UserCog,
  Users,
} from "lucide-react";
import { PageHeading, PortalCTA, PublicShell } from "./PublicShell";

const sectors = [
  {
    id: "campo",
    label: "Equipes em campo",
    icon: Mountain,
    title: "Prepare a equipe antes de sair.",
    description:
      "Organize a base da operação: quem participa, quais aparelhos estão disponíveis e o que precisa ser alinhado antes da atividade.",
    steps: [
      "Crie uma equipe para a frente de trabalho.",
      "Associe os aparelhos e os responsáveis.",
      "Registre uma conferência manual e compartilhe orientações no canal da equipe.",
    ],
    note: "O portal precisa de internet. As conferências registradas são manuais e não representam localização ou telemetria dos aparelhos.",
    code: "FIELD OPERATIONS",
    image: true,
  },
  {
    id: "logistica",
    label: "Logística",
    icon: Truck,
    title: "Uma visão organizada dos aparelhos.",
    description:
      "Mantenha um inventário comum para as pessoas que coordenam a movimentação e a distribuição dos equipamentos.",
    steps: [
      "Cadastre os aparelhos com sua identificação.",
      "Atualize o status e a pessoa responsável.",
      "Filtre a listagem e exporte os dados autorizados em CSV.",
    ],
    note: "O inventário é atualizado pela equipe. O sistema não rastreia veículos, entregas, posição ou disponibilidade física em tempo real.",
    code: "LOGISTICS",
  },
  {
    id: "obras",
    label: "Obras e manutenção",
    icon: HardHat,
    title: "Cada frente de trabalho, seu espaço.",
    description:
      "Separe as equipes da empresa e reúna os alinhamentos de cada frente em canais de texto com acesso por perfil.",
    steps: [
      "Organize as frentes de serviço em equipes.",
      "Use convites para incluir gestores e colaboradores.",
      "Compartilhe alinhamentos no canal Geral ou no canal da equipe.",
    ],
    note: "As mensagens são comunicação administrativa pela internet. Não use o portal como canal de emergência ou substituto dos procedimentos de segurança.",
    code: "WORKSITE",
  },
];

export function SolutionsPage() {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(0);
  const tabs = useRef<Array<HTMLButtonElement | null>>([]);
  const current = sectors[selected];
  const changeTab = (
    event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>,
    index: number,
  ) => {
    let next = index;
    if (event.key === "ArrowRight") next = (index + 1) % sectors.length;
    else if (event.key === "ArrowLeft")
      next = (index - 1 + sectors.length) % sectors.length;
    else if (event.key === "Home") next = 0;
    else if (event.key === "End") next = sectors.length - 1;
    else return;
    event.preventDefault();
    setSelected(next);
    tabs.current[next]?.focus();
  };
  return (
    <PublicShell active="/solucoes">
      <section className="wp-wrap wp-intro wp-solutions-intro">
        <PageHeading
          eyebrow="WALK TALK / PARA EMPRESAS"
          title={
            <>
              GENTE CONECTADA.
              <br />
              <em>OPERAÇÃO ORGANIZADA.</em>
            </>
          }
          description="Um espaço para reunir as pessoas, os equipamentos e as conversas da sua empresa. Com clareza sobre quem pode acessar cada informação."
        />
        <a className="wp-button" href="/portal">
          Criar espaço da empresa <ArrowUpRight size={18} />
        </a>
        <div className="wp-capability-strip">
          <span>
            <Users size={17} />
            Equipes e perfis
          </span>
          <span>
            <Radio size={17} />
            Inventário de aparelhos
          </span>
          <span>
            <MessagesSquare size={17} />
            Mensagens por canal
          </span>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section className="wp-wrap wp-sector-section">
        <div className="wp-section-heading">
          <span className="wp-eyebrow">DO SEU JEITO DE TRABALHAR</span>
          <h2>Uma base. Diferentes rotinas.</h2>
          <p>Explore exemplos de como organizar o portal para sua operação.</p>
        </div>
        <div
          className="wp-sector-tabs"
          role="tablist"
          aria-label="Cenários de uso"
        >
          {sectors.map(({ id, label, icon: Icon }, index) => (
            <button
              key={id}
              ref={(node) => {
                tabs.current[index] = node;
              }}
              role="tab"
              id={`sector-${id}`}
              aria-selected={selected === index}
              aria-controls="sector-panel"
              tabIndex={selected === index ? 0 : -1}
              onKeyDown={(event) => changeTab(event, index)}
              onClick={() => setSelected(index)}
            >
              <Icon size={19} />
              {label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div
          className="wp-sector-panel"
          role="tabpanel"
          id="sector-panel"
          aria-labelledby={`sector-${current.id}`}
          tabIndex={0}
        >
          <div
            className={`wp-sector-visual ${current.image ? "has-photo" : ""}`}
          >
            {current.image && (
              <img
                src="/landscapes/expedition.webp"
                alt="Paisagem montanhosa para ilustrar equipes em campo"
                width="1536"
                height="1024"
                loading="lazy"
              />
            )}
            <div className="wp-sector-overlay">
              <span>{current.code}</span>
              <current.icon size={58} strokeWidth={1} />
              <strong>{current.label}</strong>
              <small>Cenário ilustrativo de organização</small>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="wp-sector-copy">
            <span className="wp-eyebrow">FLUXO SUGERIDO / 0{selected + 1}</span>
            <h3>{current.title}</h3>
            <p>{current.description}</p>
            <ol>
              {current.steps.map((step, index) => (
                <li key={step}>
                  <span>0{index + 1}</span>
                  {step}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
            <p className="wp-sector-note">{current.note}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section className="wp-wrap wp-section">
        <div className="wp-section-heading">
          <span className="wp-eyebrow">ACESSO COM CONTEXTO</span>
          <h2>Cada pessoa, seu papel.</h2>
          <p>
            As permissões são verificadas no servidor e limitadas à empresa.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="wp-three-grid wp-roles">
          <article>
            <UserCog />
            <h3>Administrador</h3>
            <p>Cria e organiza o espaço da empresa.</p>
            <ul>
              <li>
                <Check />
                Gerencia convites e perfis
              </li>
              <li>
                <Check />
                Organiza equipes e aparelhos
              </li>
              <li>
                <Check />
                Acessa os canais da empresa
              </li>
            </ul>
          </article>
          <article>
            <Building2 />
            <h3>Gestor</h3>
            <p>Acompanha a rotina e os equipamentos.</p>
            <ul>
              <li>
                <Check />
                Gerencia equipes e aparelhos
              </li>
              <li>
                <Check />
                Consulta o inventário da empresa
              </li>
              <li>
                <Check />
                Participa dos canais de comunicação
              </li>
            </ul>
          </article>
          <article>
            <Users />
            <h3>Colaborador</h3>
            <p>Participa com acesso ao seu contexto.</p>
            <ul>
              <li>
                <Check />
                Consulta aparelhos próprios e da equipe
              </li>
              <li>
                <Check />
                Confere aparelhos atribuídos a si
              </li>
              <li>
                <Check />
                Acessa Geral e o canal da sua equipe
              </li>
            </ul>
          </article>
        </div>
        <p className="wp-fineprint wp-role-note">
          O convite do portal e o acesso à hospedagem são controles separados.
          Para participar, a pessoa também precisa estar autorizada a abrir o
          site.
        </p>
      </section>
      <section className="wp-wrap wp-health-band">
        <div className="wp-health-band-icon">
          <HeartPulse size={37} strokeWidth={1.2} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <span className="wp-eyebrow">UMA ROTINA QUE TAMBÉM CUIDA</span>
          <h2>Saúde ocupacional é informação.</h2>
          <p>
            Uma página dedicada à prevenção de LER/DORT, ergonomia e organização
            do trabalho. Para consultar, conversar e buscar orientação.
          </p>
          <a className="wp-text-link" href="/saude">
            Explorar saúde ocupacional <ArrowUpRight size={17} />
          </a>
        </div>
        <ClipboardCheck
          className="wp-health-decoration"
          size={115}
          strokeWidth={0.7}
        />
      </section>
      <PortalCTA />
    </PublicShell>
  );
}

tests/experience.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";
for (const width of [360, 768, 1280]) {
  test(`layout ${width}px sem overflow e menu acessível`, async ({
    page,
  }, testInfo) => {
    await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 900 });
    const errors: string[] = [];
    page.on("pageerror", (error) => errors.push(error.message));
    await page.goto("/");
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toContainText(
      "LONGE DE TUDO.",
    );
    expect(
      await page.evaluate(
        () => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth,
      ),
    ).toBe(true);
    if (width === 360) {
      await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Abrir menu" }).click();
      await page
        .getByRole("navigation", { name: "Navegação principal" })
        .getByRole("link", { name: "Como funciona", exact: true })
        .click();
      await expect(page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Navegação principal" })).toBeHidden();
    }
    for (const id of ["expedicao", "jornada", "cobertura", "beneficios"]) {
      await page.locator(`#${id}`).scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
      await expect(page.locator(`#${id} .section`)).toBeVisible();
    }
    await page.evaluate(() => window.scrollTo(0, 0));
    await expect(page.locator(".section-placeholder")).toHaveCount(0);
    await page.screenshot({
      path: testInfo.outputPath(`walk-talk-${width}.png`),
      fullPage: true,
    });
    expect(errors).toEqual([]);
  });
}
test("PTT: mouse, espaço, cancelamento e som desligado inicialmente", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await page.goto("/");
  await expect(
    page.getByRole("button", { name: "Ativar sons" }),
  ).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
  const ptt = page.locator("#ptt");
  await ptt.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  const box = (await ptt.boundingBox())!;
  await page.mouse.move(box.x + 30, box.y + 20);
  await page.mouse.down();
  await expect(ptt).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
  await page.mouse.up();
  await expect(ptt).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
  await expect(
    page.getByText("Sinal enviado ao satélite · simulação"),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await ptt.focus();
  await page.keyboard.down("Space");
  await expect(ptt).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
  await page.keyboard.up("Space");
  await expect(ptt).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
  await page.keyboard.down("Space");
  await page.evaluate(() => window.dispatchEvent(new Event("blur")));
  await expect(ptt).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
  await page.keyboard.up("Space");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Ativar sons" }).click();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole("button", { name: "Silenciar sons" }),
  ).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
});
test("simulador: limites, teclado, seis etapas, detalhe e reinício", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await page.goto("/#simulador");
  const sim = page.locator("#simulator-panel");
  await sim.focus();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Reiniciar simulação" }).click();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole("button", { name: "Passo anterior" }),
  ).toBeDisabled();
  await sim.focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
  await expect(sim.getByRole("heading")).toHaveText("Sua voz vira dados");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Detalhado", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(sim).toContainText("pacotes digitais");
  await page
    .getByRole("button", {
      name: "Passo 6: A sua voz, do outro lado",
      exact: true,
    })
    .click();
  await expect(sim.getByRole("heading")).toHaveText("A sua voz, do outro lado");
  await expect(
    page.getByRole("button", { name: "Próximo", exact: true }),
  ).toBeDisabled();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Reiniciar simulação" }).click();
  await expect(sim.getByRole("heading")).toHaveText("Pressione e fale");
});
test("mapa, benefícios, instruções de instalação e PWA", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/#cobertura");
  await page
    .locator(".city-options")
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Tóquio" })
    .click();
  await expect(page.locator(".route-panel")).toContainText("Tóquio");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Inverter origem e destino" }).click();
  await expect(page.locator(".route-place").first()).toContainText("Tóquio");
  await page.locator("#beneficios").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  const benefit = page.locator(".benefit-card").first().getByRole("button");
  await benefit.click();
  await expect(benefit).toHaveAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
  await expect(
    page.getByText("A disponibilidade real depende", { exact: false }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await benefit.click();
  await expect(benefit).toHaveAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Como instalar" }).click();
  await expect(page.locator(".install-message")).toContainText("Safari");
  const manifest = await (await page.request.get("/manifest.json")).json();
  expect(manifest.display).toBe("standalone");
  expect(manifest.icons).toHaveLength(3);
  await expect
    .poll(() =>
      page.evaluate(
        async () => !!(await navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration()),
      ),
    )
    .toBe(true);
});
test("PTT por toque encerra ao cancelar o gesto", async ({ browser }) => {
  const context = await browser.newContext({
    viewport: { width: 360, height: 780 },
    hasTouch: true,
    isMobile: true,
    reducedMotion: "reduce",
  });
  const page = await context.newPage();
  await page.goto("http://localhost:4173");
  const ptt = page.locator("#ptt");
  await ptt.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  const box = (await ptt.boundingBox())!;
  const cdp = await context.newCDPSession(page);
  await cdp.send("Input.dispatchTouchEvent", {
    type: "touchStart",
    touchPoints: [{ x: box.x + 35, y: box.y + 20 }],
  });
  await expect(ptt).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
  await cdp.send("Input.dispatchTouchEvent", {
    type: "touchCancel",
    touchPoints: [],
  });
  await expect(ptt).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
  await context.close();
});

test("PWA: página offline sem armazenar contas ou conversas no cache", async ({
  page,
  context,
}) => {
  await page.goto("/");
  await page.evaluate(async () => {
    await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
  });
  await page.reload();
  await expect
    .poll(() => page.evaluate(() => !!navigator.serviceWorker.controller))
    .toBe(true);
  await context.setOffline(true);
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveText(
    "Vamos nos reconectar.",
  );
  const cachedPaths = await page.evaluate(async () => {
    const keys = await caches.keys();
    return (
      await Promise.all(
        keys.map(async (key) =>
          (await (await caches.open(key)).keys()).map(
            (request) => new URL(request.url).pathname,
          ),
        ),
      )
    ).flat();
  });
  expect(
    cachedPaths.some(
      (path) => path.startsWith("/api/") || path.startsWith("/portal"),
    ),
  ).toBe(false);
  await context.setOffline(false);
});

test("produto: pontos clicáveis e fechamento pelo teclado", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await page.goto("/");
  const antenna = page.getByRole("button", {

  await expect(page.locator("#product-detail")).toContainText(
    "Conexão que vai além",
  );
  await page
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Fechar detalhe do aparelho" })
    .focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  await expect(antenna).toHaveAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  await page
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Explorar Comando PTT", exact: true })
    .click();
  await expect(page.locator("#product-detail")).toContainText(

  );

test("jornada: reprodução automática, pausa e controle manual", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await page.goto("/#simulador");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Reiniciar simulação" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Reproduzir jornada" }).click();
  await expect(page.locator("#simulator-panel h3")).toHaveText(
    "Sua voz vira dados",
    { timeout: 6000 },
  );
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Pausar jornada" }).click();
  await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
  await expect(page.locator("#simulator-panel h3")).toHaveText(
    "Sua voz vira dados",
  );
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Reproduzir jornada" }).click();
  await page
    .getByRole("button", {
      name: "Passo 6: A sua voz, do outro lado",
      exact: true,
    })
    .click();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole("button", { name: "Reproduzir jornada" }),
  ).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
  await expect(page.locator("#simulator-panel h3")).toHaveText(
    "A sua voz, do outro lado",
  );
});
tests/pages.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";

const publicRoutes = ["/produto", "/solucoes", "/ajuda", "/privacidade"];

test("páginas: navegação conecta produto, soluções, ajuda e privacidade", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/produto");
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toContainText("HORIZONTE.");
  const navigation = page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Navegação principal" });
  await navigation.getByRole("link", { name: "Soluções", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/solucoes$/);
  await expect(navigation.getByRole("link", { name: "Soluções", exact: true })).toHaveAttribute("aria-current", "page");
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toContainText("OPERAÇÃO ORGANIZADA.");
  await navigation.getByRole("link", { name: "Ajuda", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/ajuda$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole("contentinfo").getByRole("link", { name: "Privacidade e dados", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/privacidade$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole("contentinfo").getByRole("link", { name: "Portal da empresa", exact: true })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/portal");
});

test("páginas: menu mobile fecha com Escape, retorna foco e navega para saúde", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 360, height: 900 });
  await page.goto("/produto");
  const navigation = page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Navegação principal" });
  const toggle = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Abrir menu", exact: true });
  await expect(navigation).toBeHidden();
  await toggle.click();
  await expect(navigation).toBeVisible();
  await navigation.getByRole("link", { name: "Soluções", exact: true }).focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
  await expect(navigation).toBeHidden();
  await expect(toggle).toBeFocused();
  await expect(toggle).toHaveAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  await toggle.click();
  await navigation.getByRole("link", { name: "Saúde ocupacional", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/saude$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toContainText("CUIDAR DA ROTINA.");
});

test("soluções: teclado percorre cenários e apresenta o fluxo correspondente", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/solucoes");
  const tabs = page.getByRole("tablist", { name: "Cenários de uso" });
  const field = tabs.getByRole("tab", { name: "Equipes em campo", exact: true });
  const logistics = tabs.getByRole("tab", { name: "Logística", exact: true });
  const worksite = tabs.getByRole("tab", { name: "Obras e manutenção", exact: true });
  const panel = page.getByRole("tabpanel");
  await expect(field).toHaveAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
  await field.focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
  await expect(logistics).toBeFocused();
  await expect(logistics).toHaveAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
  await expect(panel).toContainText("Uma visão organizada dos aparelhos.");
  await expect(panel).toContainText("exporte os dados autorizados em CSV");
  await page.keyboard.press("End");
  await expect(worksite).toBeFocused();
  await expect(panel).toContainText("Cada frente de trabalho, seu espaço.");
  await expect(panel).toContainText("não use o portal como canal de emergência", { ignoreCase: true });
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
  await expect(field).toBeFocused();
  await expect(panel).toContainText("Prepare a equipe antes de sair.");
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowLeft");
  await expect(worksite).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press("Home");
  await expect(field).toBeFocused();
  await expect(field).toHaveAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
});

test("ajuda: busca sem acentos combina com categorias, expande resposta e permite limpar filtros", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/ajuda");
  const search = page.getByRole("searchbox", { name: /Buscar na central de ajuda/ });
  const categories = page.getByRole("group", { name: "Filtrar por assunto" });
  const results = page.getByRole("region", { name: "Perguntas frequentes", exact: true });
  const questions = results.locator("details");
  const allCount = await questions.count();
  expect(allCount).toBeGreaterThan(3);

  await categories.getByRole("button", { name: /^Equipamentos/ }).click();
  await expect(categories.getByRole("button", { name: /^Equipamentos/ })).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
  await expect(questions).toHaveCount(3);
  await search.fill("CONFERENCIA");
  await expect(questions).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(results.getByRole("status")).toHaveText("1 resultado");
  const conference = questions.filter({ hasText: "O que significa registrar uma conferência?" });
  await conference.locator("summary").click();
  await expect(conference).toHaveAttribute("open", "");
  await expect(conference).toContainText("É um registro manual feito por uma pessoa.");

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Limpar busca", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(search).toHaveValue("");
  await expect(questions).toHaveCount(3);
  await search.fill("assunto-que-nao-existe-qa");
  await expect(questions).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(results.getByRole("heading", { name: "Nenhum resultado por aqui.", exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await results.getByRole("button", { name: "Limpar filtros", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(search).toHaveValue("");
  await expect(categories.getByRole("button", { name: /^Todos/ })).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
  await expect(questions).toHaveCount(allCount);

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "instalar", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(search).toHaveValue("instalar");
  await expect(questions).toHaveCount(1);
  const install = questions.first();
  await install.locator("summary").click();
  await expect(install).toContainText("Adicionar à Tela de Início");
});

for (const width of [360, 768, 1280]) {
  test(`páginas ${width}px: leitura em modo escuro, sem overflow ou erros de execução`, async ({ page }) => {
    await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 900 });
    const errors: string[] = [];
    page.on("pageerror", (error) => errors.push(error.message));
    for (const route of publicRoutes) {
      const response = await page.goto(route);
      expect(response?.status(), route).toBe(200);
      await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toHaveCount(1);
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toHaveCount(1);
      await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toBeVisible();
      const surface = await page.locator(".wp-site").evaluate((element) => {
        const style = getComputedStyle(element);
        const channels = style.backgroundColor.match(/[\d.]+/g)?.slice(0, 3).map(Number) ?? [];
        return {
          dark: style.colorScheme.includes("dark") && channels.length === 3 && channels.every(channel => channel < 65),
          overflow: document.documentElement.scrollWidth > innerWidth + 1,
        };
      });
      expect(surface.dark, `${route} should have a dark reading surface`).toBe(true);
      expect(surface.overflow, `overflow in ${route} at ${width}px`).toBe(false);
      const images = page.locator("main img");
      for (const image of await images.all()) {
        await image.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
        await expect.poll(() => image.evaluate((node: HTMLImageElement) => node.complete && node.naturalWidth > 0)).toBe(true);
      }
    }
    expect(errors).toEqual([]);
  });
}

test("endereço inexistente: resposta 404 oferece um caminho para a ajuda", async ({ page }) => {
  const response = await page.goto("/pagina-que-nao-existe-v5");
  expect(response?.status()).toBe(404);
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toContainText("o seu caminho.");
  await page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Continuar navegando" }).getByRole("link", { name: "Central de ajuda", exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/ajuda$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole("main")).toBeVisible();
});
tests/portal.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";
import type { APIRequestContext, Page } from "@playwright/test";
import type {
  Session,
  DevicesResponse,
  TeamsResponse,
  MessagesResponse,
} from "../server/types";

const origin = "http://localhost:4173";
type TestUser = { id: string; email: string; name: string };
function identity(label: string): TestUser {
  const id = `qa-${label}-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
  return { id, email: `${id}@example.test`, name: `Pessoa ${label}` };
}
function headers(user: TestUser) {
  return {
    "oai-authenticated-user-id": user.id,
    "oai-authenticated-user-email": user.email,
    "oai-authenticated-user-full-name": encodeURIComponent(user.name),
    "oai-authenticated-user-full-name-encoding": "percent-encoded-utf-8",
  };
}
async function api<T>(
  request: APIRequestContext,
  user: TestUser,
  path: string,
  method = "GET",
  data?: unknown,
): Promise<T> {
  const response = await request.fetch(`/api/v1${path}`, {
    method,
    headers: { ...headers(user), Origin: origin },
    ...(data === undefined ? {} : { data }),
  });
  expect(
    response.ok(),
    `${method} ${path}: ${response.status()} ${await response.text()}`,
  ).toBe(true);
  return response.json() as Promise<T>;
}
async function bootstrap(
  request: APIRequestContext,
  user: TestUser,
  examples = false,
) {
  await api(request, user, "/workspace", "POST", {
    name: `Empresa QA ${user.id}`,
    withExamples: examples,
  });
}
async function openPortal(page: Page, user: TestUser, hash = "overview") {
  await page.setExtraHTTPHeaders(headers(user));
  await page.goto(`/portal#${hash}`);
  await expect(page.locator("#portal-content")).toBeVisible();
}

test("portal: onboarding cria empresa sem exemplos por padrão e mantém dados após reload", async ({
  page,
  request,
}) => {
  const user = identity("onboarding");
  await page.setExtraHTTPHeaders(headers(user));
  await page.goto("/portal");
  await expect(
    page.getByLabel("Nome da empresa", { exact: true }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  const sampleOption = page.getByRole("checkbox", {
    name: /Incluir equipes e aparelhos fictícios/,
  });
  await expect(sampleOption).not.toBeChecked();
  const name = `Empresa de onboarding ${user.id}`;
  await page.getByLabel("Nome da empresa", { exact: true }).fill(name);
  await page
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Criar empresa", exact: true })
    .click();
  await expect(page.locator(".wt-workspace")).toContainText(name);
  const session = await api<Session>(request, user, "/session");
  expect(session.member?.role).toBe("admin");
  expect(session.workspace?.isDemo).toBe(false);
  expect(
    (await api<DevicesResponse>(request, user, "/devices")).devices,
  ).toHaveLength(0);
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.locator(".wt-workspace")).toContainText(name);
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Nome da empresa", { exact: true })).toHaveCount(
    0,
  );
});

test("portal: exemplos só são criados após opção explícita", async ({
  page,
  request,
}) => {
  const user = identity("examples");
  await page.setExtraHTTPHeaders(headers(user));
  await page.goto("/portal");
  await page
    .getByLabel("Nome da empresa", { exact: true })
    .fill(`Demonstração QA ${user.id}`);
  await page
    .getByRole("checkbox", { name: /Incluir equipes e aparelhos fictícios/ })
    .check();
  await page
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Criar empresa", exact: true })
    .click();
  await expect(page.locator(".wt-demo-banner")).toContainText(
    "exemplos fictícios",
  );
  const devices = (await api<DevicesResponse>(request, user, "/devices"))
    .devices;
  expect(devices).toHaveLength(3);
  expect(devices.every((device) => device.isExample)).toBe(true);
  expect(
    (await api<TeamsResponse>(request, user, "/teams")).teams,
  ).toHaveLength(2);
  expect(
    (await api<MessagesResponse>(request, user, "/messages")).messages,
  ).toHaveLength(0);
});

test("portal: equipes e equipamento são cadastrados, editados, verificados e arquivados", async ({
  page,
  request,
}) => {
  const user = identity("inventory");
  await bootstrap(request, user);
  await openPortal(page, user, "teams");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Nova equipe", exact: true }).click();
  const teamDialog = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Criar equipe" });
  await teamDialog
    .getByLabel("Nome da equipe", { exact: true })
    .fill("Expedição QA");
  await teamDialog
    .getByLabel("Local de operação", { exact: true })
    .fill("Base de teste");
  await teamDialog
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Criar equipe", exact: true })
    .click();
  await expect(teamDialog).not.toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator(".wt-team-card")).toContainText("Expedição QA");
  await page
    .getByRole("navigation", { name: "Navegação do portal" })
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Equipamentos", exact: true })
    .click();
  await page
    .getByRole("button", { name: /^(Novo|Cadastrar) equipamento$/ })
    .first()
    .click();
  const dialog = page.getByRole("dialog");
  await dialog
    .getByLabel("Nome do equipamento", { exact: true })
    .fill("Rádio principal QA");
  await dialog
    .getByLabel("Número de série / patrimônio", { exact: true })
    .fill(`QA-${user.id}`);
  await dialog.getByLabel("Modelo", { exact: true }).fill("Modelo de teste");
  await dialog
    .getByLabel("Equipe", { exact: true })
    .selectOption({ label: "Expedição QA" });
  await dialog
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Cadastrar equipamento", exact: true })
    .click();
  await expect(dialog).not.toBeVisible();
  let devices = (await api<DevicesResponse>(request, user, "/devices")).devices;
  expect(devices).toHaveLength(1);
  expect(devices[0].teamName).toBe("Expedição QA");
  await page.reload();
  await expect(
    page.getByText("Rádio principal QA", { exact: true }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  await page
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Editar Rádio principal QA", exact: true })
    .click();
  await page
    .getByRole("dialog")
    .getByLabel("Nome do equipamento", { exact: true })
    .fill("Rádio revisado QA");
  await page
    .getByRole("dialog")
    .getByLabel("Status", { exact: true })
    .selectOption("maintenance");
  await page
    .getByRole("dialog")
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Salvar alterações", exact: true })
    .click();
  await expect(
    page.getByText("Rádio revisado QA", { exact: true }),
  ).toBeVisible();
  devices = (await api<DevicesResponse>(request, user, "/devices")).devices;
  expect(devices[0].status).toBe("maintenance");
  await page
    .locator(".wt-equipment-card")
    .filter({ hasText: "Rádio revisado QA" })
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Verificar", exact: true })
    .click();
  await expect
    .poll(
      async () =>
        (await api<DevicesResponse>(request, user, "/devices")).devices[0]
          .lastCheckAt,
    )
    .not.toBeNull();
  await page
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Arquivar Rádio revisado QA", exact: true })
    .click();
  const archiveDialog = page.getByRole("dialog");
  await archiveDialog
    .getByRole("button", { name: /^(Arquivar|Confirmar arquivamento)/ })
    .click();
  await expect(archiveDialog).not.toBeVisible();
  await expect
    .poll(
      async () =>
        (await api<DevicesResponse>(request, user, "/devices")).devices.length,
    )
    .toBe(0);
});

test("portal: conversa é persistente e conteúdo HTML permanece texto", async ({
  page,
  request,
}) => {
  const user = identity("messages");
  await bootstrap(request, user);
  await openPortal(page, user, "messages");
  const message =
    '<img src=x onerror="window.__qaInjected=true"> Atualização literal de teste.';
  await page.getByLabel("Mensagem para Geral", { exact: true }).fill(message);
  await page
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Enviar mensagem", exact: true })
    .click();
  const log = page.getByRole("log", { name: "Mensagens do canal Geral" });
  await expect(log).toContainText(message);
  await expect(log.locator("img")).toHaveCount(0);
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => "__qaInjected" in window)).toBe(false);
  await page.reload();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole("log", { name: "Mensagens do canal Geral" }),
  ).toContainText(message);
  expect(
    (await api<MessagesResponse>(request, user, "/messages")).messages,
  ).toHaveLength(1);
});

test("portal: convite é aceito e colaborador não recebe controles administrativos", async ({
  page,
  request,
  browser,
}) => {
  const admin = identity("invite-admin"),
    collaborator = identity("invite-colleague");
  await bootstrap(request, admin);
  await openPortal(page, admin, "settings");
  await page
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Criar convite", exact: true })
    .click();
  const inviteDialog = page.getByRole("dialog");
  await inviteDialog.getByLabel(/E-mail da pessoa/).fill(collaborator.email);
  await inviteDialog
    .getByLabel("Perfil", { exact: true })
    .selectOption("collaborator");
  await inviteDialog
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Gerar convite", exact: true })
    .click();
  const inviteLink = inviteDialog.getByLabel("Link do convite", {
    exact: true,
  });
  await expect(inviteLink).toBeVisible();
  const url = await inviteLink.inputValue();
  expect(new URL(url).pathname).toBe("/portal");
  const context = await browser.newContext({
    extraHTTPHeaders: headers(collaborator),
    reducedMotion: "reduce",
  });
  try {
    const invitedPage = await context.newPage();
    await invitedPage.goto(url);
    await expect(
      invitedPage.getByText(collaborator.email, { exact: true }),
    ).toBeVisible();
    await invitedPage
      .getByRole("button", { name: "Aceitar convite", exact: true })
      .click();
    await expect(invitedPage.locator("#portal-content")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(
      invitedPage.getByRole("button", {
        name: "Acessos e convites",
        exact: true,
      }),
    ).toHaveCount(0);
    await invitedPage.goto(`${origin}/portal#devices`);
    await expect(invitedPage.locator("#portal-content")).toBeVisible();
    await expect(
      invitedPage.getByRole("button", {
        name: /^(Novo|Cadastrar) equipamento$/,
      }),
    ).toHaveCount(0);
    const denied = await request.get("/api/v1/invitations", {
      headers: headers(collaborator),
    });
    expect(denied.status()).toBe(403);
    await invitedPage.goto(`${origin}/portal#settings`);
    await expect(
      invitedPage.getByRole("button", { name: "Criar convite", exact: true }),
    ).toHaveCount(0);
    expect(
      (await api<Session>(request, collaborator, "/session")).member?.role,
    ).toBe("collaborator");
  } finally {
    await context.close();
  }
});

test("saúde: busca, FAQ e observações locais não enviam nem persistem dados", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  const writes: string[] = [];
  page.on("request", (request) => {
    if (!["GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"].includes(request.method()))
      writes.push(`${request.method()} ${request.url()}`);
  });
  await page.goto("/saude");
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toContainText(
    "CUIDAR DA ROTINA.",
  );
  await page.getByLabel("Buscar orientações", { exact: true }).fill("pausas");
  await expect(page.locator(".wt-health-topic-card")).toHaveCount(1);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Limpar busca", exact: true }).click();
  const checkbox = page.getByRole("checkbox", {
    name: "O mobiliário permite ajustes para a tarefa?",
    exact: true,
  });
  await checkbox.check();
  await expect(checkbox).toBeChecked();
  const faq = page.locator(".wt-health-faq details").filter({
    hasText: "Existe um intervalo de pausa válido para todo trabalho?",
  });
  await faq.locator("summary").click();
  await expect(faq).toHaveAttribute("open", "");
  await expect(faq).toContainText("Não adote uma regra única");
  await page.reload();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole("checkbox", {
      name: "O mobiliário permite ajustes para a tarefa?",
      exact: true,
    }),
  ).not.toBeChecked();
  expect(writes).toEqual([]);
});

for (const width of [360, 768, 1280]) {
  test(`portal e saúde ${width}px: layouts sem overflow e navegação utilizável`, async ({
    page,
    request,
  }, testInfo) => {
    const user = identity(`layout-${width}`);
    await bootstrap(request, user, true);
    await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 900 });
    const errors: string[] = [];
    page.on("pageerror", (error) => errors.push(error.message));
    await openPortal(page, user);
    for (const view of [
      "overview",
      "devices",
      "teams",
      "messages",
      "settings",
      "health",
    ]) {
      await page.goto(`/portal#${view}`);
      await expect(page.locator("#portal-content")).toBeVisible();
      await expect(page.locator(".wt-loading")).toHaveCount(0);
      expect(
        await page.evaluate(
          () => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth + 1,
        ),
        `overflow in ${view} at ${width}`,
      ).toBe(true);
    }
    await page.goto("/portal");
    await expect(page.locator("#portal-content")).toBeVisible();
    if (width === 360) {
      const menu = page.getByRole("button", {
        name: "Abrir navegação do portal",
        exact: true,
      });
      await menu.click();
      await page
        .getByRole("navigation", { name: "Navegação do portal" })
        .getByRole("button", { name: "Equipamentos", exact: true })
        .click();
      await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#devices$/);
      await expect(menu).toHaveAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
    }
    await page.screenshot({
      path: testInfo.outputPath(`portal-${width}.png`),
      fullPage: true,
    });
    await page.goto("/saude");
    await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1 })).toBeVisible();
    expect(
      await page.evaluate(
        () => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth + 1,
      ),
    ).toBe(true);
    await page.screenshot({
      path: testInfo.outputPath(`health-${width}.png`),
      fullPage: true,
    });
    expect(errors).toEqual([]);
  });
}
tests/upgrade.spec.ts
import { test, expect } from "@playwright/test";

test("PTT: feedback acompanha a mensagem e reinicia em uma nova pressão", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await page.goto("/");
  const ptt = page.locator("#ptt");
  const feedback = page.getByRole("region", {
    name: "Acompanhe a transmissão simulada",
  });
  const status = feedback.getByRole("status");
  await expect(status).toHaveText("Pronto para conectar");
  await expect(feedback.getByRole("listitem")).toHaveCount(3);
  await expect(feedback).toContainText(
    "Simulação visual. Nenhuma voz é gravada ou enviada.",
  );

  await ptt.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
  await ptt.focus();
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
    await page.keyboard.down("Space");
    await expect(ptt).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
    await expect(status).toHaveText("Preparando sua mensagem");
    await page.keyboard.up("Space");
    await expect(ptt).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
    await expect(status).toHaveText("Sinal em trânsito");
    await expect(
      feedback.locator(".transmission-connector i").first(),
    ).toHaveCSS("animation-name", "none");
    await expect(status).toHaveText("Mensagem recebida", { timeout: 5000 });
  }
});

test("menu mobile: Escape retorna foco, clique fora e desktop encerram o menu", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 390, height: 900 });
  await page.goto("/");
  const navigation = page.getByRole("navigation", {
    name: "Navegação principal",
  });
  const menu = page.locator(".menu-toggle");
  await expect(menu).toHaveAttribute("aria-controls", "primary-navigation");
  await menu.click();
  await expect(navigation).toBeVisible();
  await navigation.getByRole("link").first().focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
  await expect(navigation).toBeHidden();
  await expect(menu).toBeFocused();
  await expect(menu).toHaveAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

  await menu.click();
  await expect(navigation).toBeVisible();
  await page.mouse.click(8, 550);
  await expect(navigation).toBeHidden();
  await expect(menu).toHaveAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");

  await menu.click();
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1280, height: 900 });
  await expect(navigation).toBeVisible();
  await expect(menu).toHaveAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  await expect(menu).toBeHidden();
  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 390, height: 900 });
  await expect(navigation).toBeHidden();
  await expect(page.locator(".nav-cta")).toHaveAttribute("href", "/portal");
});

test("expedição: abas seguem setas, Home e End e levam ao único PTT", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await page.goto("/#expedicao");
  const tabs = page.getByRole("tablist", { name: "Momentos da expedição" });
  const departure = tabs.getByRole("tab", { name: /A partida/ });
  const crossing = tabs.getByRole("tab", { name: /A travessia/ });
  const reunion = tabs.getByRole("tab", { name: /O reencontro/ });
  const panel = page.locator("#field-panel");

  await expect(crossing).toHaveAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
  await crossing.focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
  await expect(reunion).toBeFocused();
  await expect(reunion).toHaveAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
  await expect(panel).toContainText("Chegamos ao ponto de encontro");

  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowRight");
  await expect(departure).toBeFocused();
  await expect(panel).toContainText("Estamos começando a trilha");
  await page.keyboard.press("ArrowLeft");
  await expect(reunion).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press("Home");
  await expect(departure).toBeFocused();
  await expect(departure).toHaveAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
  await page.keyboard.press("End");
  await expect(reunion).toBeFocused();
  await expect(reunion).toHaveAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
  await expect(panel).toHaveAttribute("aria-labelledby", "field-tab-2");
  await expect(tabs.locator('[tabindex="0"]')).toHaveCount(1);

  const action = panel.getByRole("link", { name: "Experimente o PTT" });
  await expect(action).toHaveAttribute("href", "#ptt");
  await action.click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/#ptt$/);
  await expect(page.locator("#ptt")).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(page.locator("#ptt")).toBeInViewport();
});

test("aparelho: cenário alternável e Escape devolve foco ao ponto explorado", async ({
  page,
}) => {
  await page.goto("/");
  const scenes = page.getByRole("group", { name: "Cenário do aparelho" });
  const orbit = scenes.getByRole("button", { name: "Órbita", exact: true });
  const expedition = scenes.getByRole("button", {
    name: "Expedição",
    exact: true,
  });
  await orbit.click();
  await expect(orbit).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
  await expect(expedition).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
  await expedition.click();
  await expect(expedition).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
  await expect(orbit).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
  await expect(
    page.locator(".product-landscape img, img.product-landscape"),
  ).toHaveAttribute("alt", "");

  const antenna = page.getByRole("button", {
    name: "Explorar Antena satelital",
    exact: true,
  });
  await antenna.click();
  await expect(antenna).toHaveAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
  await page
    .getByRole("button", { name: "Fechar detalhe do aparelho" })
    .focus();
  await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
  await expect(antenna).toHaveAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
  await expect(antenna).toBeFocused();
  await expect(
    page.getByRole("button", { name: "Fechar detalhe do aparelho" }),
  ).toHaveCount(0);
});

for (const width of [360, 768, 1280]) {
  test(`expedição ${width}px: imagem carregada e todos os momentos sem overflow`, async ({
    page,
  }) => {
    await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 900 });
    await page.goto("/#expedicao");
    const section = page.locator("#expedicao .field-section");
    await expect(section).toBeVisible();
    const photograph = section.locator(".field-photograph img");
    await expect
      .poll(() =>
        photograph.evaluate(
          (image: HTMLImageElement) => image.complete && image.naturalWidth > 0,
        ),
      )
      .toBe(true);

    const tabs = section.getByRole("tab");
    for (let moment = 0; moment < 3; moment++) {
      await tabs.nth(moment).click();
      await expect(tabs.nth(moment)).toHaveAttribute("aria-selected", "true");
      expect(
        await page.evaluate(
          () => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth,
        ),
      ).toBe(true);
      const panel = await section.locator("#field-panel").boundingBox();
      expect(panel).not.toBeNull();
      expect(panel!.x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
      expect(panel!.x + panel!.width).toBeLessThanOrEqual(width + 1);
    }
  });
}

vite.config.ts
import { sites } from "@openai/sites-vite-plugin";
import tailwindcss from "@tailwindcss/postcss";
import vinext from "vinext";
import { defineConfig } from "vite";
import hosting from "./.openai/hosting.json";

// Logical binding names come from the Sites manifest. The ID is only a local
// emulator placeholder; Sites provisions the real database on deployment.
const localBindingConfig = {
  main: "worker/index.ts",
  compatibility_date: "2026-05-15",
  compatibility_flags: ["nodejs_compat"],
  assets: {
    directory: "dist/client",
    binding: "ASSETS",
    not_found_handling: "none" as const,
    html_handling: "none" as const,
  },
  d1_databases: hosting.d1
    ? [
        {
          binding: hosting.d1,
          database_name: "walk-talk-d1",
          database_id: "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000",
        },
      ]
    : [],
};

export default defineConfig(async () => {
  process.env.WRANGLER_WRITE_LOGS ??= "false";
  process.env.WRANGLER_LOG_PATH ??= ".wrangler/logs";
  process.env.MINIFLARE_REGISTRY_PATH ??= ".wrangler/registry";
  process.env.CLOUDFLARE_CF_FETCH_ENABLED ??= "false";
  process.env.WRANGLER_SEND_METRICS ??= "false";
  const { cloudflare } = await import("@cloudflare/vite-plugin");
  return {
    css: { postcss: { plugins: [tailwindcss()] } },
    plugins: [
      vinext(),
      sites(),
      cloudflare({
        viteEnvironment: { name: "rsc", childEnvironments: ["ssr"] },
        config: localBindingConfig,
      }),
    ],
  };
});
worker/index.ts
import vinextHandler from "vinext/server/fetch-handler";
import { handleApi } from "../server/index";
import type { ApiEnvironment } from "../server/d1";

export default {
  async fetch(
    request: Request,
    env: ApiEnvironment,
    ctx: ExecutionContext,
  ): Promise<Response> {
    const path = new URL(request.url).pathname;
    if (path === "/api/v1" || path.startsWith("/api/v1/"))
      return handleApi(request, env);
    return vinextHandler.fetch(request, env, ctx);
  },
};
WALK TALK — projeto educativo em React, TypeScript e Vite. Nenhum áudio é capturado ou transmitido.
